{{important|This is a WIP page. The information is not yet full. It is planned to divide the page into several after completion.}}
==Rendering==
===ANGLE===
Because Black Mesa uses Qt 5.9 version, it doesn't use DirectX directly, instead, used '''ANGLE''' rendering layer. '''ANGLE''' (Almost Native Graphics Layer Engine) in Qt 5.9 is a layer that allows Qt to render its OpenGL ES UI via DirectX rather than the native Windows OpenGL driver. This is especially important for Qt 5 on Windows, as Qt Quick/QML in Qt 5 relied on OpenGL/OpenGL ES and OpenGL drivers on Windows have historically been less predictable than DirectX. For Qt, this looks like OpenGL ES 2.0, but '''ANGLE''' internally translates these calls to DirectX.
{{note|This might be the reason that causes rendering issues of the UI on native {{linux|4}} game build. Use {{proton|4}} as a workaround, {{code|-oldgameui}} launch option also can be used to launch the game with VGUI.}}

===DirectX Versions===
If the game doesn't use DirectX Vulkan via {{code|-disabledxvk}} launch option (the default launch option in Steam UI), the UI can use two versions of DirectX. By default, it's DirectX 11 (can be used via {{code|-forceuid3d11}} launch option as well), but can also be used DirectX 9 ({{code|-forceuid3d9}} launch option). Due to the no-mixed-API restriction, if the game runs in DirectX Vulkan (via {{code|-enabledxvk}} launch option or '''DXVK''' launch option in Steam UI), Qt UI is forced to run DirectX 9.

==BlackMesaEngine QML Type==
Provides access to the underlying Black Mesa engine interface, enabling communication between the QML UI and the game engine.

Import Statement: {{code|import CrowbarCollective 1.0}}. Singleton instance: {{code|BlackMesaEngine}}.

===Detailed Description===
'''BlackMesaEngine''' is a singleton type exposed to the QML environment that provides a public set of functions for interacting with the Source engine running Black Mesa. It allows modders and UI developers to execute engine commands, retrieve game state, interact with [[Steam]], manage saves, manipulate [[ConVar|console variables]] (ConVars or Cvar for short), query input bindings, and receive engine/UI signals without needing to write C++ code.

Because it is registered as a singleton, there is no need to instantiate it. Simply use {{code|BlackMesaEngine.<methodName>()}} after importing the module.

Code example:
<source lang=qml>
import CrowbarCollective 1.0

Component.onCompleted: {
    console.log(BlackMesaEngine.getLevelName())
}
</source>

===Enumerations===
====KeyCode====
Defines the numeric codes for keyboard keys, mouse buttons, generic joysticks, XBox 360 controllers, and Steam controllers. Used for input binding and key capturing.

The full list is large and maps to the engine button code table. For UI code, prefer converting between key names and key codes with {{code|keyButtonStringToCode()}} and {{code|keyButtonCodeToString()}}.

Example names include '''KEY_ESCAPE''', '''KEY_ENTER''', '''KEY_SPACE''', '''KEY_UP''', '''KEY_DOWN''', '''MOUSE_LEFT''', '''MOUSE_RIGHT''', and '''KEY_XBUTTON_A'''.

====ServerEntry Object====
Provided by the {{code|matchmakingServerResponded}} signal. Represents a single multiplayer game server, an engine-provided object describing one server browser result. Treat it as a QML var object and read only the fields exposed by the current game build.
{| class="standard-table"
|- style="box-shadow:0 1px #525252; position:sticky; z-index:10; top:-1px"
! Key
! Type
! Description
|-
| name
| string
| Server display name.
|-
| connectionAddressString
| string
| "IP:port" string used to connect (e.g., '''193.104.68.59:27032''').
|-
| map
| string
| The internal current map name running on the server (e.g., ''dm_crossfire'').
|-
| ping
| int
| Latency in milliseconds.
|-
| playersOnServerCurrently
| int
| Current number of human players.
|-
| playersOnServerMaximum
| int
| Maximum player slots.
|-
| botsOnServerCurrently
| int
| Number of bots on the server.
|-
| gameDirectory
| string
| Game directory name (e.g., ''bms'').
|-
| gameDescription
| string
| "Human-readable" game directory name string (e.g., ''Black Mesa'').
|-
| isPasswordProtected
| bool
| Whether the server requires a password to join.
|-
| isSecure
| bool
| Whether the server is VAC-secured.
|-
| steamApplicationId
| int
| Steam App ID of the game.
|-
| lastPlayed
| date
| ISO 8601 date string representing the last time the player connected to that server. Only valid for '''Favorites'''/'''History''' server lists.
|-
|}

One property is not from the engine but injected by the UI at runtime: in '''routeview-join.qml''', ''serverEntry.lan'' (bool type) is set to 1 for LAN servers and 0 for Internet servers before the entry is appended to the list ('''routeview-join.qml''' around lines 62-74).

===Methods===
====Execution & Commands====
*{{code|void executeServerCommand(string commandString)}} - executes a command directly on the server.
*{{code|void executeClientCommand(string commandString)}} - executes a client-side engine command.
*{{code|void executeClientCommandUnrestricted(string commandString)}} - executes a client-side engine command, bypassing standard restriction checks. Use only when a UI feature specifically requires unrestricted command execution.

====Game & Level State====
*{{code|bool isInGame()}} - returns true if the player is actively in a level.
*{{code|bool isInMultiplayerMode()}} - returns true if the current session is a multiplayer game.
*{{code|bool isLevelMainMenuBackground()}} - returns true if the currently loaded map is the main menu background map.
*{{code|string getGameDirectory()}} - returns the name of the game directory (e.g., ''bms'').
*{{code|string getChapterName()}} - returns the name of the current chapter.
*{{code|string getMainMenuBackgroundName()}} - returns the name of the map currently used for the main menu background.
*{{code|string getLevelName()}} - returns the full name of the current level.
*{{code|string getLevelNameShort()}} - returns the shortened file name of the current level.
*{{code|int getLevelVersion()}} - returns the BSP version of the current level.

====Multiplayer Configuration====
*{{code|void writeHostMultiplayerSettings()}} - saves current host configuration for multiplayer to disk.
*{{code|list getAvailableMultiplayerModels()}} - returns a list of '''MultiplayerModel''' objects representing available multiplayer characters.
*{{code|var getCurrentMultiplayerModel()}} - returns a '''CurrentMultiplayerModel''' object describing the current player's chosen multiplayer model and selected skin.
*{{code|int getCurrentMultiplayerModelSkins(string model)}} - returns the total number of skins available for the specified model path.
*{{code|void setCurrentMultiplayerModel(string model, int skin)}} - applies the selected model path and skin index to the current player.
*{{code|int getMaxClients()}} - returns the maximum number of clients allowed on the current server/session.

====Input & Bindings====
*{{code|string keyLookupBinding(string bindingString)}} - looks up the key currently bound to the specified action string.
*{{code|string keyLookupBindingExact(string bindingString)}} - looks up the exact key bound to the specified action string.
*{{code|string keyBindingForKey(int keyButtonCode)}} - returns the action string currently bound to the provided '''KeyCode'''.
*{{code|string keyButtonCodeToString(int keyButtonCode)}} - converts a KeyCode integer to its string representation.
*{{code|int keyButtonStringToCode(string keyButtonString)}} - converts a string representation of a key to its '''KeyCode''' integer.
*{{code|void requestKeyCapture()}} - requests that the UI capture the next key press. Once captured, emits the {{code|keyCaptured(int keyButtonCode)}} signal.
*{{code|void grabInputForGameUI()}} - forces the engine to direct input to the game UI.
*{{code|void releaseInputFromGameUI()}} - releases input focus from the game UI back to the engine/gameplay.

====Console Variables====
*{{code|real getConsoleVariableAsFloat(string variableName)}} - returns the console variable value as a floating-point number.
*{{code|int getConsoleVariableAsInt(string variableName)}} - returns the console variable value as an integer.
*{{code|bool getConsoleVariableAsBoolean(string variableName)}} - returns the console variable value as a boolean.
*{{code|vector3d getConsoleVariableAsVector(string variableName)}} - returns the console variable value as a vector3d.
*{{code|string getConsoleVariableAsString(string variableName)}} - returns the console variable value as a string.
*{{code|void setConsoleVariableAsFloat(string variableName, real value)}} - sets the console variable value from a floating-point number.
*{{code|void setConsoleVariableAsInt(string variableName, int value)}} - sets the console variable value from an integer.
*{{code|void setConsoleVariableAsBoolean(string variableName, bool value)}} - sets the console variable value from a boolean.
*{{code|void setConsoleVariableAsVector(string variableName, vector3d value)}} - sets the console variable value from a vector3d.
*{{code|void setConsoleVariableAsString(string variableName, string value)}} - sets the console variable value from a string.

====Console Output & Autocomplete====
*{{code|list listConsoleAutocompleteEntries(string partialCommandString)}} - returns a list of commands that match the partial string for autocomplete.
*{{code|list listConsoleSuggestions(string commandString, string partialArgumentString)}} - returns a list of argument suggestions for the specified command.
*{{code|void printConsoleMessage(color color, string text)}} - adds colored text to the UI console message queue. Multi-line text is split into separate message entries.

====Video & Rendering====
*{{code|bool isWindowedMode()}} - returns true if the game is running in a window.
*{{code|bool isWindowedBorderlessMode()}} - returns true if the game is running in windowed borderless mode.
*{{code|list listVideoModes()}} - returns a list of supported display modes for the current hardware. Each object contains width, height, bitsPerPixel, and refreshRate.

====Audio====
*{{code|void playSoundEffect(string soundName)}} - plays a UI or game sound effect globally.
*{{code|int playAmbientSound(string soundName, real volume)}} - plays an ambient sound effect and returns its unique ID.
*{{code|bool isSoundStillPlaying(int soundId)}} - returns true if the sound associated with soundId is currently active.
*{{code|void stopSoundById(int soundId)}} - halts the sound associated with soundId.
*{{code|void setVolumeById(int soundId, real volume)}} - adjusts the volume of an actively playing sound by its ID.

====File System & Localization====
*{{code|string getLocalizedString(string stringId)}} - translates a localization token (e.g., ''#GameUI_Quit'') into the current language string. If the string does not start with #, it is returned unchanged.
*{{code|bool isDirectory(string fileName, string pathId)}} - checks if the specified path resolves to a directory.
*{{code|list listDirectoryEntries(string wildCard, string pathId)}} - lists directory contents based on a wildcard search.
*{{code|string readAllText(string fileName, string pathId)}} - reads the full contents of a file and returns it as a UTF-8 string. Returns an empty string if the file cannot be opened.

====Save System & Campaigns====
*{{code|list listSaveEntries()}} - returns a list of SaveEntry objects representing current save files.
*{{code|bool removeSaveEntry(string saveEntryName)}} - deletes a specific save file.
*{{code|list listCampaignEntries()}} - parses JSON files in the campaigns/ directory and returns a list of available campaigns. Each returned object also includes isOfficial.

====Steam & Achievements====
*{{code|int getSteamApplicationId()}} - returns the '''Steam''' '''AppID''' for the game.
*{{code|void activateSteamOverlay(string dialogName)}} - opens the '''Steam Overlay''' to a specific dialog page.
*{{code|void activateSteamOverlayToWebPage(string url)}} - opens the '''Steam Overlay''' web browser to the specified URL.
*{{code|list listAchievementEntries()}} - returns a list of '''AchievementEntry''' objects, containing details such as name, description, count, goal, '''isAchieved''', '''shouldHideUntilAchieved''', '''imageSourceUnlocked''' and '''imageSourceLocked'''.

====Matchmaking & Servers====
Following methods that returns a string value are methods that return a query handle (a string), the handle can be used to match responses to the query that triggered them.
*{{code|string requestFavoritesServerList()}} -  Player's Steam-favorited servers.
*{{code|string requestFriendsServerList()}} - Servers where Steam friends are currently playing.
*{{code|string requestHistoryServerList()}} - Servers the player recently joined.
*{{code|string requestInternetServerList()}} - Steam master server list.
*{{code|string requestLANServerList()}} - Local network discovery.
*{{code|string requestSpectatorServerList()}} - starts a server list query and returns a queryHandle string, which is used to identify responses in the matchmakingServerResponded signal.
*{{code|void matchmakingCancelQuery(string queryHandle)}} - cancels an active matchmaking query.

====Engine Info====
*{{code|int getEngineBuildNumber()}} - returns the engine build number.
*{{code|string getProductVersionString()}} - returns the product version string.
*{{code|int getClientVersion()}} - returns the client version number.
*{{code|void setShouldUpdateProgressBar(bool shouldUpdateProgressBar)}} - sets whether the loading progress bar should be updated.
*{{code|void setRootPanelVisibility(bool rootPanelVisibility)}} - sets root UI panel visibility.

===Signals===
*{{code|levelLoadingStarted(string levelName, bool isBackgroundLevel, bool showProgressDialog)}} - emitted when map loading begins.
*{{code|levelLoadingFinished(bool isError, string failureReason, string extendedReason)}} - emitted when a map has finished loading or failed.
*{{code|disconnectedFromServer()}} - emitted when the client drops connection to the server.
*{{code|updateProgressValue(real progressValue, string statusText)}} - emitted iteratively during a map load to update loading bars.
*{{code|gameUIActived()}} - emitted when the game UI gains focus. The spelling is intentional and matches the exposed API.
*{{code|gameUIHidden()}} - emitted when the game UI loses focus and closes.
*{{code|keyCaptured(int keyButtonCode)}} - emitted after '''requestKeyCapture()''' is called and the user presses a key. '''keyButtonCode''' corresponds to the '''KeyCode''' enum.
*{{code|gameConsoleActived()}} - emitted when the developer console opens. The spelling is intentional and matches the exposed API.
*{{code|gameConsoleHidden()}} - emitted when the developer console closes.
*{{code|gameConsoleCleared()}} - emitted when the developer console output is cleared.
*{{code|gameConsoleMessagesReceived(list messagesList)}} - emitted periodically with a queue of console logs. Each object contains color and text.
*{{code|matchmakingServerResponded(var serverEntry, string queryHandle)}} - emitted when a server responds during a matchmaking query.
*{{code|matchmakingQueryCompleted(string queryHandle)}} - emitted when a matchmaking query finishes scanning.
*{{code|navigationSourceChanged(int navigationSourceType)}} - emitted when the input device changes. Known values are 0 for keyboard/mouse, 1 for controller, and 2 for '''Steam''' controller.

==VFS Protocol (vfs://)==
'''vfs://''' is a custom URL scheme registered into Qt's networking stack. It lets QML code load files through the {{source|4}} Engine's Virtual File System — meaning files can be resolved from [[VPK]] archives, mounted game directories, or any other path the engine has mounted, rather than the raw OS file system.

It is implemented as a custom '''QNetworkAccessManager''' (or equivalent network handler) that intercepts '''vfs://''' URLs and routes them through the Source Engine's '''IFileSystem''' interface. This is described in the Engine Integration Layer as the "Custom Network Access Manager" integration point. 

===URL Format===
<source lang=qml>
vfs:///path/to/file
</source>

The path is relative to the engine's search paths (same resolution rules as {{code|filesystem->Open()}}). The triple slash (///) is standard for a local-path URI with an empty host.

===How it's used in QML===
====Loading fonts via FontLoader====
'''theme.qml''' uses '''vfs:///''' to load all UI fonts from the engine file system:

<source lang=qml>
FontLoader { source: "vfs:///ui/fonts/firamono-regular.ttf" }  
FontLoader { source: "vfs:///ui/fonts/notosans-light.ttf" }
</source>

Example can be found in '''theme.qml''' around lines 84-104.

====Reading config files via XMLHttpRequest====

'''input-bindings-manager.qml''' uses '''vfs:///''' to read '''config.cfg''' through an '''XMLHttpRequest'''-based helper:

<source lang=qml>
_readConfigFile("vfs:///cfg/config_example.cfg", function (response) { ... })
</source>

Example can be found in '''input-bindings-manager.qml''' around lines 247-249.

==image:// Image Provider==
'''image://''' is a QML built-in URL scheme for loading images through a custom image provider — a C++ class (QQuickImageProvider) registered into the QQmlEngine. The format is:

<source lang=qml>
image://<provider-id>/<path>  
</source>

In Black Mesa, the provider ID is game, so all image URLs start with '''image://game/'''. It is registered by BlackMesaUI.dll and handles both VTF and standard image formats, PNG normally, resolving paths through the Source Engine VFS.

The '''image://game/''' provider is the only way to load a VTF file in QML, the standard schemes do not know how to decode VTF.

===How to use it===

Assign the URL to the source property of a QML Image element:

<source lang=qml>
Image {  
    source: "image://game/ui/images/nav-logo.png"  
}
</source>

Example can be found in '''component-header.qml''' around lines 159-167.

===URL format===
<source lang=qml>
image://game/<vfs-path>  
</source>

The path after '''image://game/''' is the same VFS path that would be passed to the Source Engine file system - it can point inside VPK archives, game directories, etc.

====All usage patterns in the codebase====

Static PNG UI images:
<source lang=qml>
Image { source: "image://game/ui/images/unavailable.png" }
</source>

Example can be found in '''routeview-unavailable.qml''' around lines 5-12.


VTF background textures (wallpaper):
<source lang=qml>
background01: "image://game/materials/console/background01.vtf"
</source>

Example can be found in '''component-wallpaper.qml''' around lines 21-25.


Dynamic path construction (map thumbnails):
<source lang=qml>
source: [  
    "image://game/materials/vgui/multiplayer/map_backgrounds/",  
    model.value,  
    "_0_widescreen.vtf"  
].join('')
</source>

Example can be found in '''component-optionslist-input-map.qml''' around lines 86-95.


State-based image switching (hover effect):
<source lang=qml>
source: discordLinkArea.containsMouse  
    ? "image://game/ui/images/discord-purple.png"  
    : "image://game/ui/images/discord-white.png"
</source>

Example can be found in '''component-footer.qml''' around lines 143-144.


Asynchronous loading (for heavy textures, avoids blocking the UI thread):
<source lang=qml>
Image {  
    source: "image://game/materials/vgui/multiplayer/map_backgrounds/"+map+"_0_widescreen_16_10.vtf"  
    asynchronous: true  
}
</source>


Example can be found in '''routeview-join.qml''' around lines 249-257.


Path stored in a data model (save game thumbnail):
<source lang=qml>
thumbnailUrl: "image://game/ui/images/newSaveThumbnail.png"
</source>

Example can be found in '''routeview-savegame.qml''' at line 55.

==Files Structure==
===component-wallpaper.qml===  
  
{{code|component-wallpaper.qml}} is the full-screen background layer rendered behind all other UI elements. It is registered in {{code|qmldir}} as {{code|Wallpaper 1.0}} and instantiated once in {{code|main.qml}} as {{code|id: wallpaper}}, filling {{code|appRoot}}.  
  
The component manages four independently animated visual layers stacked in Z-order: a background photograph, a Mesa logo watermark, two gradient overlays, and a dark skrim. All four layers share a common {{code|suppressAnimations}} flag that disables their {{code|Behavior}} animations during level loads.  
  
====Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Default  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|backgroundImageUrl}}  
| {{code|alias}}  
| —  
| Alias for <code><nowiki>backgroundImage.source</nowiki></code>. Set internally by {{code|updateBackgroundImage()}}.  
|-  
| {{code|showImage}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|true}}  
| Controls the opacity of the background photograph ({{code|1.0}} when true, {{code|0.0}} when false). Animated over 1250 ms.  
|-  
| {{code|showExtras}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| Controls the opacity of both gradient overlay rectangles. Never set to {{code|true}} by any caller in the codebase; the gradients are always hidden at runtime.  
|-  
| {{code|showSkrim}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| Controls the opacity of the dark skrim rectangle. Animated over 600 ms.  
|-  
| {{code|darkenSkrim}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| Selects between {{code|Theme.opacity.pageSkrimLight}} (0.75) and {{code|Theme.opacity.pageSkrimDark}} (0.85) for the skrim opacity target.  
|-  
| {{code|suppressAnimations}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| When {{code|true}}, disables all four {{code|Behavior on opacity}} animations. Set to {{code|isLoadingLevel}} by {{code|main.qml}} so transitions are instant during level loads.  
|-  
| {{code|_skrimOpacity}}  
| {{code|real}}  
| computed  
| Private. <code><nowiki>darkenSkrim ? Theme.opacity.pageSkrimDark : Theme.opacity.pageSkrimLight</nowiki></code>.  
|-  
| {{code|_mapname}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| {{code|""}}  
| Private. Stores the last map name passed to {{code|updateBackgroundImage()}} so subsequent calls without an argument can reuse it (e.g. on window resize).  
|-  
| {{code|_maps}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| set in {{code|Component.onCompleted}}  
| Private. JavaScript object mapping map name strings to background key strings.  
|-  
| {{code|_files}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| set in {{code|Component.onCompleted}}  
| Private. JavaScript object mapping background key strings to {{code|image://game/}} VTF URLs.  
|}  
  
====Component.onCompleted — Data Initialization====  
  
On construction, two JavaScript objects are built and assigned to {{code|_files}} and {{code|_maps}}.  
  
=====_files=====  
  
Maps 19 background keys ({{code|background01}} through {{code|background19}}) to VTF image URLs under {{code|image://game/materials/console/}}. Each key has a standard variant and a {{code|_widescreen}} variant, giving 38 entries total.  
  
=====_maps=====  
  
Maps source strings to background keys. For the 19 numbered backgrounds the mapping is trivially 1-1 ({{code|background01}} → {{code|'background01'}}, etc.). Four additional entries map specific BSP map names to background keys for use during inter-chapter loads:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Map name  
! Background key  
|-  
| {{code|bm_c0a0a}}  
| {{code|background01}}  
|-  
| {{code|bm_c0a0b}}  
| {{code|background01}}  
|-  
| {{code|bm_c0a0c}}  
| {{code|background01}}  
|-  
| {{code|bm_c1a0a}}  
| {{code|background02}}  
|}  
  
A TODO comment in the source notes that only four map-name aliases exist and that the mapping is incomplete.  
  
====Functions====  
  
=====updateBackgroundImage(mapname)=====  
  
Public. Called by {{code|main.qml}} from {{code|onWidthChanged}} and {{code|onHeightChanged}} (without an argument, to recompute widescreen selection on resize), and from {{code|routeview-video.qml}} after a resolution change.  
  
If {{code|mapname}} is provided, it is stored in {{code|_mapname}}. The function then calls {{code|_getUrl(_mapname, widescreen)}} where the widescreen flag is <code><nowiki>appRoot.width / appRoot.height >= 1.586</nowiki></code> (approximately a 16:10 aspect ratio threshold). If the resolved URL differs from the current {{code|backgroundImageUrl}}, it is assigned, triggering the 1250 ms fade animation (unless {{code|suppressAnimations}} is {{code|true}}).  
  
=====_getUrl(mapname, widescreen)=====  
  
Private. Looks up {{code|_maps[mapname]}} to get a background key, appends {{code|'_widescreen'}} if {{code|widescreen}} is {{code|true}}, then returns {{code|_files[key]}}. Returns {{code|undefined}} if {{code|mapname}} is not in {{code|_maps}}.  
  
====Visual Children====  
  
=====backgroundImage=====  
  
An {{code|Image}} that fills the parent. Its {{code|opacity}} is {{code|1.0}} when {{code|showImage}} is {{code|true}} and {{code|0.0}} otherwise, animated over 1250 ms. The {{code|source}} is controlled via the {{code|backgroundImageUrl}} alias.  
  
=====bottomLeftMesaLogo=====  
  
An {{code|Image}} displaying {{code|ui/images/logo-mark.png}}, anchored to the bottom-right corner of the parent with zero margin. Width and height are both <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(750 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code> (750×738 native pixels). This element has no opacity control and is always visible regardless of any property flags.  
  
=====subBackgroundGradient=====  
  
A {{code|Rectangle}} rotated −90° around its top-left corner, producing a vertical gradient that runs from bottom to top of the screen. The gradient goes from {{code|subNavBackground}} (first stop, position 0.0) to {{code|transparent}} (position 1.0). Opacity is {{code|0.7}} when {{code|showExtras}} is {{code|true}}, otherwise {{code|0.0}}, animated over 1250 ms.  
  
=====backgroundGradient=====  
  
Identical rotation and sizing to {{code|subBackgroundGradient}}. The gradient goes from {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}} ({{code|#e47125}}, orange) at position 0.0 to {{code|transparent}} at position 1.0. Opacity is {{code|0.15}} when {{code|showExtras}} is {{code|true}}, otherwise {{code|0.0}}, animated over 1250 ms.  
  
=====skrim=====  
  
A {{code|Rectangle}} filling the parent with color {{code|Theme.colors.skrim}} ({{code|#2d2d2d}}). Opacity is {{code|_skrimOpacity}} when {{code|showSkrim}} is {{code|true}}, otherwise {{code|0.0}}, animated over 600 ms (shorter than the image/gradient animations).  
  
====Usage in main.qml====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Binding  
! Value  
! Effect  
|-  
| {{code|showSkrim}}  
| <code><nowiki>isInGame || router.currentRoute !== Routes.index</nowiki></code>  
| Skrim is shown on all pages except the main menu index, and always when in-game.  
|-  
| {{code|darkenSkrim}}  
| {{code|isInGame}}  
| Uses the darker opacity (0.85) when in-game, lighter (0.75) on menu pages.  
|-  
| {{code|suppressAnimations}}  
| {{code|isLoadingLevel}}  
| All opacity transitions are instant during level loads.  
|}  
  
{{code|wallpaper.updateBackgroundImage()}} (no argument) is called from {{code|onWidthChanged}} and {{code|onHeightChanged}} to reselect the standard or widescreen image variant when the window is resized. {{code|wallpaper.updateBackgroundImage(mapname)}} is called with a map name when a level begins loading.  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''{{code|subNavBackground}} is an unqualified identifier.''' On line 147, the first {{code|GradientStop}} of {{code|subBackgroundGradient}} uses {{code|subNavBackground}} as its color value without the {{code|Theme.colors.}} prefix. {{code|subNavBackground}} is not defined anywhere in the component's scope or any ancestor scope. At runtime this resolves to {{code|undefined}}, and QML will silently substitute a default color (typically black or transparent), making the gradient visually incorrect. The correct reference is {{code|Theme.colors.subNavBackground}} ({{code|#111111}}).  
  
2. '''{{code|showExtras}} is never set to {{code|true}}.''' The {{code|main.qml}} instantiation does not bind {{code|showExtras}}, and no other file in the codebase sets it. Both gradient overlays ({{code|subBackgroundGradient}} and {{code|backgroundGradient}}) are permanently invisible at runtime.  
  
3. '''Widescreen VTF mismatches in {{code|_files}}.''' Several widescreen keys point to the wrong VTF file:  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Key  
! Assigned VTF  
! Expected VTF  
|-  
| {{code|background06_widescreen}}  
| {{code|background02_widescreen.vtf}}  
| {{code|background06_widescreen.vtf}}  
|-  
| {{code|background09_widescreen}}  
| {{code|background10_widescreen.vtf}}  
| {{code|background09_widescreen.vtf}}  
|-  
| {{code|background10_widescreen}}  
| {{code|background11_widescreen.vtf}}  
| {{code|background10_widescreen.vtf}}  
|-  
| {{code|background11_widescreen}}  
| {{code|background12_widescreen.vtf}}  
| {{code|background11_widescreen.vtf}}  
|}  
These appear to be copy-paste or off-by-one errors. {{code|background12_widescreen}} correctly maps to {{code|background12_widescreen.vtf}}, so the sequence resumes correctly from key 12 onward.  
  
4. '''{{code|_mapname}} starts empty.''' On construction, {{code|_mapname}} is {{code|""}}. If {{code|updateBackgroundImage()}} is called without an argument before any map name has been provided (e.g. on the first {{code|onWidthChanged}} event), {{code|_getUrl("", ...)}} returns {{code|undefined}} and {{code|backgroundImageUrl}} is not updated. The background image remains unset until a map name is supplied.  
  
5. '''{{code|bottomLeftMesaLogo}} uses {{code|Theme.widthScale}} for both dimensions.''' The height is <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(750 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code> rather than <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(738 * Theme.heightScale)</nowiki></code>. This preserves the aspect ratio of the logo relative to screen width, but the logo will appear slightly distorted on non-16:9 displays where {{code|widthScale}} and {{code|heightScale}} differ.  
  
6. '''{{code|_maps}} 1-1 section is redundant.''' The TODO comment expresses a desire for a 1-1 mapping between map names and background filenames. The 19 numbered entries in {{code|_maps}} already are 1-1 ({{code|background01}} → {{code|'background01'}}), making the entire {{code|_maps}} lookup unnecessary for those keys — {{code|_files[mapname]}} would produce the same result directly. Only the four BSP map name aliases at the bottom justify the indirection.  

===devtest.qml===  
  
{{code|devtest.qml}} is a standalone developer test harness. It is '''not registered''' in {{code|qmldir}}, is '''not referenced''' anywhere in the codebase, and has no corresponding entry in {{code|routes.qml}}. It cannot be loaded through any normal UI flow and exists solely as an offline development tool for exercising engine API calls.  
  
The root element is a {{code|FocusScope}} (id: {{code|rootItem}}) with a fixed size of 1600×900. It does not use {{code|Theme}}, {{code|L10n}}, {{code|Sound}}, or any other singleton from the production UI.  
  
====Private State — {{code|d}}====  
  
A {{code|QtObject}} (id: {{code|d}}) holds one private property:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Purpose  
|-  
| {{code|queryHandle}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| Stores the handle returned by <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.requestInternetServerList()</nowiki></code>, used later to cancel the query.  
|}  
  
====Deferred Startup Timer — {{code|deferredCompletedTimer}}====  
  
A {{code|Timer}} with {{code|interval: 1250}}, {{code|repeat: false}}, and {{code|running: true}}. It fires automatically 1250 ms after construction and performs three engine calls:  
  
# <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.listSaveEntries()</nowiki></code> — result is JSON-logged and assigned to {{code|listSaveEntriesGrid.model}}.  
# <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.requestInternetServerList()</nowiki></code> — handle is stored in {{code|d.queryHandle}}.  
# <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.listAchievementEntries()</nowiki></code> — result is JSON-logged to the console only; not displayed in the UI.  
  
====Server Response Handler — {{code|Connections}}====  
  
Listens to {{code|BlackMesaEngine.onMatchmakingServerResponded}}. On each response:  
  
# Logs {{code|queryHandle}}, {{code|serverEntry.steamApplicationId}}, and <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getSteamApplicationId()</nowiki></code> to the console.  
# Filters the entry: only appends to {{code|serverEntriesList.model}} if <code><nowiki>serverEntry.steamApplicationId === BlackMesaEngine.getSteamApplicationId()</nowiki></code> '''and''' <code><nowiki>serverEntry.gameDirectory === "bms"</nowiki></code>.  
  
Two alternative filter conditions are present as comments: one for CS:GO (app ID 730, directory {{code|csgo}}) and one for Black Mesa (app ID 362890). The comment notes that the CS:GO filter was used because the Black Mesa server list was empty at the time of writing.  
  
The inline comment also documents the full shape of a server entry object:  
  
{| class="standard-table"

====Visual Layout====  
  
The root {{code|Rectangle}} fills the parent with a grey background and contains a {{code|ColumnLayout}} with three vertically-stacked sections.  
  
=====Save Entries Grid — {{code|listSaveEntriesGrid}}=====  
  
A {{code|GridView}} with {{code|cellWidth: 160}} and {{code|cellHeight: 90}}. Its model is populated by the startup timer. Each cell renders the save thumbnail via {{code|modelData.thumbnailUrl}}. A right-click {{code|MouseArea}} ({{code|acceptedButtons: Qt.RightButton}}) calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.removeSaveEntry(modelData.name)</nowiki></code> and immediately refreshes the model by calling <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.listSaveEntries()</nowiki></code> again.

====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''{{code|queryHandle}} is referenced unqualified in the {{code|Connections}} block.''' Line 69 logs {{code|queryHandle}} directly. In QML scope resolution, this does not resolve to {{code|d.queryHandle}} — {{code|queryHandle}} is a property of the {{code|d}} object, not of {{code|rootItem}} or any enclosing scope. The value logged will be {{code|undefined}}. The correct reference is {{code|d.queryHandle}}.  
  
2. '''{{code|QtGraphicalEffects 1.0}} is imported but unused.''' No graphical effect type is instantiated anywhere in the file.  
  
3. '''{{code|QtQuick.Layouts 1.2}} differs from the rest of the codebase.''' All production files use {{code|QtQuick.Layouts 1.1}}. This is a minor inconsistency with no functional impact.  
  
4. '''Fixed 1600×900 size with no {{code|Theme}} scaling.''' The root item is hardcoded to 1600×900 and no {{code|Theme.widthScale}} or {{code|Theme.heightScale}} is applied to any dimension. The file is not intended for production display.  
  
5. '''Native Qt {{code|Button}} elements instead of custom button components.''' The production UI uses {{code|CTAButton}}, {{code|DefaultButton}}, etc. This file uses the unstyled {{code|QtQuick.Controls 2.2}} {{code|Button}} directly.  
  
6. '''{{code|listAchievementEntries()}} output is console-only.''' The achievement data is fetched and logged but never displayed in the UI, unlike the save entries and server entries which have dedicated visual sections.  
  
7. '''The {{code|Rectangle}} border overlay inside {{code|RowLayout}} uses {{code|anchors.fill: parent}}.''' The {{code|RowLayout}} is not an {{code|Item}} subtype that supports {{code|anchors.fill}} children in the same way as a plain {{code|Item}}. The border rectangle will fill the layout but may not clip correctly against the button children.

===input-bindings-manager.qml===  
  
{{code|input-bindings-manager.qml}} is registered in {{code|qmldir}} as {{code|singleton InputBindingsManager 1.0}}. It is the central state manager for the key bindings system, responsible for:  
  
* Translating between Qt key/mouse enum values and Source engine key name strings.  
* Mapping engine command strings to localized human-readable labels.  
* Loading and reverse-indexing the current bindings from {{code|config.cfg}}.  
* Managing the key-capture lifecycle (selection → capture → conflict resolution → write).  
* Emitting signals that cause binding display components to refresh.  
  
It is a {{code|pragma Singleton}} {{code|QtObject}} with 374 lines.  
  
====Static lookup tables====  
  
All three tables are built as immediately-invoked function expressions (IIFEs) and frozen with {{code|Object.freeze()}} at construction time. They are {{code|readonly property variant}}.  
  
=====commandMap=====  
  
Maps engine command strings to localized label strings sourced from <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.options.bindings</nowiki></code>. Contains 49 entries across five logical groups:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Group  
! Commands  
|-  
| Movement  
| {{code|+forward}}, {{code|+back}}, {{code|+moveleft}}, {{code|+moveright}}, {{code|+jump}}, {{code|+duck}}, {{code|+speed}}  
|-  
| Combat  
| {{code|+use}}, {{code|+attack}}, {{code|+attack2}}, {{code|+reload}}, {{code|impulse 100}}, {{code|+zoom}}  
|-  
| Weapons  
| {{code|invnext}}, {{code|invprev}}, {{code|lastinv}}, {{code|slot1}}–{{code|slot6}}, {{code|use weapon_crowbar}}, {{code|use weapon_glock}}, {{code|use weapon_357}}, {{code|use weapon_mp5}}, {{code|use weapon_shotgun}}, {{code|use weapon_crossbow}}, {{code|use weapon_rpg}}, {{code|use weapon_tau}}, {{code|use weapon_gluon}}, {{code|use weapon_hivehand}}, {{code|use weapon_frag}}, {{code|use weapon_tripmine}}, {{code|use weapon_satchel}}, {{code|use weapon_snark}}  
|-  
| Misc  
| {{code|save quick}}, {{code|load quick}}, {{code|toggleconsole}}, <code><nowiki>incrementvar closecaption 0 1 1</nowiki></code>, {{code|+moveup}}, {{code|+movedown}}, {{code|+lookup}}, {{code|+lookdown}}, {{code|+left}}, {{code|+right}}  
|-  
| Multiplayer  
| {{code|chooseteam}}, {{code|+voicerecord}}  
|}  
  
=====mouseMap=====  
  
Maps Qt mouse button enum values and wheel direction strings to Source engine key name strings. References {{code|src/inputsystem/key_translation.cpp}} in a comment.  
  
{| class="standard-table"

====Mutable state properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|bindings}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| Command → key array map loaded from {{code|config.cfg}} by {{code|getBindings()}}. Keyed by command string; values are arrays of bound key name strings.  
|-  
| {{code|bindingsKeyMap}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| Reverse index of {{code|bindings}}. Keyed by key name string; values are arrays of command strings. Used for conflict detection.  
|-  
| {{code|inputBindingCallback}}  
| {{code|var}} (default {{code|null}})  
| The closure set during key capture. Non-null while capture is in progress.  
|-  
| {{code|isBinding}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| Computed as <code><nowiki>!!inputBindingCallback</nowiki></code>. {{code|true}} while key capture is active.  
|-  
| {{code|isSelected}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| Computed as <code><nowiki>!!selectedCfg</nowiki></code>. {{code|true}} while a binding cell is selected but capture has not yet started.  
|-  
| {{code|selectedCfg}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| The binding configuration object for the currently selected cell. Set by {{code|selectBinding()}}; cleared by {{code|clearSelection()}}.  
|-  
| {{code|conflicts}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| Array of human-readable command label strings that conflict with the key pressed during capture. {{code|null}} when no conflict is pending.  
|-  
| {{code|conflictCfg}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| Object containing {{code|bindingCfg}}, {{code|key}}, {{code|confirm}} (function), and {{code|cancel}} (function). Set when a conflict is detected; consumed by {{code|routeview-bindings.qml}} to display the conflict alert.  
|}  
  
====Signals====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Signal  
! Fired when  
|-  
| {{code|bindingSet()}}  
| A key is successfully bound (no conflict, or conflict confirmed).  
|-  
| {{code|bindingClear()}}  
| A binding is cleared via {{code|clearSelectedBinding()}}.  
|-  
| {{code|defaultsRestored()}}  
| Default bindings are restored via {{code|restoreDefaultBindings()}}.  
|}  
  
All three signals are connected in {{code|component-binding.qml}} to call {{code|refreshBindings()}}, which re-reads {{code|config.cfg}} and updates the displayed key labels.  
  
====Functions====  
  
=====Private helpers=====  
  
{{code|_readConfigFile(url, callback)}} — Opens a VFS file via {{code|XMLHttpRequest}}, splits the response on newlines, and matches each line against the regex <code><nowiki>/bind\s+"(.*)"\s+"(.*)"/</nowiki></code>. Builds a command → key array map and passes it to {{code|callback}}. Used to read both {{code|vfs:///cfg/config.cfg}} and {{code|vfs:///cfg/config_default.cfg}}.  
  
{{code|_saveBindingModifications()}} — Executes <code><nowiki>exec userconfig.cfg; host_writeconfig</nowiki></code> via {{code|BlackMesaEngine.executeClientCommandUnrestricted()}} to persist all in-memory binding changes to disk.  
  
{{code|_resetConflicts()}} — Sets both {{code|conflicts}} and {{code|conflictCfg}} to {{code|null}}.  
  
=====Public API=====  
  
{{code|getBindingComponentConfig(command)}} — Returns a plain object <code><nowiki>{label, type: 'binding', command, key1: '', key2: ''}</nowiki></code> for initializing a binding row in a {{code|ListModel}}.  
  
{{code|getBindings(commands, callback)}} — Reads {{code|config.cfg}}, populates {{code|bindings}} and {{code|bindingsKeyMap}}, then calls {{code|callback}} with a filtered map containing only the entries whose command strings appear in the {{code|commands}} array. Called by {{code|component-binding.qml}} on load and after every {{code|bindingSet}}/{{code|bindingClear}}/{{code|defaultsRestored}} signal.  
  
{{code|selectBinding(cfg)}} — Sets {{code|selectedCfg}} to {{code|cfg}}, making {{code|isSelected}} true. Called by {{code|component-optionslist-input-binding.qml}} when the user first clicks a {{code|BindingCell}}.  
  
{{code|clearSelection()}} — Sets {{code|selectedCfg}} to {{code|null}}.  
  
{{code|handleBindingResponse(event)}} — Invokes {{code|inputBindingCallback(event)}} and immediately sets {{code|inputBindingCallback}} to {{code|null}}, ending the capture session. Called by {{code|main.qml}} from three sources: {{code|onKeyCaptured}} (keyboard), a full-screen {{code|MouseArea}} {{code|onClicked}} (mouse buttons), and the same {{code|MouseArea}} {{code|onWheel}} (scroll wheel).  
  
{{code|startBindingRequest()}} — Begins the key capture sequence:  
# Snapshots {{code|selectedCfg}} into a local {{code|cfg}} variable.  
# Calls {{code|clearSelection()}} (sets {{code|isSelected}} to false).  
# Calls {{code|cfg.begin()}} to put the {{code|BindingCell}} into the {{code|"binding"}} visual state.  
# Calls {{code|BlackMesaEngine.requestKeyCapture()}} to tell the engine to route the next key event to the UI.  
# Sets {{code|inputBindingCallback}} to a closure that handles the captured event (see below).  
  
{{code|clearSelectedBinding()}} — Unbinds the key at {{code|selectedCfg[selectedCfg.ordinal]}} via {{code|BlackMesaEngine.executeClientCommandUnrestricted('unbind ...')}}, saves, and emits {{code|bindingClear()}}.  
  
{{code|restoreDefaultBindings()}} — Reads {{code|config_default.cfg}} via XHR (result is discarded), then executes {{code|unbindall}} and {{code|exec config_default.cfg}}, saves, and emits {{code|defaultsRestored()}}.  
  
====Key capture lifecycle====  
  
The full flow from user interaction to engine write:  
  
# User clicks a {{code|BindingCell}} (first click) → {{code|component-optionslist-input-binding.qml}} calls {{code|InputBindingsManager.selectBinding(cfg)}} → {{code|isSelected}} becomes {{code|true}}.  
# User clicks the same cell again (second click) → {{code|BindingCell.startBinding()}} calls {{code|InputBindingsManager.startBindingRequest()}} → {{code|isBinding}} becomes {{code|true}}.  
# {{code|main.qml}} activates a full-screen {{code|MouseArea}} at {{code|z: 2000}} (enabled only while {{code|isBinding}} is {{code|true}}) to intercept mouse input.  
# The engine fires {{code|onKeyCaptured}} (keyboard) or the {{code|MouseArea}} fires {{code|onClicked}}/{{code|onWheel}} → {{code|handleBindingResponse(event)}} is called.  
# Inside {{code|inputBindingCallback}}:  
#* If {{code|event.key [[=]] 'ESCAPE'}}: calls {{code|cancel()}}, which calls {{code|cfg.end()}} with the original key and resets conflicts. No binding change occurs.  
#* If the pressed key is already bound to a ''different'' command: sets {{code|conflicts}} and {{code|conflictCfg}}. The UI ({{code|routeview-bindings.qml}}) shows a conflict alert with Confirm/Cancel buttons. {{code|conflictCfg.confirm}} and {{code|conflictCfg.cancel}} are the resolution callbacks.  
#* Otherwise: calls the inner {{code|setBinding()}} helper, which executes {{code|unbind}} on the old key and {{code|bind KEY "COMMAND"}} for each of {{code|key1}} and {{code|key2}}, then saves and emits {{code|bindingSet()}}.  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''{{code|bindingsKeyMap.push(command)}} is a bug.''' In {{code|getBindings()}}, when a key is already present in {{code|bindingsKeyMap}}, the code calls {{code|bindingsKeyMap.push(command)}} instead of {{code|bindingsKeyMap[key].push(command)}}. {{code|bindingsKeyMap}} is a plain object, not an array, so {{code|push}} is {{code|undefined}} and this call throws a TypeError. The second command for any multiply-bound key is never added to the reverse map, meaning conflicts involving keys bound to more than one command are silently missed.  
  
2. '''{{code|restoreDefaultBindings}} reads {{code|config_default.cfg}} but discards the result.''' The XHR parse is entirely redundant. The function executes {{code|unbindall; exec config_default.cfg}} unconditionally regardless of the parsed content. The commented-out block that would have used the parsed data was never re-enabled.  
  
3. '''{{code|isBinding}} and {{code|isSelected}} are writable.''' Both are declared as {{code|property bool}} rather than {{code|readonly property bool}}. External code can overwrite them, breaking the invariant that they reflect {{code|!!inputBindingCallback}} and {{code|!!selectedCfg}} respectively.  
  
4. '''{{code|Qt.Return}} instead of {{code|Qt.Key_Return}}.''' The Enter key entry in {{code|keyMap}} uses {{code|Qt.Return}} while every other entry uses the {{code|Qt.Key_*}} form. {{code|Qt.Return}} is a valid alias in Qt 5 but is inconsistent with the rest of the map.  
  
5. '''{{code|onKeyCaptured}} escape-to-deselect branch references undefined {{code|event}}.''' In {{code|main.qml}}, the {{code|onKeyCaptured}} handler contains an {{code|else if}} branch that checks <code><nowiki>InputBindingsManager.isSelected && event.key === Qt.Key_Escape</nowiki></code>. The {{code|onKeyCaptured}} signal provides {{code|keyButtonCode}}, not {{code|event}}. The {{code|event}} identifier is undefined in this scope, so the condition always evaluates to {{code|false}} (or throws a TypeError). Pressing Escape while a cell is selected but not in capture mode does not clear the selection via this path.  
  
6. '''{{code|keyMap}} does not cover numpad keys.''' The Source engine supports {{code|KP_INS}}, {{code|KP_END}}, {{code|KP_UPARROW}}, etc. None of the {{code|Qt.Key_0}}–{{code|Qt.Key_9}} entries distinguish between the main row and the numpad ({{code|Qt.KeypadModifier}} is not checked), and no {{code|Qt.Key_*}} numpad-specific constants are mapped.

===l10n.qml===  
  
{{code|l10n.qml}} is the '''central localization singleton''' for the entire UI. It is registered in {{code|qmldir}} as {{code|singleton L10n 1.0 l10n.qml}} and imported implicitly by every QML file in the module. Its sole purpose is to expose all UI-facing strings as a single frozen JavaScript object tree, fetched from the engine's localization system at construction time.  
  
The file is 410 lines and is a {{code|pragma Singleton}} {{code|QtObject}}.  
  
====Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|strings}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| The full string map. Initialized by calling {{code|_getStringMap()}} at construction time. Not {{code|readonly}} — it can be reassigned from outside the singleton (see Notable Issues).  
|}  
  
====Functions====  
  
=====getString(key)=====  
  
A thin public wrapper around <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString(key)</nowiki></code>. Returns the localized string for the given engine localization key. Used internally by {{code|_getStringMap()}} to populate every entry in the string map, and also called directly by a small number of files (see Notable Issues).  
  
=====refreshStrings()=====  
  
Reassigns {{code|strings}} by calling {{code|_getStringMap()}} again. Intended to be called after a language change to rebuild the string map with the new locale. However, {{code|refreshStrings()}} is '''never called from any other QML file''' in the codebase — it is defined but has no callers. Language changes at runtime therefore cannot update the UI without a full restart.  
  
=====_getStringMap()=====  
  
Private function. Builds and returns the complete string map as a deeply frozen JavaScript object ({{code|Object.freeze()}} applied at every level). Called once at construction time and again if {{code|refreshStrings()}} is ever invoked. All string values are fetched via {{code|getString()}} at the moment of the call; the map is a snapshot, not a live binding.  
  
====strings — Top-Level Structure====  
  
The {{code|strings}} object has ten top-level keys, each a frozen sub-object.  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Key

=====options sub-object=====  
  
The {{code|options}} sub-object has three nested frozen objects.  
  
'''{{code|options.bindings}}''' — 49 entries mapping binding setting keys to their localized label strings. Grouped semantically into movement (7), combat (16), misc (10), multiplayer (2), and weapons (14). Used by {{code|component-binding-movement.qml}}, {{code|component-binding-combat.qml}}, {{code|component-binding-misc.qml}}, {{code|component-binding-weapons.qml}}, and {{code|component-binding-multiplayer.qml}}.  
  
'''{{code|options.labels}}''' — 92 entries providing row label strings for all options pages. Organized into logical groups by prefix:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Prefix  
! Count  
! Options page  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>game_</nowiki></code>  
| 12  
| {{code|routeview-game.qml}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>video_</nowiki></code> (setting labels)  
| 28  
| {{code|routeview-video.qml}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>video_desc_help_</nowiki></code>  
| 20  
| {{code|routeview-video.qml}} (option description panel)  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>video_desc_usage_</nowiki></code>  
| 7  
| {{code|routeview-video.qml}} (CPU/GPU/RAM impact labels)  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>audio_</nowiki></code>  
| 7  
| {{code|routeview-audio.qml}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>controller_</nowiki></code>  
| 7  
| {{code|routeview-input.qml}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>mouse_</nowiki></code>  
| 6  
| {{code|routeview-input.qml}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>binding_</nowiki></code>  
| 5  
| {{code|routeview-bindings.qml}} (category list labels)  
|}  
  
'''{{code|options.values}}''' — 69 entries providing localized display strings for select/carousel option values. Groups: boolean (no/yes/on/off), quality presets (Potato through Custom), difficulty levels, announcer types, aspect ratios, display modes, water detail levels, texture filtering modes, speaker configurations, captioning modes, and 29 language names.  
  
====Usage across the codebase====  
  
{{code|L10n.strings.*}} is the standard access pattern and is used by 22 QML files. The four files that call {{code|L10n.getString()}} directly — {{code|component-optionslist-input-select.qml}}, {{code|component-optionslist-row.qml}}, {{code|routeview-game.qml}}, and {{code|utils.qml}} — bypass the frozen map and always fetch a live value from the engine, meaning those strings would update immediately if {{code|refreshStrings()}} were ever called, while all other strings would not.  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''{{code|refreshStrings()}} is never called.''' The function is defined but has no callers in the codebase. A language change at runtime cannot update any {{code|L10n.strings.*}} binding without a full UI reload.  
  
2. '''{{code|strings}} is not {{code|readonly}}.''' The property is declared as {{code|property variant strings}}, not {{code|readonly property variant strings}}. Any component can reassign the entire string map from outside the singleton, bypassing {{code|Object.freeze()}}.  
  
3. '''{{code|video_desc_usage_ram}} key name is misleading.''' The property is named {{code|ram}} but its localization key is {{code|#QTUI_Option_Video_Desc_Usage_VRAM}}. The label describes VRAM (video memory), not system RAM.  
  
4. '''{{code|menus.addons}} is never used.''' {{code|component-header.qml}} uses {{code|L10n.strings.menus.installed}} ({{code|#QTUI_Menu_Workshop_Installed}}) for the Workshop Addons tab label, not {{code|menus.addons}} ({{code|#QTUI_Menu_Workshop_Addons}}). The {{code|addons}} key is defined but has no callers.  
  
5. '''{{code|menus.campaigns}} references an unreachable route.''' {{code|routeview-campaigns.qml}} is an unregistered stub with no entry in {{code|routes.qml}}. The {{code|menus.campaigns}} string is defined but cannot be displayed through any normal navigation flow.  
  
6. '''Trailing comma in {{code|navigation}} sub-object.''' The last entry ({{code|change_tabs}}) is followed by a trailing comma. This is valid in ES5+ JavaScript but is inconsistent with all other sub-objects in the file, which do not have trailing commas.  
  
7. '''{{code|options.labels}} mixes two localization key namespaces.''' Most keys use {{code|#QTUI_*}} keys, but {{code|game_multiplayermodel}} uses {{code|#GameUI_CharacterModel}} and {{code|game_multiplayerskin}} uses {{code|#GameUI_CharacterSkin}} — legacy {{code|GameUI}} keys from the Source engine's original localization system, inconsistent with the rest of the file.

===main.qml===  
  
{{code|main.qml}} is the '''root QML document''' of the Black Mesa menu UI. It is the top-level {{code|Item}} instantiated by the engine and acts as the orchestration layer: it owns all global state, wires the engine's C++ signals to the QML layer, and composes the three primary visual regions ({{code|Header}}, {{code|Router}}, {{code|Footer}}) into a single full-screen layout.  
  
It is not registered in {{code|qmldir}} and is not a named component — it is loaded directly by the engine as the entry point.  
  
====Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Default  
! Purpose  
|- 
| {{code|emptyRoute}}  
| {{code|readonly Route}}  
| <code><nowiki>Routes.empty</nowiki></code>  
| Convenience alias for the empty route, used throughout the engine event handlers.  
|-  
| {{code|mainMenuIndexRoute}}  
| {{code|readonly Route}}  
| <code><nowiki>Routes.index</nowiki></code>  
| Convenience alias for the index route.  
|-  
| {{code|isLoadingLevel}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| Set to {{code|true}} between {{code|onLevelLoadingStarted}} and {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}}. Hides the {{code|Header}} and suppresses wallpaper animations during loads.  
|-  
| {{code|didLoadLevel}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| Hack flag: set to {{code|true}} in {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}} and consumed in {{code|onGameUIHidden}} to distinguish a level-load hide (no fade animation) from a player-initiated ESC hide (fade animation).

| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| Custom in-game tracking flag. Not derived from {{code|BlackMesaEngine.isInGame()}} directly because the engine considers the main menu background map to be "in game". Updated in multiple engine event handlers.

| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}

| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| Set to {{code|true}} while a save is in progress. Disables the {{code|appRoot}} opacity animation and hides the root panel to prevent the menu overlay from appearing in the save thumbnail screenshot.  
|-  
| {{code|_isInitialLoad}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|true}}  
| Tracks whether the UI has ever completed a level load. Cleared in {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}} and in {{code|autoBackgroundMusicTimer}}. Used to suppress the "in game" state and loading progress bar on the very first startup load.

| {{code|loadingErrorFlag}}  
| {{code|bool}}

|-  
| {{code|levelLoadingFinished_FailureReason}}  
| {{code|string}}

|-  
| {{code|loadPercent}}  
| {{code|real}}  
| {{code|0.0}}  
| Updated by {{code|onUpdateProgressValue}}. Passed to {{code|Footer}} to drive the loading progress bar.  
|-  
| {{code|isMenuBlocked}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| Set to {{code|true}} during the {{code|appRoot}} opacity animation. The {{code|Connections}} block is {{code|enabled: !isMenuBlocked}}, so all engine signals are ignored while the menu is animating open or closed.  
|}  
  
====Theme Scaling Bindings====  
  
Two {{code|Binding}} elements keep {{code|Theme.widthScale}} and {{code|Theme.heightScale}} in sync with the window dimensions at all times:  
  
* <code><nowiki>Theme.widthScale = appRoot.width / 1920</nowiki></code>  
* <code><nowiki>Theme.heightScale = appRoot.height / 1080</nowiki></code>  
  
{{code|onWidthChanged}} and {{code|onHeightChanged}} additionally write {{code|Theme.rootWidth}} and {{code|Theme.rootHeight}} (which cannot be driven by {{code|Binding}} because they are plain writable properties, not bindable targets in the same way) and call {{code|wallpaper.updateBackgroundImage()}} to re-evaluate the widescreen threshold.  
  
====Focus, Visibility, and Key Handling====  
  
{{code|appRoot}} holds {{code|focus: true}} so it is the root of the keyboard focus chain. {{code|Keys.onPressed}} delegates all key events to {{code|BlackMesaUtils.navigationUIHandle(event)}} unless {{code|BlackMesaUtils.disableUINavigation}} is {{code|true}} (set by {{code|InputBindingsManager}} during key capture).  
  
{{code|onVisibleChanged}} calls {{code|BlackMesaEngine.setRootPanelVisibility(visible)}} to notify the engine whenever the root panel is shown or hidden.  
  
====Opacity Animation====  
  
A {{code|Behavior on opacity}} wraps the root fade in a {{code|SequentialAnimation}}:  
  
# Sets {{code|isMenuBlocked [[=]] true}} (disabling engine signal processing).  
# Runs a 625 ms {{code|NumberAnimation}} on {{code|appRoot.opacity}}.  
# Sets {{code|isMenuBlocked [[=]] false}}.  
  
The behavior is {{code|enabled: !didLoadLevel && !appRoot.isSaving}}, so it is bypassed when the UI is hiding due to a completed level load (instant cut) or a save operation (instant hide for screenshot).  
  
====Layout====  
  
The three primary UI regions are arranged in a {{code|GridLayout}} with {{code|flow: GridLayout.TopToBottom}} and {{code|rows: 3}}:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Row  
! Component  
! {{code|id}}  
! Height  
|-  
| 1  
| {{code|Header}}  
| {{code|header}}  
| <code><nowiki>128 * Theme.heightScale</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| 2  
| {{code|Router}}  
| {{code|router}}  
| Fill remaining height ({{code|Layout.fillHeight: true}})  
|-  
| 3  
| {{code|Footer}}  
| {{code|footer}}  
| <code><nowiki>72 * Theme.heightScale</nowiki></code>  
|}  
  
=====Wallpaper=====  
  
A {{code|Wallpaper}} ({{code|component-wallpaper.qml}}) fills the entire {{code|appRoot}} behind the layout. It is not part of the {{code|GridLayout}}. Key bindings:  
  
* <code><nowiki>showSkrim: isInGame || router.currentRoute !== Routes.index</nowiki></code> — applies a dark overlay whenever the player is in-game or on any route other than the main index.  
* <code><nowiki>darkenSkrim: isInGame</nowiki></code> — uses the darker skrim opacity ({{code|Theme.opacity.pageSkrimDark}}) when in-game.  
* <code><nowiki>suppressAnimations: isLoadingLevel</nowiki></code> — disables crossfade animations during level loads to reduce GPU load.  
  
{{code|Wallpaper}} internally maintains a map of 19 background image keys ({{code|background01}}–{{code|background19}}) to {{code|.vtf}} file paths, with separate widescreen variants. The widescreen path is chosen when <code><nowiki>appRoot.width / appRoot.height >= 1.586</nowiki></code>. It also renders a bottom-right Black Mesa logo watermark, a left-edge gradient using {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}}, and a secondary gradient using {{code|Theme.colors.subNavBackground}}, all of which fade in/out via {{code|showExtras}}.  
  
=====Header=====  
  
The {{code|Header}} ({{code|component-header.qml}}) is the top navigation bar. It is passed:  
  
* {{code|currentRoute: router.currentRoute}} — used to highlight the active main-nav and sub-nav tabs and to determine the header state.  
* {{code|isInGame: appRoot.isInGame}} — used to enable/disable the Save Game sub-nav button.  
* {{code|hide: true}} — starts hidden; set to {{code|false}} by engine event handlers once the UI is ready.  
* {{code|visible: !appRoot.isLoadingLevel}} — completely hidden (not just off-screen) during level loads.  
  
The header has three states: {{code|hidden}}, {{code|subnav-closed}}, and {{code|subnav-open}}. State transitions are 250 ms {{code|NumberAnimation}} slides using {{code|Easing.OutQuint}}. The sub-nav row switches its {{code|ListModel}} based on the first path segment of {{code|currentRoute.path}} ({{code|campaign}}, {{code|multiplayer}}, {{code|options}}, {{code|steamworkshop}}).  
  
The header also executes <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.executeClientCommandUnrestricted("exec server.cfg;")</nowiki></code> in the {{code|multiplayerNavModel.Component.onCompleted}} handler to pre-load server configuration when the multiplayer sub-nav model is first created.  
  
=====Router=====  
  
The {{code|Router}} ({{code|router.qml}}) fills the space between the header and footer. It receives {{code|isInGame: appRoot.isInGame}} and propagates three signals upward to {{code|main.qml}}:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Signal  
! Handler in {{code|main.qml}}  
! Action  
|-  
| {{code|onLoadGameRequest}}  
| Sets {{code|footer.levelName}} to {{code|savegame.chapterName}}  
| Calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.loadGame(savegame.savename)}}  
|-  
| {{code|onLoadMapRequest}}  
| Sets {{code|footer.levelName}} to {{code|mapname}}  
| Calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.loadMap(filename)}}  
|-  
| {{code|onSaveGameRequest}}  
| Sets {{code|appRoot.isSaving [[=]] true}}, hides {{code|appRoot}}  
| Calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.saveGame(filename, callback)}}; callback resets {{code|isSaving}}  
|}  
  
The save handler hides {{code|appRoot}} before saving so the menu overlay does not appear in the save thumbnail. A TODO comment acknowledges this as a hack requiring proper state management.  
  
=====Footer=====  
  
The {{code|Footer}} ({{code|component-footer.qml}}) is the bottom bar. It switches between two {{code|Component}} instances via a {{code|Loader}}:  
  
* '''{{code|menuFooter}}''' — shown when {{code|isLoading}} is {{code|false}}. Contains a compass icon, a Quit button, a Back/Back to Game button, a {{code|MenuNavGuide}}, and a Discord link.  
* '''{{code|loadingFooter}}''' — shown when {{code|isLoading}} is {{code|true}}. Contains a rotating loading quote (cycling every 5 seconds from {{code|L10n.strings.loading.quotes}}), a level name label, and a progress bar driven by {{code|loadPercent}}.  
  
The Quit button routes to {{code|Routes.savebeforequit}} when {{code|isInGame}} is {{code|true}}, or {{code|Routes.quit}} otherwise. The Back button routes to {{code|Routes.back}} (pop) when {{code|_showBackButton}} is {{code|true}}, or calls {{code|BlackMesaEngine.executeClientCommand('gameui_hide')}} when {{code|_showBackToGameButton}} is {{code|true}}.  
  
The Discord link opens {{code|BlackMesaUtils.discordLink}} via {{code|Qt.openUrlExternally}} after a 500 ms {{code|setTimeout}} delay (to allow the click sound to play first).  
  
====Binding Capture MouseArea====  
  
A full-screen {{code|MouseArea}} with {{code|z: 2000}} sits above all other content. It is {{code|enabled}} only when {{code|InputBindingsManager.isBinding}} is {{code|true}}. When active, it intercepts all mouse button clicks and scroll wheel events and forwards them to {{code|InputBindingsManager.handleBindingResponse}} with the appropriate key string from {{code|InputBindingsManager.mouseMap}}.  
  
====Engine Event Handlers====  
  
All engine signals are handled in a single {{code|Connections}} block targeting {{code|BlackMesaEngine}}, with {{code|ignoreUnknownSignals: true}} and {{code|enabled: !isMenuBlocked}}.  
  
=====onKeyCaptured=====  
  
Converts the raw {{code|keyButtonCode}} to a string via {{code|BlackMesaEngine.keyButtonCodeToString()}}. If {{code|InputBindingsManager.isBinding}} is {{code|true}}, forwards to {{code|handleBindingResponse}}. If a binding cell is selected and Escape is pressed, calls {{code|InputBindingsManager.clearSelection()}}.  
  
=====onGameUIActived=====  
  
Note: the signal name is {{code|onGameUIActived}} (missing the 't' in "Activated") — this matches the engine's signal name.  
  
# Calls {{code|BlackMesaEngine.grabInputForGameUI()}} to capture keyboard/mouse input.  
# Recomputes {{code|isInGame}}: {{code|true}} only if the engine reports in-game, {{code|_isInitialLoad}} is {{code|false}}, the level is not the main menu background, and no level is currently loading.  
# On the initial load, calls {{code|wallpaper.updateBackgroundImage()}} with the main menu background name.  
# If {{code|loadingErrorFlag}} is set, clears it and routes to {{code|Routes.multiplayer}} to show the server list after a failed connection.  
# Otherwise, if not currently loading and either this is the initial load or the UI was not already visible, unhides the header and routes to {{code|Routes.index}}.  
# Makes {{code|appRoot}} visible and forces active focus.  
# If single-player and in-game, executes {{command|setpause}} with {{code|nomsg}} to pause the game silently.  
  
=====onGameUIHidden=====  
  
# Resets {{code|didLoadLevel}} to {{code|false}}.  
# Closes the dev console.  
# Hides {{code|appRoot}}.  
# Hides the header (the footer fades with {{code|appRoot}} and does not need explicit hiding).  
# Routes to {{code|emptyRoute}}, passing {{code|didLoadLevel}} as the {{code|immediate}} flag — if {{code|true}}, the transition is instant (level just loaded); if {{code|false}}, the normal fade-out animation plays.  
# Calls {{code|BlackMesaEngine.releaseInputFromGameUI()}}.  
# If single-player and in-game, executes {{command|unpause}} with {{code|nomsg}}.  
  
=====onLevelLoadingStarted=====  
  
# Executes {{command|unpause}} with {{code|nomsg}}.  
# Determines {{code|shouldUpdateProgressBar}}: {{code|true}} if the engine's {{code|showProgressDialog}} flag is set, or if the level is the main menu background, or if the level name is empty.  
# If {{code|shouldUpdateProgressBar}} and the UI is already visible, manually fires {{code|BlackMesaEngine.gameUIActived()}} to trigger the {{code|onGameUIActived}} handler. This is acknowledged as a cross-handler hack.  
# Updates the wallpaper to the loading level's background.  
# Calls {{code|BlackMesaEngine.setShouldUpdateProgressBar(shouldUpdateProgressBar)}}.  
# Releases input, sets {{code|isInGame [[=]] false}}, {{code|isLoadingLevel [[=]] true}}, shows the wallpaper image, hides extras, stops the background music timer, routes to {{code|emptyRoute}} immediately, and hides the header.  
  
=====onLevelLoadingFinished=====  
  
The most complex handler. Receives {{code|isError}}, {{code|failureReason}}, {{code|showProgressDialog}}, and {{code|levelName}} as injected variables from the engine.  
  
# Clears {{code|_isInitialLoad}}, {{code|isLoadingLevel}}, sets {{code|didLoadLevel [[=]] true}}.  
# Recomputes {{code|isInGame}} and {{code|isInMultiplayerMode}}.  
# Resets {{code|loadPercent}} and {{code|footer.levelName}}.  
  
Then branches on three cases:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Case  
! Condition  
! Actions  
|-  
| Load error  
| <code><nowiki>isError === true</nowiki></code>  
| Stores {{code|failureReason}}, shows {{code|alertLoadFailed}}, sets {{code|loadingErrorFlag [[=]] true}}, resets {{code|didLoadLevel}} and {{code|isInGame}} to {{code|false}}.  
|-  
| Main menu background loaded  
| <code><nowiki>isLevelMainMenuBackground</nowiki></code>  
| Calls {{code|Sound.startMusic()}}, unhides header, routes to index, updates wallpaper, grabs input.  
|-  
| Game level loaded  
| else  
| Sets {{code|didLoadLevel [[=]] false}}, {{code|isInGame [[=]] false}}, unhides header, calls {{code|Sound.stopMusic()}}, routes to index, updates wallpaper.  
|}  
  
The "game level loaded" branch sets {{code|isInGame [[=]] false}} temporarily. The correct {{code|isInGame}} value is recomputed in the subsequent {{code|onGameUIActived}} call when the player opens the menu.  
  
=====onDisconnectedFromServer=====  
  
Updates both {{code|footer.isInGame}} and {{code|appRoot.isInGame}} from {{code|BlackMesaEngine.isInGame()}} filtered by {{code|isLevelMainMenuBackground()}}.  
  
=====onUpdateProgressValue=====  
  
Sets {{code|appRoot.loadPercent [[=]] progressValue}}. Drives the loading progress bar in {{code|loadingFooter}}.  
  
====autoBackgroundMusicTimer====  
  
A one-shot {{code|Timer}} with a 1250 ms interval that starts running immediately on construction. It is a fallback for the case where the engine never fires {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}} (e.g., the UI is loaded without a level load event, or the engine starts in a state where the loading sequence is already complete).  
  
On trigger:  

1. Sets {{code|_isInitialLoad [[=]] false}} and {{code|isLoadingLevel [[=]] false}}.  

2. Unhides the header.  

3. Shows wallpaper extras.  

4. Routes to {{code|Routes.index}}.  

5. Updates the wallpaper.  

6. Calls {{code|Sound.startMusic()}}.  
  
The timer is stopped in {{code|onLevelLoadingStarted}} to prevent it from firing during a level load. A TODO comment describes this as "bitfucking".  
  
====alertLoadFailed====  
  
An inline {{code|Alert}} ({{code|component-alert.qml}}) shown when a level load fails (e.g., failed server connection). It uses {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString()}} directly for both its title ({{code|#BlackMesaUI_Error}}) and button label ({{code|#GameUI_OK}}), bypassing the {{code|L10n}} singleton. It has a single {{code|DefaultButton}} that resets {{code|levelLoadingFinished_FailureReason}} and calls {{code|alertLoadFailed.hide()}}.  
  
The {{code|Alert}} component itself ({{code|component-alert.qml}}) is a full-screen {{code|MouseArea}} that intercepts all pointer events to prevent click-through. It animates the modal panel in from {{code|y+300}} over 600 ms using {{code|Easing.InOutQuint}}, and out to {{code|y+300}} then immediately to {{code|y+30000}} (off-screen) on hide. On show, it calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements()}} and {{code|BlackMesaUtils.afterTraverseCallback()}} after a 450 ms delay to register the alert's buttons with the controller navigation system.  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''{{code|onGameUIActived}} typo.''' The signal handler name is missing the 't' in "Activated". This matches the engine's signal name ({{code|gameUIActived}}), so it works correctly, but it is a persistent typo in both the engine and the QML layer.  
  
2. '''Cross-handler invocation in {{code|onLevelLoadingStarted}}.''' The handler manually calls {{code|BlackMesaEngine.gameUIActived()}} to trigger {{code|onGameUIActived}} under certain conditions. The comment acknowledges this as "not good" and explains that it is the reason for the complex conditional logic in {{code|onGameUIActived}}.  
  
3. '''{{code|isInGame}} is set to {{code|false}} in the "game level loaded" branch of {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}}.''' This is a temporary state: the correct value is recomputed in the next {{code|onGameUIActived}}. However, between {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}} and {{code|onGameUIActived}}, any code that reads {{code|appRoot.isInGame}} will see {{code|false}} even though the player is in-game.  
  
4. '''{{code|_isInitialLoad}} check in {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}} line 301 is redundant.''' {{code|_isInitialLoad}} is set to {{code|false}} on the immediately preceding line, so the expression <code><nowiki>!_isInitialLoad && ...</nowiki></code> always evaluates to {{code|true && ...}}.  
  
5. '''{{code|autoBackgroundMusicTimer}} and {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}} both clear {{code|_isInitialLoad}}.''' If the engine fires {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}} normally, the timer fires 1250 ms later and redundantly re-runs the index routing and music start logic. The timer is not stopped in {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}}.  
  
6. '''{{code|component-wallpaper.qml}} has mismatched widescreen file mappings.''' {{code|background06_widescreen}} maps to {{code|background02_widescreen.vtf}} (should be {{code|background06_widescreen.vtf}}). {{code|background09_widescreen}}, {{code|background10_widescreen}}, and {{code|background11_widescreen}} are each off by one index.  
  
7. '''{{code|component-wallpaper.qml}} references {{code|subNavBackground}} without the {{code|Theme.colors.}} prefix.''' The gradient stop on line 147 uses the bare identifier {{code|subNavBackground}}, which is not in scope. This resolves to {{code|undefined}}, making the gradient stop effectively transparent.  
  
8. '''{{code|alertLoadFailed}} bypasses {{code|L10n}}.''' Both the title and button label use {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString()}} directly, inconsistent with all other alert dialogs in the codebase.  
  
9. '''{{code|component-alert.qml}} positions the hidden modal at {{code|anchors.verticalCenterOffset: 30000}}.''' This is a hack to move the modal off-screen in the hidden state without using {{code|visible: false}} (which would prevent the show animation from running). The value 30000 is large enough to guarantee the modal is off-screen at any resolution.  
  
10. '''{{code|Connections}} block is disabled during menu animation.''' Setting {{code|enabled: !isMenuBlocked}} means that if the engine fires {{code|onGameUIHidden}} or {{code|onLevelLoadingStarted}} while the 625 ms open/close animation is playing, those signals are silently dropped. This is the intended behavior (to prevent visual artifacts from rapid ESC presses), but it means the UI can get into an inconsistent state if the engine fires critical signals during the animation window.

===qmldir. ===  
  
{{code|qmldir}} is the QML module manifest for the Black Mesa UI module. It is read by the QML engine at startup to register all named types and singletons that can be imported by other {{code|.qml}} files in the same module. It does not need to be explicitly imported — all registered types are available globally within the module.  
  
The file has 40 lines and registers 34 named types across six logical groups, separated by blank lines.  
  
====Format====  
  
Each non-blank line follows one of two forms:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Form  
! Meaning  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>singleton TypeName 1.0 filename.qml</nowiki></code>  
| Registers a {{code|pragma Singleton}} {{code|QtObject}} as a module-wide singleton. Only one instance exists for the lifetime of the application.  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>TypeName 1.0 filename.qml</nowiki></code>  
| Registers a reusable component. A new instance is created each time the type is used in QML.  
|}  
  
The version number is {{code|1.0}} for every entry.  
  
====Singletons (lines 1–6)====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Registered name  
! Source file  
! Purpose  
|-  
| {{code|L10n}}  
| {{code|l10n.qml}}  
| Localization singleton. Exposes all UI strings via {{code|L10n.strings.*}} and provides {{code|getDateTimeString()}} and locale helpers.  
|-  
| {{code|Theme}}  
| {{code|theme.qml}}  
| Design system singleton. Provides scaling properties ({{code|widthScale}}, {{code|heightScale}}), colors, opacities, sounds, fonts, and the locale dictionary.  
|-  
| {{code|Sound}}  
| {{code|sound.qml}}  
| Audio singleton. Wraps {{code|BlackMesaEngine}} audio calls for music ({{code|startMusic}}, {{code|stopMusic}}) and UI sound effects ({{code|playEffect}}).  
|-  
| {{code|BlackMesaUtils}}  
| {{code|utils.qml}}  
| Utility singleton. Provides save/load game helpers, a {{code|setTimeout}} polyfill, and the entire keyboard/controller UI navigation system ({{code|navigationUIHandle}}, {{code|traverseUIElements}}, etc.).  
|-  
| {{code|InputBindingsManager}}  
| {{code|input-bindings-manager.qml}}  
| Key binding singleton. Manages the in-memory binding state, conflict detection, and flush/reset operations for the bindings page.  
|-  
| {{code|Routes}}  
| {{code|routes.qml}}  
| Route registry singleton. Declares all 22 named {{code|Route}} objects as {{code|readonly property Route}} members.  
|}  
  
====Primitive (line 8)====

! Purpose  
|-  
| {{code|Triangle}}  
| {{code|triangle.qml}}  
| A clipped, rotated {{code|Rectangle}} that renders a triangle. Controlled by a {{code|corner}} property (0–3) and a {{code|color}} alias.  
|}

|-  
| {{code|Router}}  
| {{code|router.qml}}

|-  
| {{code|VerticalScrollBar}}  
| {{code|component-scrollbar-vertical.qml}}

|-  
| {{code|BindingList}}  
| {{code|component-binding.qml}}  
| The key binding list for a single category. Renders a {{code|ListView}} of binding rows, each with a label and one or two {{code|BindingCell}} inputs.  
|-

|-

| {{code|component-menu-navigation-guide.qml}}

====Files present in the directory but not registered====  
  
The following {{code|.qml}} files exist in {{code|bms/ui/}} but have no entry in {{code|qmldir}}. They are either loaded by filename string at runtime, used as inline sub-components via {{code|Qt.createComponent}}, or are unreachable stubs.  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! File  
! Reason not registered  
|-  
| {{code|main.qml}}  
| The application root. Loaded directly by the engine as the entry point, not imported as a named type.  
|-  
| {{code|devtest.qml}}
