====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''No explicit module declaration.''' A {{code|qmldir}} file can begin with a {{code|module}} directive (e.g. <code><nowiki>module com.crowbarcollective.blackmesa</nowiki></code>) to give the module a qualified import path. This file has none, meaning the module is identified only by its file system location and is imported implicitly by the engine rather than via an {{code|import}} statement.  
  
2. '''All versions are {{code|1.0}}.''' No component has been versioned beyond {{code|1.0}}. QML's versioning system allows older consumers to pin to a specific API version, but since all types share the same version, the versioning provides no practical isolation.  
  
3. '''{{code|BindingCell}} is registered but its sibling input types are not.''' {{code|component-optionslist-input-bindingcell.qml}} is registered as {{code|BindingCell 1.0}}, but the six other input types (slider, select, checkbox, map, binding, separator, text) are not registered. This inconsistency suggests {{code|BindingCell}} was promoted to a named type because it is also used directly by {{code|component-binding.qml}} outside of the {{code|OptionsListRow}} context, while the other input types are only ever used inside {{code|OptionsListRow}}.

===route.qml===  
  
{{code|route.qml}} is the '''Route data component'''. It defines the {{code|Route}} type used by {{code|routes.qml}} to declare every named navigation destination in the menu system. It is registered in {{code|qmldir}} as {{code|Route 1.0}}.  
  
{{code|Route}} is a pure data object — it has no visual content and emits no signals. All 22 route instances in {{code|routes.qml}} are {{code|readonly property Route}} children of the {{code|Routes}} singleton.  
  
====Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Default  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|name}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| (empty)  
| Unique string identifier for the route. Used as the lookup key in <code><nowiki>Routes[newRouteName]</nowiki></code> and stored in {{code|RouteView.routeName}} after a push or replace.  
|-  
| {{code|path}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| (empty)  
| Dot-separated hierarchy string (e.g. <code><nowiki>"campaign.newgame"</nowiki></code>). Used exclusively by {{code|component-header.qml}} for tab highlighting and sub-nav visibility. Not used by the router.  
|-  
| {{code|qmlFile}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| (empty)  
| Filename of the {{code|RouteView}} QML file to load (e.g. <code><nowiki>"routeview-newgame.qml"</nowiki></code>). Passed directly to <code><nowiki>outlet[config.fn](route.qmlFile, ...)</nowiki></code> in {{code|router.qml}}.  
|-  
| {{code|alert}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| If {{code|true}}, the route is always pushed onto the {{code|StackView}} (never replaced), can only be dismissed by a pop, and causes {{code|component-header.qml}} to hide the navigation bar.  
|-  
| {{code|action}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| (empty)  
| If non-empty, the router executes <code><nowiki>actions[route.action]()</nowiki></code> and returns immediately without loading any {{code|RouteView}}. Currently only {{code|"OPEN_STEAMWORKSHOP"}} is defined.  
|-  
| {{code|passthrough}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| If {{code|true}}, the router immediately resolves the route to {{code|Routes[route.destination]}} before any further processing. Used for top-level category routes ({{code|campaign}}, {{code|multiplayer}}, {{code|options}}, {{code|steamworkshop}}) that have no view of their own.  
|-  
| {{code|destination}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| (empty)  
| The {{code|name}} of the route to redirect to when {{code|passthrough}} is {{code|true}}.  
|-  
| {{code|rank}}  
| {{code|real}}  
| {{code|0.0}}  
| Numeric rank relative to all other routes. Used by {{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}} in {{code|router.qml}} to select a left or right slide transition: <code><nowiki>currentRoute.rank > route.rank ? 'right' : 'left'</nowiki></code>. Only applied when both the current and new routes are non-alert, non-index, non-empty routes (the {{code|replace}} case).  
|}  
  
====How each property is consumed====  
  
=====name=====  
  
{{code|router.qml}} looks up routes by name via <code><nowiki>Routes[newRouteName]</nowiki></code>. After a push or replace, {{code|route.name}} is written to the new {{code|RouteView}}'s {{code|routeName}} property. After a pop, {{code|currentRoute}} is restored by reading <code><nowiki>Routes[outlet.currentItem.routeName]</nowiki></code>.  
  
{{code|name}} is also compared directly in transition logic: <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.back.name</nowiki></code>, <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code>, <code><nowiki>currentRoute.name === Routes.index.name</nowiki></code>.  
  
=====path=====  
  
Used only by {{code|component-header.qml}} in three ways:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Expression  
! Purpose  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>currentRoute.path.indexOf('.') === -1</nowiki></code>  
| Determines whether to show the sub-nav bar. A path with no dot (e.g. {{code|"index"}}, {{code|"achievements"}}) means no sub-nav; a dotted path (e.g. {{code|"campaign.newgame"}}) means show sub-nav.  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>currentRoute.path.split('.')[0] === routeName</nowiki></code>  
| Determines which main nav tab (Campaign, Multiplayer, Options, etc.) is highlighted as {{code|selected}}.  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>currentRoute.path.split('.')[1] === routeName</nowiki></code>  
| Determines which sub-nav tab (New Game, Load Game, etc.) is highlighted as {{code|selected}}.  
|}  
  
=====alert=====  
  
Checked in three places:  
  
1. {{code|router.qml}} — {{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}} uses the {{code|up}} transition for alert routes and always uses {{code|push}} (never {{code|replace}}). When going back from an alert route, the {{code|down}} transition is used instead of {{code|up}}.  

2. {{code|component-header.qml}} — {{code|setState()}} sets the header state to {{code|"hidden"}} when <code><nowiki>currentRoute.alert</nowiki></code> is {{code|true}}, hiding both the main nav and sub-nav bars.  

3. {{code|component-optionslist.qml}} — checks {{code|currentRoute.alert}} to suppress the settings-flush confirmation when an alert is active.  
  
=====action=====  
  
Checked in {{code|router.qml}} after passthrough resolution and same-route deduplication, but before transition logic. If {{code|route.action}} is non-empty, the router calls <code><nowiki>actions[route.action]()</nowiki></code> and returns without modifying the stack or {{code|currentRoute}}. The only defined action is {{code|OPEN_STEAMWORKSHOP}}, which opens the Steam Workshop overlay via {{code|BlackMesaEngine.activateSteamOverlayToWebPage}}.  
  
=====passthrough / destination=====  
  
The first check in {{code|setRoute()}}. If <code><nowiki>route.passthrough</nowiki></code> is {{code|true}}, the local {{code|route}} variable is immediately replaced with <code><nowiki>Routes[route.destination]</nowiki></code>. All subsequent logic (same-route check, action check, transition config, push/replace) operates on the resolved destination route, not the passthrough route. The passthrough route itself is never pushed onto the stack and never becomes {{code|currentRoute}}.  
  
=====rank=====  
  
Used only in the {{code|else}} branch of {{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}}, which is reached when both the current and new routes are non-alert, non-index, non-empty, and non-back. In this case the router uses {{code|replace}} (not {{code|push}}) and selects a horizontal slide direction based on rank comparison. Higher rank = further right in the conceptual hierarchy. Navigating to a higher-rank route slides left (new content enters from the right); navigating to a lower-rank route slides right.  
  
The rank values assigned in {{code|routes.qml}} are:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Route group  
! Routes and ranks  
|-  
| Campaign  
| {{code|campaign}} 1.0, {{code|newgame}} 1.1, {{code|loadgame}} 1.2, {{code|savegame}} 1.3  
|-  
| Multiplayer  
| {{code|multiplayer}} 2.0, {{code|join}} 2.1, {{code|host}} 2.2  
|-  
| Options  
| {{code|options}} 3.0, {{code|game}} 3.1, {{code|input}} 3.2, {{code|bindings}} 3.3, {{code|video}} 3.4, {{code|audio}} 3.5, {{code|policy}} 3.6, {{code|translators}} 3.7  
|-  
| Achievements  
| {{code|achievements}} 4.0  
|-  
| Workshop  
| {{code|steamworkshop}} 5.0, {{code|addons}} 5.1, {{code|browse}} 5.2  
|-  
| Special (no rank / 0.0)  
| {{code|back}}, {{code|empty}}, {{code|index}}, {{code|quit}}, {{code|savebeforequit}}  
|}  
  
====Notable Issues====  

1. '''{{code|anchors.fill: parent}} on a data object.''' {{code|Route}} extends {{code|Item}} and sets {{code|anchors.fill: parent}}, making each {{code|Route}} instance a full-screen visual item parented to the {{code|Routes}} singleton {{code|Item}}. Since {{code|Route}} renders nothing, this is unnecessary overhead. The commented-out {{code|// width: parent.width}} and {{code|// height: parent.height}} lines suggest {{code|anchors.fill}} was a later substitution. A {{code|QtObject}} base would be more appropriate for a pure data type.  
  
2. '''{{code|action}} and {{code|qmlFile}} are mutually exclusive but not enforced.''' If both are set on a route, {{code|action}} takes priority and {{code|qmlFile}} is silently ignored. No validation or warning is produced.  
  
3. '''{{code|passthrough}} and {{code|action}} interaction is undefined.''' If a route has both {{code|passthrough: true}} and {{code|action}} set, the passthrough is resolved first, then {{code|action}} is checked on the ''resolved'' route — not the original. The {{code|action}} on the passthrough route itself is never evaluated.  
  
4. '''{{code|back}} is a sentinel, not a real route.''' {{code|Routes.back}} has only {{code|name: "back"}} set. It has no {{code|path}}, {{code|qmlFile}}, {{code|rank}}, or any other property. The router detects it by name comparison before any other logic and converts it to a {{code|pop}} operation. It is never pushed onto the stack and never becomes {{code|currentRoute}}.  
  
5. '''{{code|rank}} is a {{code|real}}, not an {{code|int}}.''' This allows fractional ranks (e.g. {{code|1.1}}, {{code|3.4}}) to express ordering within a group without requiring a global sequential integer scheme. However, ranks across groups (e.g. navigating from {{code|audio}} rank 3.5 to {{code|achievements}} rank 4.0) will also produce a left-slide transition, which may not always be the intended visual direction for cross-group navigation.

===router.qml===  
  
{{code|router.qml}} is the '''central navigation controller''' for the entire menu system. It is registered in {{code|qmldir}} as {{code|Router 1.0}} (not a singleton) and instantiated once in {{code|main.qml}} as {{code|Router { id: router }}}. It extends {{code|StackView}} from {{code|QtQuick.Controls 2.2}}, using the stack to manage the lifecycle of {{code|RouteView}} instances.  
  
====Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|currentRoute}}  
| {{code|Route}}  
| The {{code|Route}} object corresponding to the currently active view. Updated by {{code|setRoute()}} after every successful navigation.  
|-  
| {{code|isInGame}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| Bound to {{code|appRoot.isInGame}} in {{code|main.qml}}. Passed to each new {{code|RouteView}} as a construction-time property.  
|}  
  
====Signals====  
  
Three signals are declared on the router and re-emitted upward to {{code|main.qml}}, which handles the actual engine calls:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Signal  
! Handler in {{code|main.qml}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>saveGameRequest(string filename)</nowiki></code>  
| Hides {{code|appRoot}}, calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.saveGame(filename, callback)}}, restores visibility in the callback.  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>loadGameRequest(variant savegame)</nowiki></code>  
| Sets {{code|footer.levelName}} to {{code|savegame.chapterName}}, calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.loadGame(savegame.savename)}}.  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>loadMapRequest(string filename, string mapname)</nowiki></code>  
| Sets {{code|footer.levelName}} to {{code|mapname}}, calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.loadMap(filename)}}.  
|}  
  
====Initialization====  
  
{{code|Component.onCompleted}} calls <code><nowiki>outlet.setRoute(emptyRoute.name)</nowiki></code>. {{code|emptyRoute}} is not defined in {{code|router.qml}}; it resolves from the parent scope in {{code|main.qml}} where <code><nowiki>readonly property Route emptyRoute: Routes.empty</nowiki></code> is declared. This is an implicit scope dependency.  
  
====actions====  
  
A plain JavaScript object mapping action name strings to zero-argument functions. Currently contains one entry:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Key  
! Behaviour  
|-  
| {{code|OPEN_STEAMWORKSHOP}}  
| Calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.activateSteamOverlayToWebPage("https://steamcommunity.com/app/362890/workshop")</nowiki></code>.  
|}  
  
When {{code|setRoute()}} encounters a {{code|Route}} with a non-empty {{code|action}} property, it looks up the action by name in this object and calls it instead of performing a stack transition.  
  
====Private signal handlers====  
  
Four private functions (prefixed {{code|_}}) are connected to the corresponding signals on each newly pushed {{code|RouteView}}. They relay the signal up the component tree:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Function  
! Behaviour  
|-  
| {{code|_routeRequestHandler(routeName)}}  
| Calls {{code|outlet.setRoute(routeName)}}.  
|-  
| {{code|_loadGameRequestHandler(savegame)}}  
| Re-emits {{code|outlet.loadGameRequest(savegame)}}.  
|-  
| {{code|_loadMapRequestHandler(filename, mapname)}}  
| Re-emits {{code|outlet.loadMapRequest(filename, mapname)}}.  
|-  
| {{code|_saveGameRequestHandler(filename)}}  
| Re-emits {{code|outlet.saveGameRequest(filename)}}.  
|}  
  
====_getRouteTransitionConfig====  
  
Determines the {{code|StackView}} operation ({{code|push}}, {{code|pop}}, or {{code|replace}}), the animation transition pair, and the construction-time properties to pass to the new {{code|RouteView}}. Returns a plain JavaScript config object.  
  
=====Transition selection logic=====  
  
Evaluated in order; the first matching branch wins:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Condition  
! Stack operation  
! Transition  
|-  
| No {{code|currentRoute}} (initial navigation)  
| {{code|push}}  
| {{code|down}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.back.name</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|pop}}  
| {{code|down}} if current route is an alert; {{code|up}} otherwise  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>route.alert === true</nowiki></code> or <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|push}}  
| {{code|up}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>currentRoute.name === Routes.index.name</nowiki></code> or <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|push}}  
| {{code|down}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>currentRoute.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|push}}  
| {{code|down}}  
|-  
| All other cases (rank-based lateral navigation)  
| {{code|replace}}  
| {{code|right}} if <code><nowiki>currentRoute.rank > route.rank</nowiki></code>; {{code|left}} otherwise  
|}  
  
The {{code|operation}} field is set to {{code|StackView.Immediate}} when {{code|skipTransition}} is {{code|true}} (used during level loading), and {{code|StackView.Transition}} otherwise.  
  
The {{code|properties}} field always contains <code><nowiki>{ routeName: route.name, isInGame: outlet.isInGame }</nowiki></code>, which are passed as construction-time properties to the new {{code|RouteView}}.  
  
====setRoute====  
  
The main public entry point for all navigation. Signature: <code><nowiki>setRoute(newRouteName, skipTransition, allowSameRoute)</nowiki></code>.  
  
Execution order:  
  
# Look up <code><nowiki>route = Routes[newRouteName]</nowiki></code>.  
# If {{code|route.passthrough}} is {{code|true}}, replace {{code|route}} with <code><nowiki>Routes[route.destination]</nowiki></code>.  
# If {{code|!allowSameRoute}} and the requested route is already the current route, return early (no-op).  
# If {{code|outlet.currentItem}} has an {{code|onRouteWillChange}} function, call it with {{code|newRouteName}}. If it returns {{code|true}}, the navigation is intercepted and {{code|setRoute}} returns. (Used by {{code|routeview-video.qml}} to show the unsaved-changes dialog.)  
# If {{code|route.action}} is non-empty, call <code><nowiki>actions[route.action]()</nowiki></code> and return. No stack transition occurs.  
# Call {{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}} to determine the config.  
# Execute the stack operation:  
#* '''pop''': Sets {{code|popEnter}}/{{code|popExit}}, calls {{code|outlet.pop(operation)}}, then sets {{code|currentRoute}} from {{code|outlet.currentItem.routeName}}.  
#* '''push''' or '''replace''': Sets the corresponding {{code|pushEnter}}/{{code|pushExit}} or {{code|replaceEnter}}/{{code|replaceExit}} properties, calls <code><nowiki>outlet[fn](route.qmlFile, properties, operation)</nowiki></code>, then sets {{code|currentRoute = route}}.  
# Connects {{code|routeRequest}}, {{code|loadGameRequest}}, {{code|loadMapRequest}}, and {{code|saveGameRequest}} on {{code|outlet.currentItem}} to the four private handler functions.  
  
====parseRouteUIItems====  
  
Called from a {{code|ScriptAction}} at the end of every enter transition. Calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements(appRoot)}} followed by {{code|BlackMesaUtils.afterTraverseCallback()}} to rebuild the keyboard/controller navigation state for the newly visible view. {{code|appRoot}} is resolved from the parent scope in {{code|main.qml}} (implicit scope dependency).  
  
====routeTransitions====  
  
A readonly {{code|QtObject}} containing four named transition pairs. Each pair has an {{code|enter}} and an {{code|exit}} {{code|Transition}} reference:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Name  
! Enter: slides in from  
! Exit: slides out to  
! Position axis  
! Duration  
|-  
| {{code|down}}  
| Top (y: −300 → 0)  
| Bottom (y: 0 → 300)  
| Y  
| 600 ms (position), 300 ms (opacity, delayed 300 ms)  
|-  
| {{code|up}}  
| Bottom (y: 300 → 0)  
| Top (y: 0 → −300)  
| Y  
| 600 ms (position), 300 ms (opacity, delayed 300 ms)  
|-  
| {{code|left}}  
| Right (x: 300 → 0)  
| Left (x: 0 → −300)  
| X  
| 600 ms (position), 300 ms (opacity, delayed 300 ms)  
|-  
| {{code|right}}  
| Left (x: −300 → 0)  
| Right (x: 0 → 300)  
| X  
| 600 ms (position), 300 ms (opacity, delayed 300 ms)  
|}  
  
All eight {{code|Transition}} objects are declared as direct children of the {{code|StackView}} (not inside {{code|routeTransitions}}); {{code|routeTransitions}} holds references to them by id.  
  
=====Enter transition structure=====  
  
Every enter transition uses a {{code|ParallelAnimation}} containing:  
# A {{code|NumberAnimation}} on the position property (x or y), running for the full 600 ms with {{code|Easing.InOutQuint}}.  
# A {{code|SequentialAnimation}} containing:  
## A {{code|ScriptAction}} calling {{code|BlackMesaUtils.clearUIElementsData()}} — resets keyboard/controller navigation state immediately.  
## A {{code|PauseAnimation}} of 300 ms — waits until the position animation is halfway complete before fading in.  
## A {{code|NumberAnimation}} on {{code|opacity}} from 0.0 to 1.0 over 300 ms — fades the new view in.  
## A {{code|ScriptAction}} calling {{code|parseRouteUIItems()}} — traverses the now-visible view to register its interactive elements.  
  
=====Exit transition structure=====  
  
Every exit transition uses a {{code|ParallelAnimation}} containing:  
# A {{code|NumberAnimation}} on the position property, 600 ms, {{code|Easing.InOutQuint}}.  
# A {{code|NumberAnimation}} on {{code|opacity}} from 1.0 to 0.0 over 300 ms (no delay — the exiting view fades out immediately).  
  
====Callers of setRoute====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Caller  
! Condition  
! Route requested  
|-  
| {{code|main.qml}} {{code|Component.onCompleted}}  
| Initialization  
| {{code|emptyRoute}} ({{code|Routes.empty}})  
|-  
| {{code|main.qml}} {{code|onGameUIActived}}  
| Loading error  
| {{code|Routes.multiplayer}}  
|-  
| {{code|main.qml}} {{code|onGameUIActived}}  
| Initial load or UI was hidden  
| {{code|mainMenuIndexRoute}} ({{code|Routes.index}})  
|-  
| {{code|main.qml}} {{code|onGameUIHidden}}  
| UI dismissed  
| {{code|emptyRoute}}, {{code|skipTransition = didLoadLevel}}  
|-  
| {{code|main.qml}} {{code|onLevelLoadingStarted}}  
| Level load begins  
| {{code|emptyRoute}}, {{code|skipTransition = true}}  
|-  
| {{code|main.qml}} {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}}  
| Level finished loading  
| {{code|mainMenuIndexRoute}}  
|-  
| {{code|main.qml}} {{code|autoBackgroundMusicTimer.onTriggered}}  
| Fallback timer (1250 ms after startup)  
| {{code|mainMenuIndexRoute}}  
|-  
| {{code|component-header.qml}}  
| Tab click  
| Various, via {{code|onRouteRequest: router.setRoute(routeName)}}  
|-  
| {{code|component-footer.qml}}  
| Button click  
| Various, via {{code|onRouteRequest: router.setRoute(routeName)}}  
|-  
| Any {{code|RouteView}} child  
| Internal navigation  
| Via {{code|routeRequest}} signal → {{code|_routeRequestHandler}}  
|}  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''Implicit scope dependencies.''' {{code|emptyRoute}} and {{code|appRoot}} are referenced inside {{code|router.qml}} but defined in {{code|main.qml}}. This works because QML resolves identifiers through the visual parent scope, but it creates a hidden coupling: {{code|router.qml}} cannot be used outside of {{code|main.qml}} without breaking.  
  
2. '''Dead branch: <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code> on line 65.''' The condition on line 62 already catches all navigations to the empty route ({{code|route.name === Routes.empty.name}}). The same sub-condition on line 65 is therefore unreachable. The intended {{code|down}} transition for navigating to the empty route via that branch is never applied through that path (though it is applied via line 62's {{code|up}} transition, which takes precedence).  
  
3. '''{{code|isInGame}} is not reactively bound in the pushed {{code|RouteView}}.''' The value is captured at push time via {{code|properties: { isInGame: outlet.isInGame }}}. If {{code|appRoot.isInGame}} changes after the view is pushed (e.g. during a multiplayer disconnect), the active {{code|RouteView}}'s {{code|isInGame}} property will not update until the next navigation.  
  
4. '''Signal connections are never explicitly disconnected.''' After a {{code|pop}} or {{code|replace}}, the destroyed {{code|RouteView}}'s signals are cleaned up automatically by the QML engine. This is safe in practice but means there is no explicit lifecycle management for signal connections.  
  
5. '''{{code|allowSameRoute}} is never used by any caller.''' The parameter exists to allow reloading the same route, but no call site in the codebase passes it as {{code|true}}.  
  
6. '''Empty stack after {{code|pop}} is not guarded.''' After {{code|outlet.pop()}}, {{code|currentRoute}} is set from {{code|outlet.currentItem.routeName}}. If the stack is empty after the pop (which should not happen in normal flow but is not structurally prevented), {{code|outlet.currentItem}} will be {{code|null}} and the property access will throw a TypeError.  
  
7. '''{{code|actions}} is a plain JavaScript object.''' It cannot be extended or overridden from outside {{code|router.qml}} without modifying the file. Adding new action-type routes requires editing the {{code|actions}} literal directly.

===routes.qml===  
  
{{code|routes.qml}} is registered in {{code|qmldir}} as the {{code|Routes}} singleton. It is the central route registry for the entire UI — a flat list of {{code|Route}} objects, each describing one navigable destination. {{code|router.qml}} consumes this singleton exclusively via {{code|Routes[routeName]}} lookups.  
  
The file is 182 lines and contains 22 route definitions organized into six logical groups.  
  
====Route component====  
  
Each entry is an instance of {{code|Route}} (defined in {{code|route.qml}}, registered in {{code|qmldir}} as {{code|Route 1.0}}). The {{code|Route}} type is an {{code|Item}} with the following properties:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Default  
! Purpose  
|-  
| {{code|name}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| (empty)  
| Unique identifier used as the lookup key in {{code|Routes[name]}} and stored in {{code|RouteView.routeName}}.  
|-  
| {{code|path}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| (empty)  
| Dot-separated logical path (e.g. {{code|"campaign.newgame"}}). Used by {{code|component-header.qml}} to determine which main-nav and sub-nav tabs to highlight.  
|-  
| {{code|qmlFile}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| (empty)  
| Filename of the {{code|RouteView}} QML file to load. Passed directly to {{code|StackView.push()}} or {{code|StackView.replace()}}.  
|-  
| {{code|alert}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| If {{code|true}}, the route is always pushed onto the stack (never replaced) and can only be dismissed by a pop. Alert routes use the {{code|upEnterTransition}} animation.  
|-  
| {{code|action}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| (empty)  
| If set, {{code|router.qml}} executes {{code|actions[route.action]()}} instead of loading a view. No {{code|qmlFile}} is needed.  
|-  
| {{code|passthrough}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| If {{code|true}}, the route is immediately resolved to {{code|Routes[route.destination]}} before any transition logic runs.  
|-  
| {{code|destination}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| (empty)  
| The {{code|name}} of the route to redirect to when {{code|passthrough}} is {{code|true}}.  
|-  
| {{code|rank}}  
| {{code|real}}  
| {{code|0.0}}  
| Numeric position in the navigation hierarchy. When transitioning between two non-special routes, the router compares ranks to choose a left or right slide animation: higher rank slides in from the right, lower rank slides in from the left.  
|}  
  
====Route types====  
  
There are five distinct route types, distinguished by which properties are set:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Type  
! Defining property  
! Router behaviour  
! Examples  
|-  
| Normal  
| {{code|qmlFile}} set, {{code|alert}} false  
| {{code|StackView.replace()}} with rank-based left/right transition  
| {{code|newgame}}, {{code|video}}, {{code|achievements}}  
|-  
| Alert  
| <code><nowiki>alert: true</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|StackView.push()}} with {{code|upEnterTransition}}; popped (not replaced) on dismiss  
| {{code|quit}}, {{code|savebeforequit}}  
|-  
| Passthrough  
| <code><nowiki>passthrough: true</nowiki></code>  
| Immediately resolved to {{code|Routes[destination]}} before any transition  
| {{code|campaign}}, {{code|multiplayer}}, {{code|options}}, {{code|steamworkshop}}  
|-  
| Action  
| {{code|action}} set  
| Executes {{code|router.actions[route.action]()}}; no view is loaded  
| {{code|browse}}  
|-  
| Special  
| Neither {{code|qmlFile}} nor {{code|action}} nor {{code|passthrough}}  
| Handled by dedicated logic in {{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}}  
| {{code|back}}, {{code|empty}}, {{code|index}}  
|}  
  
====Special routes====  
  
=====back=====  
  
A sentinel value with only {{code|name: "back"}} set. When {{code|router.setRoute("back")}} is called, {{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}} detects <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.back.name</nowiki></code> and sets {{code|config.fn = 'pop'}}. The transition direction depends on whether the current route is an alert: alert routes pop with {{code|downEnterTransition}}, normal routes pop with {{code|upEnterTransition}}. {{code|back}} has no {{code|path}}, no {{code|qmlFile}}, and no {{code|rank}}.  
  
=====empty=====  
  
Loads {{code|routeview-empty.qml}}, which is a blank {{code|RouteView {}}} with no content. Used as the initial state of the router (set in {{code|router.qml Component.onCompleted}}) and as the state during level loading and when the game UI is hidden. Has {{code|path: "empty"}} but no {{code|rank}} (defaults to {{code|0.0}}); the router handles it with special-case logic rather than rank comparison.  
  
=====index=====  
  
Loads {{code|routeview-index.qml}}, the main menu hub. Has {{code|path: "index"}} but no {{code|rank}} (defaults to {{code|0.0}}). The router special-cases it: any transition away from {{code|index}} uses {{code|downEnterTransition}} regardless of the destination's rank.

====How routes are consumed====  
  
{{code|router.qml}} is the sole consumer of {{code|Routes}}. Its {{code|setRoute(newRouteName)}} function performs the following steps in order:  
  
# Look up the route: <code><nowiki>var route = Routes[newRouteName]</nowiki></code>.  
# Resolve passthrough: if <code><nowiki>route.passthrough</nowiki></code>, replace with <code><nowiki>Routes[route.destination]</nowiki></code>.  
# Guard same-route: return early if the resolved route name equals {{code|currentRoute.name}} (unless {{code|allowSameRoute}} is set).  
# Guard navigation intercept: call <code><nowiki>outlet.currentItem.onRouteWillChange(newRouteName)</nowiki></code> if it exists; return early if it returns {{code|true}} (used by {{code|routeview-video.qml}} to block navigation with unsaved changes).  
# Execute action: if <code><nowiki>route.action</nowiki></code> is set, call <code><nowiki>actions[route.action]()</nowiki></code> and return. The only registered action is {{code|OPEN_STEAMWORKSHOP}}, which calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.activateSteamOverlayToWebPage(...)</nowiki></code>.  
# Determine transition: call {{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}} to select the animation direction and stack operation ({{code|push}}, {{code|replace}}, or {{code|pop}}).  
# Execute transition: call {{code|outlet.push()}}, {{code|outlet.replace()}}, or {{code|outlet.pop()}} with the resolved {{code|qmlFile}} and properties.  
# Connect signals: connect the new {{code|RouteView}}'s {{code|routeRequest}}, {{code|loadGameRequest}}, {{code|loadMapRequest}}, and {{code|saveGameRequest}} signals to the router's handlers.  
  
====Transition direction logic====  
  
{{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}} selects one of four named transitions based on the current and target route:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Condition (evaluated in order)  
! Stack operation  
! Transition  
|-  
| No current route (initial load)  
| {{code|push}}  
| {{code|down}} (default)  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.back.name</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|pop}}  
| {{code|down}} if current route is alert; {{code|up}} otherwise  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>route.alert || route.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|push}}  
| {{code|up}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>currentRoute.name === Routes.index.name</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|push}}  
| {{code|down}}  
|-  
| (all other cases)  
| {{code|replace}}  
| {{code|right}} if <code><nowiki>currentRoute.rank > route.rank</nowiki></code>; {{code|left}} otherwise  
|}  
  
Each named transition is a {{code|ParallelAnimation}} combining a positional slide ({{code|x}} or {{code|y}}, ±300px, 600ms, {{code|Easing.InOutQuint}}) with an opacity fade (0→1 or 1→0, 300ms). Enter transitions also fire two {{code|ScriptAction}} steps: {{code|BlackMesaUtils.clearUIElementsData()}} at the start and {{code|parseRouteUIItems()}} (which calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements()}} and {{code|BlackMesaUtils.afterTraverseCallback()}}) after the 300ms pause, so the navigation system is rebuilt for the new view once it is visible.  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''{{code|rank}} on passthrough routes is never used.''' Passthrough routes are resolved to their destination before {{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}} is called. The {{code|rank}} values on {{code|campaign}} (1.0), {{code|multiplayer}} (2.0), {{code|options}} (3.0), and {{code|steamworkshop}} (5.0) are therefore dead data.  
  
2. '''Dead branch for <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code>.''' The condition appears twice in {{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}}: once on line 62 (combined with the alert check, always matched first) and once on line 65 (unreachable). The intended {{code|down}} transition for navigating to the empty route via the second branch is never applied.  
  
3. '''{{code|Routes}} is an {{code|Item}}, not a {{code|QtObject}}.''' The root element is {{code|Item}} rather than {{code|QtObject}}. Since {{code|Route}} extends {{code|Item}} (it uses {{code|anchors.fill: parent}}), each of the 22 {{code|Route}} children is a full visual item parented to the {{code|Routes}} singleton, which is itself parented to the QML engine's root. This is unnecessary overhead; {{code|QtObject}} children would suffice for a pure data registry.  
  
4. '''{{code|browse}} has no {{code|path}}.''' The {{code|path}} property is used by {{code|component-header.qml}} to determine which tab to highlight. Since {{code|browse}} has no {{code|path}}, navigating to it (which opens the Steam overlay without changing the active view) leaves the header in whatever state it was in before, which is the correct visual behaviour but is implicit rather than explicit.  
  
5. '''No route for {{code|routeview-campaigns.qml}}.''' The file exists in the UI directory but has no corresponding entry in {{code|routes.qml}} and is unreachable through any normal navigation flow.

===routeview.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview.qml}} is the '''base class''' for every page in the Black Mesa menu system. It is registered in {{code|qmldir}} as the {{code|RouteView}} component (version 1.0) and is used as the root element of all 19 {{code|routeview-*.qml}} files.  
  
The file is 28 lines. It defines no visual content of its own — it is a pure structural contract: a {{code|FocusScope}} that fills its parent, declares four signals, and exposes two properties that the router populates on every push.  
  
====Base Type====  
  
{{code|RouteView}} extends {{code|FocusScope}} (from {{code|QtQuick 2.9}}), not {{code|Item}}. This means:  
  
* Keyboard focus is scoped to the view. When a {{code|RouteView}} has active focus, {{code|Keys}} handlers inside it receive events before the parent.  
* {{code|activeFocus}} propagates inward to the first focusable child, which is the mechanism that allows {{code|main.qml}}'s {{code|Keys.onPressed}} to delegate to {{code|BlackMesaUtils.navigationUIHandle(event)}}.  
  
The root element fills its parent on all four sides and also explicitly sets {{code|width}} and {{code|height}} to {{code|parent.width}} and {{code|parent.height}}, making the fill redundant but harmless.  
  
====Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Default  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|routeName}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| (empty)  
| Set by {{code|router.qml}} immediately before pushing the view onto the {{code|StackView}}. Stores the name of the route this view was loaded for. Used by the router after a {{code|pop}} to recover the name of the newly-current route: <code><nowiki>currentRoute = Routes[outlet.currentItem.routeName]</nowiki></code>.  
|-  
| {{code|isInGame}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| (undefined)  
| Set by {{code|router.qml}} on every push. Mirrors {{code|outlet.isInGame}} at the moment of navigation. Child views use this to conditionally show in-game controls (e.g. the Save Game and Disconnect buttons in {{code|routeview-index.qml}}).  
|-  
| {{code|opacity}}  
| {{code|real}}  
| {{code|0}}  
| All views start fully transparent. Every enter transition in {{code|router.qml}} animates {{code|opacity}} from {{code|0}} to {{code|1}} after a 300 ms pause. Exit transitions animate it back to {{code|0}} over 300 ms. This ensures content is never visible mid-slide.  
|}  
  
====Signals====  
  
All four signals are connected by {{code|router.qml}} immediately after each {{code|push}} or {{code|replace}} call. The connections are not disconnected on {{code|pop}}, relying on QML's automatic signal disconnection when the emitting object is destroyed.  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Signal  
! Arguments  
! Handler in {{code|router.qml}}  
! Final action  
|-  
| {{code|routeRequest}}  
| <code><nowiki>string routeName</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|_routeRequestHandler}}  
| Calls <code><nowiki>outlet.setRoute(routeName)</nowiki></code>. This is the primary navigation mechanism used by every button and alert in the UI.  
|-  
| {{code|loadGameRequest}}  
| <code><nowiki>variant savegame</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|_loadGameRequestHandler}}  
| Re-emits {{code|outlet.loadGameRequest(savegame)}}. Propagates up to {{code|main.qml}}, which calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.loadGame(savegame.savename)</nowiki></code>.  
|-  
| {{code|loadMapRequest}}  
| <code><nowiki>string filename, string mapname</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|_loadMapRequestHandler}}  
| Re-emits {{code|outlet.loadMapRequest(filename, mapname)}}. Propagates up to {{code|main.qml}}, which calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.loadMap(filename, mapname)</nowiki></code>.  
|-  
| {{code|saveGameRequest}}  
| <code><nowiki>string filename</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|_saveGameRequestHandler}}  
| Re-emits {{code|outlet.saveGameRequest(filename)}}. Propagates up to {{code|main.qml}}, which calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.saveGame(filename, callback)</nowiki></code>. Passing {{code|null}} auto-generates a filename in the format <code><nowiki>bms-NNN</nowiki></code>.  
|}  
  
====onRouteWillChange Hook====  
  
{{code|routeview.qml}} does '''not''' declare an {{code|onRouteWillChange}} function. However, {{code|router.qml}} checks for it dynamically before every navigation:  
  
<syntaxhighlight lang="qml">  
if (outlet.currentItem && typeof outlet.currentItem.onRouteWillChange === 'function') {  
    if (outlet.currentItem.onRouteWillChange(newRouteName)) {  
        return; // navigation intercepted  
    }  
}  
</syntaxhighlight>  
  
If the function exists on the current view and returns a truthy value, the navigation is cancelled. Only {{code|routeview-video.qml}} implements this hook, using it to show an unsaved-changes confirmation dialog when the user tries to navigate away with pending video settings.  
  
====Usage====  
  
Every {{code|routeview-*.qml}} file uses {{code|RouteView}} as its root element. The component is loaded dynamically by {{code|router.qml}} via <code><nowiki>outlet[config.fn](route.qmlFile, config.properties, config.operation)</nowiki></code>, where {{code|route.qmlFile}} is the filename string defined in {{code|routes.qml}} for each route.  
  
The full list of files that extend {{code|RouteView}} is:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! File  
! Route name  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-achievements.qml}} || {{code|achievements}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-addons.qml}} || {{code|addons}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-audio.qml}} || {{code|audio}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-bindings.qml}} || {{code|bindings}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-campaigns.qml}} || (unregistered stub)  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-empty.qml}} || {{code|empty}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-game.qml}} || {{code|game}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-host.qml}} || {{code|host}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-index.qml}} || {{code|index}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-input.qml}} || {{code|input}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-join.qml}} || {{code|join}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-loadgame.qml}} || {{code|loadgame}}

|-  
| {{code|routeview-savebeforequit.qml}} || {{code|savebeforequit}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-savegame.qml}} || {{code|savegame}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-translators.qml}} || {{code|translators}}  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-unavailable.qml}} || (unregistered fallback)  
|-  
| {{code|routeview-video.qml}} || {{code|video}}  
|}  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''Signal connections are never explicitly disconnected.''' After a {{code|pop}}, the destroyed {{code|RouteView}} instance's signals are disconnected automatically by the QML engine. However, after a {{code|replace}}, the replaced item is also destroyed, so the same automatic cleanup applies. The comment in {{code|router.qml}} acknowledges this implicitly. In practice this is safe, but it means there is no explicit lifecycle management for signal connections.  
  
2. '''{{code|opacity: 0}} is set on the base class, not in the transition.''' If a {{code|RouteView}} is ever instantiated outside of {{code|router.qml}}'s transition system (e.g. directly in a {{code|Loader}}), it will be permanently invisible unless {{code|opacity}} is explicitly overridden, since no transition will run to animate it to {{code|1}}.  
  
3. '''{{code|routeName}} is a writable string, not a reference to a {{code|Route}} object.''' The router stores only the name string, not the full {{code|Route}} object. After a {{code|pop}}, the router must look up the route by name via <code><nowiki>Routes[outlet.currentItem.routeName]</nowiki></code>. If {{code|routeName}} is ever set to a value that does not correspond to a key in {{code|Routes}}, {{code|currentRoute}} will be set to {{code|undefined}}, breaking all subsequent transition logic.

===routeview-achievements.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-achievements.qml}} is the '''Achievements''' page. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|achievements}} route at rank 4.0, with {{code|path: "achievements"}} (no dot separator). Because the path has no dot, {{code|component-header.qml}} renders it in {{code|subnav-closed}} state — the sub-navigation bar is hidden and the page has no sub-tabs. The route is accessed directly from the main navigation bar via the {{code|achievements}} entry in {{code|mainNavModel}}.  
  
The file is 265 lines. It imports {{code|QtQuick.Controls 2.2}} in addition to the standard {{code|QtQuick 2.9}} and {{code|CrowbarCollective 1.0}}, making it one of the few route views to use Qt Quick Controls directly. The root {{code|RouteView}} has no {{code|id}}.  
  
====Top-level Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Value / Purpose  
|-  
| {{code|total}}  
| {{code|int}}  
| Total number of achievements (including hidden ones). Set in {{code|Component.onCompleted}}.  
|-  
| {{code|completed}}  
| {{code|int}}  
| Number of achievements where <code><nowiki>count === total</nowiki></code> (i.e. fully unlocked). Set in {{code|Component.onCompleted}}.  
|-  
| {{code|pageWidth}}  
| {{code|int}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(1500 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| {{code|pageHeight}}  
| {{code|int}}  
| <code><nowiki>cellH * Math.floor(Math.ceil(750 * Theme.heightScale) / cellH)</nowiki></code> — snapped downward to the nearest multiple of {{code|cellH}} to avoid partial rows.  
|-  
| {{code|headerHeight}}  
| {{code|int}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(30 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code> — uses {{code|widthScale}}, not {{code|heightScale}}.  
|-  
| {{code|cellW}}  
| {{code|int}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(750 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| {{code|cellH}}  
| {{code|int}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(150 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code> — uses {{code|widthScale}}, not {{code|heightScale}}.  
|}  
  
====Layout====  
  
The entire page is a single {{code|Item}} centered in the parent via {{code|anchors.centerIn: parent}}, sized {{code|pageWidth}} × {{code|pageHeight}}. It contains two children: a {{code|header}} strip anchored above the grid, and a {{code|GridView}} (id: {{code|grid}}) that fills the item.  
  
=====header strip=====  
  
A fixed-height {{code|Item}} (id: {{code|header}}) anchored to the bottom of the grid with a 10px scaled margin. It contains three elements laid out horizontally:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Element  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|headerText}} ({{code|Text}})  
| Displays <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString("#BlackMesaUI_Achievements_OverallProgress")</nowiki></code>. Bold, uppercase, 24px scaled, {{code|Theme.colors.headerText}}.  
|-  
| Progress bar ({{code|Rectangle}})  
| Fills the horizontal space between {{code|headerText}} and {{code|headerFraction}}. Transparent fill with a 2px {{code|Theme.colors.dimmedSlider}} border. Contains an inner {{code|Rectangle}} of color {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}} whose width is <code><nowiki>parent.width * (completed / total)</nowiki></code>.  
|-  
| {{code|headerFraction}} ({{code|Text}})  
| Right-aligned. Displays {{code|completed}} in {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}} and {{code|" / " + total}} in {{code|Theme.colors.headerText}} using inline HTML {{code|<font color="...">}} tags joined into a single string.  
|}  
  
=====GridView (grid)=====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|cellWidth}} / {{code|cellHeight}}  
| {{code|cellW}} / {{code|cellH}} (750 × 150 nominal, width-scaled)  
|-  
| Columns  
| 2 (implicit: {{code|pageWidth}} / {{code|cellW}} = 1500 / 750)  
|-  
| {{code|interactive}}  
| {{code|true}} — touch/mouse drag scrolling enabled  
|-  
| {{code|boundsBehavior}}  
| {{code|Flickable.StopAtBounds}}  
|-  
| {{code|ScrollBar.vertical}}  
| {{code|VerticalScrollBar}} with {{code|visible: true}} (always shown)  
|-  
| {{code|anchors.rightMargin}}  
| <code><nowiki>-Math.ceil(20 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code> — negative margin extends the grid beyond the parent's right edge to push the scrollbar outside the centered content area  
|}  
  
There are no {{code|Keys}} handlers and no {{code|objectName}} on any element. Keyboard and controller navigation are not supported on this page.  
  
====Delegate====  
  
Each grid cell is a {{code|MouseArea}} sized {{code|cellW}} × {{code|cellH}}. It has no {{code|onClicked}}, {{code|onEntered}}, or {{code|onExited}} handlers — it is purely a visual container with no interaction logic.  
  
=====States=====  
  
The delegate has three states. The initial state is set inline: <code><nowiki>state: count === total ? 'focused' : 'locked'</nowiki></code>.  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! State  
! {{code|background}} border / color  
! {{code|image}} border  
! {{code|achievementName}} color  
! {{code|achievementDescription}} color  
! {{code|progressText}} color  
|-  
| {{code|locked}}  
| 0px / {{code|#A6000000}}  
| 0px  
| {{code|Theme.colors.dimmedText}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.dimmedText}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.dimmedText}}  
|-  
| {{code|blurred}}  
| 0px / {{code|#F2000000}}  
| 1px  
| {{code|Theme.colors.headerText}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.headerText}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.headerText}}  
|-  
| {{code|focused}}  
| 2px / {{code|#F2000000}}  
| 1px  
| {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.headerText}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}}  
|}  
  
=====Card Layout=====  
  
Inside the {{code|MouseArea}}, a {{code|Rectangle}} (id: {{code|background}}, 730 × 130px scaled) is centered. It is divided into three horizontal zones:  
  
'''Left zone — thumbnail''' ({{code|Rectangle}} id: {{code|image}}, 120 × 120px scaled, black background):  
* Contains an {{code|Image}} (id: {{code|achievementThumbnailImage}}) sourced from {{code|thumbnailPath}}, stretched to fill.  
  
'''Center zone — text''' ({{code|Rectangle}}, 450 × 120px scaled, transparent):  
* {{code|progressBar}} ({{code|Rectangle}}) — visible when <code><nowiki>count !== total</nowiki></code>. Width = <code><nowiki>parent.width * (count / total)</nowiki></code>, color {{code|#A6000000}}. Acts as a semi-transparent overlay proportional to progress, anchored top-left and growing rightward, covering the name and description text.  
* {{code|achievementName}} ({{code|Text}}) — {{code|name}}, bold, uppercase, 26px scaled.  
* {{code|achievementDescription}} ({{code|Text}}) — {{code|description}}, regular, word-wrap, 16px scaled.  
  
'''Right zone — progress counter''' ({{code|Item}}, 140 × 120px scaled):  
* A dim overlay ({{code|Rectangle}}, {{code|#A6000000}}) visible when <code><nowiki>total > 1 && count !== total</nowiki></code>.  
* {{code|progressText}} ({{code|Text}}) — <code><nowiki>count + " / " + total</nowiki></code>, visible when {{code|total > 1}}, bold, 28px scaled, centered.  
  
====achievementsModel (ListModel)====  
  
The {{code|ListModel}} (id: {{code|achievementsModel}}) is populated entirely in {{code|Component.onCompleted}}:  
  
# Calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.listAchievementEntries()</nowiki></code> and maps each entry to a plain object: <code><nowiki>{ thumbnailPath, name, description, hidden, count, total }</nowiki></code>. {{code|thumbnailPath}} is set to {{code|entry.imageSourceLocked}} when not achieved, or {{code|entry.imageSourceUnlocked}} when achieved. {{code|name}} and {{code|description}} are resolved immediately via {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString()}}. {{code|hidden}} is {{code|true}} when <code><nowiki>!entry.isAchieved && entry.shouldHideUntilAchieved</nowiki></code>.  
# Sets {{code|total}} to {{code|achievements.length}} (count of '''all''' achievements, including hidden ones).  
# Iterates the mapped array: increments {{code|hiddenCount}} for each hidden-and-incomplete achievement; increments {{code|completed}} for each achievement where <code><nowiki>count === total</nowiki></code>.  
# Appends each achievement to the model '''only if''' it is not hidden, or if it is hidden but has been completed.  
# If {{code|hiddenCount > 0}}, appends a single synthetic "hidden" card:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Field  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|image}}  
| {{code|""}} (empty string — note: the delegate reads {{code|thumbnailPath}}, not {{code|image}})  
|-  
| {{code|name}}  
| <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString("#BlackMesaUI_Achievements_Hidden").arg(hiddenCount)</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| {{code|description}}  
| <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString("#BlackMesaUI_Achievements_HiddenDescription").arg(hiddenCount)</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| {{code|hidden}}  
| {{code|false}}  
|-  
| {{code|count}}  
| {{code|0}}  
|-  
| {{code|total}}  
| {{code|1}}  
|}  
  
The synthetic card has <code><nowiki>count: 0, total: 1</nowiki></code>, so its delegate state is {{code|locked}} ({{code|0 != 1}}) and its {{code|progressText}} is hidden ({{code|total > 1}} is false).  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''{{code|blurred}} state is unreachable.''' The delegate's {{code|MouseArea}} has no {{code|onEntered}}, {{code|onExited}}, or {{code|onClicked}} handler that transitions to {{code|blurred}}. The initial state is set directly to either {{code|focused}} or {{code|locked}} and never changes. The {{code|blurred}} state (white text, no background border) is defined but dead code.  
  
2. '''No keyboard or controller navigation.''' No element has an {{code|objectName}} matching the patterns used by {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements()}} ({{code|ui:tab}}, {{code|ui:btn:*}}, {{code|ui:option-item}}). The page cannot be navigated with a keyboard or controller beyond scrolling via the {{code|GridView}}'s built-in flick behavior, and even that requires mouse/touch since there are no {{code|Keys}} handlers.  
  
3. '''Synthetic hidden card uses wrong field name.''' The synthetic card sets {{code|image: ""}} but the delegate reads {{code|thumbnailPath}}. The {{code|thumbnailPath}} role is absent from the synthetic card's object, so {{code|achievementThumbnailImage.source}} will be {{code|undefined}}, causing a silent image load failure.  

4. '''{{code|pageHeight}} mixes {{code|widthScale}} and {{code|heightScale}}.''' The formula is <code><nowiki>cellH * Math.floor(Math.ceil(750 * Theme.heightScale) / cellH)</nowiki></code>. {{code|cellH}} uses {{code|Theme.widthScale}} while the dividend uses {{code|Theme.heightScale}}. At non-square aspect ratios the snapping calculation produces an inconsistent result.  
  
5. '''{{code|headerHeight}}, {{code|cellW}}, and {{code|cellH}} all use {{code|Theme.widthScale}}.''' None of these use {{code|Theme.heightScale}}, so the header strip and all cells scale uniformly with screen width. At non-standard aspect ratios, cells will be taller or shorter than intended relative to the screen height.  
  
6. '''{{code|total}} counts hidden achievements; the grid does not.''' The {{code|total}} property (used in the header progress bar denominator and in the {{code|headerFraction}} text) is set to {{code|achievements.length}}, which includes hidden-and-incomplete achievements. However, those achievements are not appended to the model — they are replaced by a single synthetic card. The header therefore correctly shows the true total, but the number of visible cards is less than {{code|total}}, which may confuse players who count the cards.  
  
7. '''{{code|achievementDescription}} uses explicit {{code|width}} instead of {{code|anchors.right}}.''' The width is set to <code><nowiki>parent.width - anchors.leftMargin * 2</nowiki></code>, which subtracts the left margin twice. Since there is no right margin applied, the text can overflow the right edge of the center zone by {{code|anchors.leftMargin}} pixels.  
  
8. '''{{code|VerticalScrollBar}} is always visible.''' {{code|visible: true}} is hardcoded. The scrollbar is shown even when all achievements fit within {{code|pageHeight}}, which is uncommon but possible for games with very few achievements.  
  
9. '''All strings bypass {{code|L10n}}.''' Every localized string in the file uses {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString()}} directly with {{code|#BlackMesaUI_*}} keys, inconsistent with all other route views which use the {{code|L10n}} singleton. The only {{code|L10n}} reference to achievements is {{code|L10n.strings.menus.achievements}}, used in {{code|component-header.qml}} for the main nav tab label.  
  
10. '''No {{code|id}} on the root {{code|RouteView}}.''' Child elements cannot reference the root by id. The {{code|total}} and {{code|completed}} properties on the root are accessed from the {{code|ListModel}}'s {{code|Component.onCompleted}} via implicit scope, which works but is fragile.

===routeview-addons.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-addons.qml}} is the '''Steam Workshop installed addons browser'''. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|addons}} route at path {{code|steamworkshop.addons}}, rank 5.1. Navigating to the parent {{code|steamworkshop}} route (rank 5.0) automatically redirects here because {{code|steamworkshop}} is a {{code|passthrough: true}} route with {{code|destination: "addons"}}.  
  
The file is 100 lines and is structurally near-identical to {{code|routeview-newgame.qml}}, differing only in the campaign filter predicate and the addition of an empty-state message.  
  
====Route Context====  
  
The Workshop section of the main nav ({{code|#QTUI_Menu_Workshop}}) exposes two sub-nav tabs via {{code|workshopNavModel}} in {{code|component-header.qml}}:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Tab label key  
! {{code|routeName}}  
! Behaviour  
|-  
| {{code|#QTUI_Menu_Workshop_Installed}}  
| {{code|addons}}  
| Loads {{code|routeview-addons.qml}} (this file).  
|-  
| {{code|#QTUI_Menu_Workshop_Browse}}  
| {{code|browse}}  
| Executes the {{code|OPEN_STEAMWORKSHOP}} action in {{code|router.qml}}, which calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.activateSteamOverlayToWebPage("https://steamcommunity.com/app/362890/workshop")</nowiki></code>. No QML route view is loaded.  
|}  
  
====Empty State — gameNameText====  
  
A {{code|Text}} element (id: {{code|gameNameText}}) fills the entire parent and displays the localized string {{code|#BlackMesaUI_WorkshopList_Empty}} centered both horizontally and vertically. It is only {{code|visible}} when <code><nowiki>newGameCarousel.levels.count === 0</nowiki></code>, i.e. when no workshop campaigns are installed. It uses {{code|Theme.fonts.regular}}, {{code|Theme.colors.buttonText}}, and {{code|fontSizeMode: Text.Fit}} to shrink the text if it overflows.  
  
====Carousel — newGameCarousel====  
  
A {{code|Carousel}} (id: {{code|newGameCarousel}}) implements a two-phase selection flow identical in structure to {{code|routeview-newgame.qml}}.  
  
=====Properties=====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|campaignEntries}}  
| {{code|var}}  
| Populated once at construction time. Calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.listCampaignEntries().filter(function (campaign) { return !campaign.isOfficial })</nowiki></code> — the inverse of {{code|routeview-newgame.qml}}, which filters for {{code|isOfficial}}. Contains all installed Workshop (non-official) campaigns.  
|-  
| {{code|currentlySelectedCampaign}}  
| {{code|var}}  
| Initially {{code|null}}. Set to a campaign entry object when the user selects a campaign card. Changing this property triggers the phase transition from campaign list to chapter list.  
|}  
  
=====Component.onCompleted=====  
  
Iterates {{code|campaignEntries}} and appends each item to the {{code|levels}} {{code|ListModel}}. Before appending, it sets <code><nowiki>item.levelName = ""</nowiki></code> on each campaign object, because campaign-level entries do not have a direct map to load — only their chapters do.  
  
=====onCurrentlySelectedCampaignChanged=====  
  
Fires whenever {{code|currentlySelectedCampaign}} changes. If the new value is {{code|null}}, it returns immediately (guard for the initial state). Otherwise:  
  
# Clears {{code|levels}} and repopulates it with the selected campaign's {{code|chapters}} array.  
# Sets <code><nowiki>showLeft = false</nowiki></code> and <code><nowiki>showRight = levels.count > 4</nowiki></code> to reset carousel pagination.  
# If the campaign has exactly one chapter, immediately fires <code><nowiki>newGame.loadMapRequest(currentlySelectedCampaign.chapters[0].levelName, BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString(currentlySelectedCampaign.chapters[0].title))</nowiki></code>, skipping the chapter carousel entirely.  
  
=====Delegates=====  
  
The active delegate is selected by <code><nowiki>itemComponent: currentlySelectedCampaign !== null ? chapterDelegate : campaignDelegate</nowiki></code>.  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Delegate  
! {{code|type}} label  
! {{code|onSelected}} action  
! {{code|onSelectedWithDoubleClick}} action  
|-  
| {{code|campaignDelegate}}  
| <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString("#GameUI_NewGame")</nowiki></code>  
| Sets <code><nowiki>newGameCarousel.currentlySelectedCampaign = newGameCarousel.campaignEntries[index]</nowiki></code>, triggering the phase transition.  
| Calls {{code|selected()}} — identical to single-click.  
|-  
| {{code|chapterDelegate}}  
| <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString("#GameUI_Chapter")</nowiki></code>  
| Fires <code><nowiki>newGame.loadMapRequest(model.levelName, localizedTitle)</nowiki></code>.  
| Calls {{code|selected()}} — identical to single-click.  
|}  
  
Both delegates set {{code|isLocked: false}} unconditionally. Both use {{code|L10n.strings.buttons.startGame}} ({{code|#QTUI_Buttons_StartGame}}) as the button label and {{code|model.imageSource}} as the thumbnail.  
  
====Comparison with routeview-newgame.qml====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Behaviour  
! {{code|routeview-addons.qml}}  
! {{code|routeview-newgame.qml}}  
|-  
| Campaign filter  
| <code><nowiki>!campaign.isOfficial</nowiki></code> (Workshop only)  
| <code><nowiki>campaign.isOfficial</nowiki></code> (official only)  
|-  
| Empty state message  
| Yes — {{code|#BlackMesaUI_WorkshopList_Empty}}  
| No  
|-  
| Auto-skip when one campaign  
| No — user must click the single campaign card  
| Yes — {{code|Component.onCompleted}} sets {{code|currentlySelectedCampaign}} immediately  
|-  
| Auto-skip when one chapter  
| Yes — fires {{code|loadMapRequest}} immediately  
| Yes — fires {{code|loadMapRequest}} immediately  
|-  
| Root {{code|id}}  
| {{code|newGame}} (copy-paste artifact)  
| {{code|newGame}}  
|}  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''Root {{code|id}} is {{code|newGame}}.''' The root {{code|RouteView}} uses {{code|id: newGame}}, identical to {{code|routeview-newgame.qml}}. This is a copy-paste artifact. There is no runtime conflict since each file is a separate component instance, but it is misleading — child elements reference {{code|newGame.loadMapRequest(...)}} which correctly resolves to the local root, but the name implies this is the new-game route.  
  
2. '''{{code|item.levelName [[=]] ""}} mutates engine-returned objects.''' {{code|Component.onCompleted}} directly sets {{code|item.levelName = ""}} on each object in the array returned by {{code|BlackMesaEngine.listCampaignEntries()}}. If the engine returns the same object references across calls (e.g. if the result is cached), this mutation persists and could corrupt the data seen by other consumers of {{code|listCampaignEntries()}}.  
  
3. '''No way to return to the campaign list.''' Once {{code|currentlySelectedCampaign}} is set, the only way to return to the campaign list is to navigate away from the route entirely. Setting {{code|currentlySelectedCampaign = null}} would trigger {{code|onCurrentlySelectedCampaignChanged}}, but the {{code|if (!currentlySelectedCampaign) { return }}} guard prevents the levels from being repopulated with campaigns. There is no Back button or gesture within the page to undo a campaign selection.  
  
4. '''{{code|onSelectedWithDoubleClick}} is identical to {{code|onSelected}}.''' Both delegates call {{code|selected()}} on double-click, making single-click and double-click indistinguishable.  
  
5. '''{{code|type}} label bypasses {{code|L10n}}.''' Both delegates use {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString()}} directly for the {{code|type}} label ({{code|#GameUI_NewGame}} and {{code|#GameUI_Chapter}}) instead of routing through the {{code|L10n}} singleton, inconsistent with the rest of the UI.  
  
6. '''{{code|L10n.strings.menus.addons}} is unused.''' {{code|l10n.qml}} defines {{code|addons: getString("#QTUI_Menu_Workshop_Addons")}} in the {{code|menus}} object, but {{code|workshopNavModel}} in {{code|component-header.qml}} uses {{code|labels.installed}} ({{code|#QTUI_Menu_Workshop_Installed}}) for the tab that routes to {{code|addons}}. The {{code|menus.addons}} key is never referenced anywhere in the codebase.  
  
7. '''{{code|showLeft [[=]] false}} is hardcoded on phase transition.''' When switching to chapter view, {{code|showLeft}} is always forced to {{code|false}} regardless of the chapter count. This is correct for the initial display (the carousel always starts at the leftmost position), but it means the left arrow is never shown even if the user had previously scrolled right and then triggered a re-population — though in practice this scenario cannot occur since {{code|levels.clear()}} resets the carousel position.

===routeview-audio.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-audio.qml}} is the '''Audio Options''' settings page. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|audio}} route at path {{code|options.audio}}, rank 3.5. It is accessible via the Audio tab in the Options sub-navigation, which is populated by {{code|component-header.qml}}'s {{code|optionsNavModel}} using the label <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.menus.audio</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Menu_Options_Audio}}).  
  
The file is 278 lines and follows the same structural pattern as {{code|routeview-video.qml}}: a single {{code|OptionsList}} with complex getter/setter logic, a pending-changes navigation guard, and an unsaved-changes {{code|Alert}} dialog.  
  
====Top-level Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Purpose  
|-  
| {{code|blockingRouteName}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| Stores the destination route name when navigation is blocked by the unsaved-changes guard. Cleared when the alert is dismissed via Cancel.  
|-  
| {{code|rtlLanguages}}  
| {{code|var}} (array)  
| A hardcoded list of language name strings considered right-to-left: {{code|"arabic"}}, {{code|"aramaic"}}, {{code|"azeri"}}, {{code|"dhivehi"}}, {{code|"maldivian"}}, {{code|"hebrew"}}, {{code|"kurdish"}}, {{code|"sorani"}}, {{code|"persian"}}, {{code|"farsi"}}, {{code|"urdu"}}. Used by {{code|setComplex.audio_captioningLang}} to decide whether to set {{command|cc_rightaligned}}.  
|}  
  
====onRouteWillChange====  
  
Identical in structure to {{code|routeview-video.qml}}'s guard. If {{code|audioOptions.hasPendingChanges}} is {{code|true}} and no blocking route is already set, it stores the destination in {{code|blockingRouteName}}, shows {{code|confirmAlert}}, and returns {{code|true}} to cancel the navigation.  
  
====audioOptions (OptionsList)====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|title}}  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.headers.audioOptions</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Header_Options_Audio}})  
|-  
| {{code|batchUpdates}}  
| {{code|true}} — all pending changes are flushed together on Apply  
|-  
| {{code|visibleRows}}  
| {{code|7}} — exactly matches the number of items in the model; no scrolling is required  
|-  
| {{code|anchors.verticalCenterOffset}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(20 * Theme.heightScale)</nowiki></code> — shifts the list slightly below center  
|}  
  
=====Component.onCompleted=====  
  
Calls {{code|setLanguageState()}} once on load to set the initial enabled/disabled state of the captioning language selector.  
  
=====onRequestSettingsChange=====  
  
Handles two special cases when a setting changes:  
  
* If the changed item's key is {{code|audio_captioning}}, calls {{code|setLanguageState()}} to enable or disable the language selector.  
* If the changed item's key is {{code|audio_musicVolume}}, assigns <code><nowiki>Sound.ambientVolume = model.value</nowiki></code> to attempt a live volume update.  
  
=====onSettingsReset=====  
  
Calls {{code|setLanguageState()}} after a full settings reset to re-sync the language selector's disabled state.  
  
=====setLanguageState()=====  
  
Sets <code><nowiki>models.audio_captioningLang.disabled = (models.audio_captioning.value === "0")</nowiki></code>. When captioning is off (value {{code|"0"}}), the language selector is greyed out and non-interactive.  
  
====Complex Getters (getComplex)====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Function  
! CVars read  
! Logic  
|-  
| {{code|audio_captioning()}}  
| {{command|closecaption}}, {{command|cc_subtitles}}  
| Returns {{code|"0"}} if {{command|closecaption}} is 0 (off); {{code|"1"}} if {{command|closecaption}} is 1 and {{command|cc_subtitles}} is 0 (closed captions only); {{code|"2"}} if both are 1 (subtitles).  
|-  
| {{code|audio_quality()}}  
| {{command|dsp_slow_cpu}}, {{command|snd_pitchquality}}  
| Returns {{code|"low"}} if {{command|dsp_slow_cpu}} is true; {{code|"medium"}} if {{command|dsp_slow_cpu}} is false and {{command|snd_pitchquality}} is false; {{code|"high"}} if {{command|snd_pitchquality}} is true.  
|-  
| {{code|audio_captioningLang()}}  
| {{command|cc_lang}}  
| Returns the raw string value of {{command|cc_lang}} directly.  
|}  
  
====Complex Setters (setComplex)====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Function  
! CVars written  
! Logic  
|-  
| {{code|audio_captioning(value)}}  
| {{command|closecaption}}, {{command|cc_subtitles}}  
| {{code|"0"}} → cc=0, subs=1; {{code|"1"}} → cc=1, subs=0; {{code|"2"}} → cc=1, subs=1.  
|-  
| {{code|audio_quality(value)}}  
| {{command|dsp_slow_cpu}}, {{command|snd_pitchquality}}  
| {{code|"low"}} → dsp=1, pitch=0; {{code|"medium"}} → dsp=0, pitch=0; {{code|"high"}} → dsp=0, pitch=1.  
|-  
| {{code|audio_captioningLang(value)}}  
| {{command|cc_lang}}, {{command|cc_rightaligned}}  
| Writes {{command|cc_lang}} with the value string. Then checks <code><nowiki>rtlLanguages.indexOf(value) == 0</nowiki></code>; if true, sets {{command|cc_rightaligned}} to 1, otherwise 0.  
|}  
  
====Model Items====  
  
The {{code|ListModel}} is populated in {{code|Component.onCompleted}} with 7 items:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! # !! Key !! Type !! CVar / Getter !! Range / Options !! Notes  
|-  
| 1 || {{code|audio_gameVolume}} || slider || {{command|volume}} || 0.0 – 1.0, step 0.05, percent formatter || Master game volume  
|-  
| 2 || {{code|audio_fxvolume}} || slider || {{command|snd_fxvolume}} || 0.0 – 1.0, step 0.05, percent formatter || Sound effects volume  
|-  
| 3 || {{code|audio_musicVolume}} || slider || {{command|snd_musicvolume}} || 0.0 – 1.0, step 0.05, percent formatter || Also triggers live {{code|Sound.ambientVolume}} update on change  
|-  
| 4 || {{code|audio_speakerConfig}} || select || {{command|snd_surround_speakers}} || Headphones (0), 2.0 (2), 4.0 (4), 5.1 (5), 7.1 (7) ||  
|-  
| 5 || {{code|audio_quality}} || select || {{code|getComplex}} / {{code|setComplex}} || Low, Medium, High || Maps to {{command|dsp_slow_cpu}} + {{command|snd_pitchquality}}  
|-  
| 6 || {{code|audio_captioning}} || select || {{code|getComplex}} / {{code|setComplex}} || No Captions (0), Closed Captions (1), Subtitles (2) || Maps to {{command|closecaption}} + {{command|cc_subtitles}}; changing this triggers {{code|setLanguageState()}}  
|-  
| 7 || {{code|audio_captioningLang}} || select || {{code|getComplex}} / {{code|setComplex}} || 30 language options (Arabic through Vietnamese) || Disabled when captioning is off; writes {{command|cc_lang}} and {{command|cc_rightaligned}}  
|}  
  
=====Captioning Language Options=====  
  
The 30 options in {{code|audio_captioningLang}} are, in order: Arabic, Chinese Simplified, Chinese Traditional, Croatian, Czech, Danish, Dutch, English, Finnish, French, Georgian, German, Greek, Hungarian, Indonesian, Italian, Japanese, Korean, Lithuanian, Norwegian, Polish, Portuguese, Portuguese (Brazilian), Romanian, Russian, Spanish, Spanish (Mexico), Swedish, Turkish, Ukrainian, Vietnamese.  
  
====confirmAlert====  
  
An {{code|Alert}} shown when the user navigates away with unsaved changes. Identical in structure to the one in {{code|routeview-video.qml}}:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Button type !! {{code|objectName}} !! Label !! Action  
|-  
| {{code|CTAButton}} || <code><nowiki>ui:btn:action[apply]</nowiki></code> || <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.buttons.apply</nowiki></code> || Calls {{code|audioOptions.flushPendingChanges()}}, then navigates to {{code|blockingRouteName}}.  
|-  
| {{code|WarningButton}} || <code><nowiki>ui:btn:action[discard]</nowiki></code> || <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.buttons.discard</nowiki></code> || Navigates to {{code|blockingRouteName}} without saving.  
|-  
| {{code|DefaultButton}} || <code><nowiki>ui:btn:action[cancel]</nowiki></code> || <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.buttons.cancel</nowiki></code> || Clears {{code|blockingRouteName}} and hides the alert.  
|}  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''{{code|Sound.ambientVolume}} does not exist.''' The {{code|Sound}} singleton ({{code|sound.qml}}) defines no {{code|ambientVolume}} property — its only property is {{code|ambientId}} (an {{code|int}} handle). The assignment <code><nowiki>Sound.ambientVolume = model.value</nowiki></code> in {{code|onRequestSettingsChange}} creates a dynamic property on the singleton at runtime but has no effect on the volume of the currently playing ambient track. The music volume is only applied from {{command|snd_musicvolume}} when {{code|startAmbient()}} is next called. Live music volume adjustment while the menu is open does not work.  
  
2. '''{{code|rtlLanguages.indexOf(value) == 0}} checks position, not membership.''' {{code|Array.indexOf}} returns the index of the element, or {{code|-1}} if not found. Comparing to {{code|0}} means the condition is only {{code|true}} when {{code|value}} is the '''first''' element of the array ({{code|"arabic"}}). All other RTL languages in the list — aramaic, azeri, dhivehi, maldivian, hebrew, kurdish, sorani, persian, farsi, urdu — will never set {{command|cc_rightaligned}} to 1. The correct check is <code><nowiki>rtlLanguages.indexOf(value) !== -1</nowiki></code>.  
  
3. '''{{code|audio_captioningLang}} declares {{code|valueType: "int"}} but stores strings.''' The option values are language name strings (e.g. {{code|"arabic"}}, {{code|"english"}}). The {{code|valueType}} should be {{code|"string"}}. Since this item uses a complex getter/setter and has no {{code|cvar}} field, the {{code|valueType}} is likely ignored by the {{code|OptionsList}} at runtime, making this a documentation/consistency issue rather than a functional bug.  
  
4. '''{{code|audio_captioning}} declares {{code|valueType: "int"}} but the complex getter returns strings.''' The getter returns {{code|"0"}}, {{code|"1"}}, or {{code|"2"}} as string values. As with {{code|audio_captioningLang}}, the {{code|valueType}} is irrelevant for complex-getter items, but the declaration is misleading.  
  
5. '''Trailing comma in {{code|audio_captioningLang}} options array.''' The last entry ({{code|"vietnamese"}}) is followed by a trailing comma. This is valid in ES5+ JavaScript but is inconsistent with all other option arrays in the file.  
  
6. '''{{code|disabled: false}} is explicitly set on {{code|audio_captioningLang}}.''' All other items rely on the default. This is redundant but harmless; the actual disabled state is managed dynamically by {{code|setLanguageState()}}.  
  
7. '''{{code|audio_captioning}} setter assigns {{code|subs = 1}} for the "off" case.''' When value is {{code|"0"}} (no captions), the setter writes {{command|cc_subtitles}} as {{code|1}}. This is a latent inconsistency: if {{command|closecaption}} is later set to 1 externally (e.g. via console), subtitles would be active even though the user selected "No Captions". The getter correctly handles this by checking {{command|closecaption}} first, so the UI will still display "No Captions" correctly, but the underlying CVar state is untidy.

===routeview-bindings.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-bindings.qml}} is the '''Key Bindings''' settings page. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|bindings}} route at path {{code|options.bindings}}, rank 3.3. The tab label is <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.menus.bindings</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Menu_Options_Bindings}}), rendered in the Options sub-navigation bar by {{code|component-header.qml}}.  
  
The file is 618 lines. It depends on three supporting files: {{code|input-bindings-manager.qml}} (the {{code|InputBindingsManager}} singleton), {{code|component-binding.qml}} (the {{code|BindingList}} component), and {{code|component-optionslist-input-bindingcell.qml}} (the {{code|BindingCell}} component). Five category files ({{code|component-binding-movement.qml}} through {{code|component-binding-multiplayer.qml}}) define the command lists for each tab.

====onRouteWillChange====  
  
Overrides the {{code|RouteView}} hook. Calls <code><nowiki>InputBindingsManager.clearSelection()</nowiki></code> whenever the user navigates away from the bindings page, ensuring no stale selection persists.  
  
====Layout====  
  
The page is a single centered {{code|Item}} ({{code|pageWidth}} × {{code|pageHeight}}) with three vertical sections stacked top-to-bottom:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Section  
! Height  
! Background  
|-  
| {{code|header}} ({{code|Item}})  
| {{code|headerHeight}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.modalBackground}} at {{code|Theme.opacity.modal}}  
|-  
| {{code|body}} ({{code|Rectangle}})  
| <code><nowiki>rowHeight * visibleRows</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|Theme.colors.skrim}} at {{code|Theme.opacity.modal}}  
|-  
| {{code|footer}} ({{code|Item}})  
| {{code|headerHeight}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.modalBackground}} at {{code|Theme.opacity.modal}}  
|}  
  
=====header=====  
  
Contains a single {{code|Text}} element: <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.headers.bindings</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Header_Options_Bindings}}), {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}}, bold, all-uppercase.  
  
=====body=====  
  
The body is divided into three horizontal elements:  
  
# '''{{code|categoriesList}}''' ({{code|ListView}}, left panel, width = {{code|categoryWidth}}): renders the category list. {{code|interactive: false}} — no scrolling.  
# '''{{code|border}}''' ({{code|Rectangle}}, 3px wide): a vertical divider between the category list and the content area, colored {{code|Theme.colors.modalBackground}} at {{code|Theme.opacity.modal}}.  
# '''{{code|outlet}}''' ({{code|StackView}}, right panel, fills remaining width): displays the active category's binding list.

Built in {{code|Component.onCompleted}}. Appends five category entries sorted by {{code|rank}}, then one Reset entry:

After appending, calls <code><nowiki>outlet.setCategory(categories.movement, true)</nowiki></code> to initialize the right panel to the Movement category with no transition animation.  
  
======Category list delegate (MouseArea)======  
  
Each row is a {{code|MouseArea}} with three visual states:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! State  
! {{code|focusShape}} width  
! {{code|labelText}} color  
! Sound  
|-  
| {{code|blurred}}  
| 0 (hidden)  
| {{code|Theme.colors.buttonText}} (white)  
| —  
|-  
| {{code|focused}}  
| Full parent width (orange fill)  
| {{code|Theme.colors.buttonText}} (white)  
| {{code|Theme.sounds.buttonFocused}}  
|-  
| {{code|selected}}  
| {{code|categoryLabelFocusShapeWidth}} (15px accent bar)  
| {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}} (orange)  
| {{code|Theme.sounds.buttonClicked}}  
|}  
  
Transitions: 250ms {{code|OutQuint}} for blurred↔focused, 150ms for focused→selected, 100ms for selected→blurred.  
  
The {{code|objectName}} is <code><nowiki>"ui:tab"</nowiki></code> for category nav items and <code><nowiki>"ui:btn:action[cancel]"</nowiki></code> for the Reset entry. The {{code|type: "sub-nav"}} property causes {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements()}} to register these as sub-navigation tabs.  
  
The {{code|callback}} property:  
* If the item is not already selected and {{code|action}} is {{code|"nav"}}: calls <code><nowiki>outlet.setCategory(model)</nowiki></code> and sets state to {{code|"selected"}}.  
* If {{code|action}} is {{code|"reset"}}: calls <code><nowiki>resetDefaultsAlert.show()</nowiki></code>.  
* If already selected: sets <code><nowiki>mouse.accepted = true</nowiki></code> (no-op).  
  
======outlet (StackView)======  
  
======setCategory(model, skipTransition)======  
  
Selects a transition direction by comparing {{code|currentModel.rank}} to {{code|model.rank}}:  
* Higher rank (navigating down the list) → {{code|categoryTransitions.up}} (new content slides in from below).  
* Lower rank (navigating up the list) → {{code|categoryTransitions.down}} (new content slides in from above).  
  
Then calls <code><nowiki>InputBindingsManager.clearSelection()</nowiki></code>, sets {{code|currentModel}}, and calls <code><nowiki>outlet.replace(model.qmlFile, { opacity: skipTransition ? 1.0 : 0.0 }, transition)</nowiki></code>.  
  
======Transitions======  
  
Four {{code|Transition}} objects are defined: {{code|upEnterTransition}}, {{code|upExitTransition}}, {{code|downEnterTransition}}, {{code|downExitTransition}}.  
  
Enter transitions (600ms {{code|InOutQuint}} y-slide, 300ms opacity fade-in after a 300ms pause):  
# {{code|ScriptAction}}: calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.clearUIElementsData()</nowiki></code>.  
# {{code|PauseAnimation}}: 300ms.  
# {{code|NumberAnimation}}: opacity 0.0 → 1.0 over 300ms.  
# {{code|ScriptAction}}: calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements(appRoot)</nowiki></code> and <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.afterTraverseCallback()</nowiki></code>.  
  
Exit transitions (600ms {{code|InOutQuint}} y-slide, 300ms opacity fade-out, no UI traversal).  
  
====footer====  
  
A right-aligned {{code|Row}} containing:
