| —  
| Displays <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.modalMessages.bindingInstructions</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Messages_BindingInstructions}})  
|-  
| {{code|CTAButton}} ({{code|editBindingButton}})  
| <code><nowiki>ui:btn:action[apply]</nowiki></code>  
| <code><nowiki>!InputBindingsManager.isBinding</nowiki></code>  
| <code><nowiki>!InputBindingsManager.isSelected</nowiki></code>  
| <code><nowiki>InputBindingsManager.startBindingRequest()</nowiki></code>; label: {{code|#QTUI_Buttons_EditBinding}}  
|-  
| {{code|WarningButton}} ({{code|clearBindingButton}})

|}  
  
The instructions text and the two buttons are mutually exclusive: the text is shown during active key capture, the buttons are shown otherwise.

====Connections (InputBindingsManager)====  
  
{{code|onConflictsChanged}}: When {{code|InputBindingsManager.conflicts}} becomes non-null, formats <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.modalMessages.confirmBindingOverwrite</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Messages_ConfirmBindingOverwrite}}) by replacing {{code|{0}}} with the pressed key and {{code|{1}}} with the conflicting command name(s), both wrapped in an HTML {{code|<span>}} colored {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}}. Sets the formatted string as {{code|confirmBindingOverwriteAlert.messageText}} and shows the alert. When {{code|conflicts}} becomes null, hides the alert.  
  
====InputBindingsManager singleton (input-bindings-manager.qml)====  
  
Registered in {{code|qmldir}} as {{code|singleton InputBindingsManager 1.0}}. Manages all binding state and engine communication.  
  
=====Static maps=====  
  
Three read-only IIFEs built at construction time:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Contents  
|-  
| {{code|commandMap}}  
| Maps engine command strings (e.g. {{code|'+forward'}}) to localized label strings from <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.options.bindings</nowiki></code>. 34 entries total.  
|-  
| {{code|mouseMap}}  
| Maps Qt mouse button enum values (e.g. {{code|Qt.LeftButton}}) to engine key name strings (e.g. {{code|'MOUSE1'}}). 7 entries: MOUSE1–MOUSE5, MWHEELUP, MWHEELDOWN.  
|-  
| {{code|keyMap}}  
| Maps Qt key enum values (e.g. {{code|Qt.Key_Escape}}) to engine key name strings (e.g. {{code|'ESCAPE'}}). Covers A–Z, 0–9, F1–F12, modifier keys, navigation keys, and common punctuation.  
|}  
  
=====State properties=====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|bindings}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| Current bindings map: command → array of key strings. Populated by {{code|getBindings()}}.  
|-  
| {{code|bindingsKeyMap}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| Reverse map: key string → array of command strings. Used for conflict detection.  
|-  
| {{code|inputBindingCallback}}  
| {{code|var}}

| {{code|bool}}

| {{code|bool}}  
| <code><nowiki>!!selectedCfg</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| {{code|selectedCfg}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| The currently selected binding cell config object.  
|-  
| {{code|conflicts}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| Array of conflicting command label strings, or {{code|null}}.  
|-  
| {{code|conflictCfg}}  
| {{code|variant}}  
| Object with {{code|bindingCfg}}, {{code|key}}, {{code|confirm()}}, {{code|cancel()}} for the pending conflict resolution.  
|}  
  
=====Signals=====  
  
{{code|bindingSet()}}, {{code|bindingClear()}}, {{code|defaultsRestored()}} — emitted after the corresponding operation completes. {{code|BindingList}} instances listen to these to call {{code|refreshBindings()}}.  
  
=====Functions=====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Function  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|_readConfigFile(url, callback)}}  
| XHR GET of a VFS config file. Parses lines matching <code><nowiki>/bind\s+"(.*)"\s+"(.*)"/</nowiki></code> into a command→keys map, then calls {{code|callback(map)}}.  
|-  
| {{code|_saveBindingModifications()}}  
| Executes <code><nowiki>exec userconfig.cfg; host_writeconfig</nowiki></code> to persist changes.  
|-  
| {{code|_resetConflicts()}}  
| Nulls {{code|conflicts}} and {{code|conflictCfg}}.  
|-  
| {{code|getBindingComponentConfig(command)}}  
| Returns <code><nowiki>{ label: commandMap[command], type: 'binding', command, key1: '', key2: '' }</nowiki></code>.  
|-  
| {{code|getBindings(commands, callback)}}  
| Reads {{code|vfs:///cfg/config.cfg}}, populates {{code|bindings}} and {{code|bindingsKeyMap}}, calls {{code|callback}} with a map filtered to the requested commands.  
|-  
| {{code|selectBinding(cfg)}}  
| Sets {{code|selectedCfg}}.  
|-  
| {{code|clearSelection()}}  
| Nulls {{code|selectedCfg}}.  
|-  
| {{code|handleBindingResponse(event)}}  
| Calls {{code|inputBindingCallback(event)}} and nulls it. Called by the engine when a key is captured.  
|-  
| {{code|startBindingRequest()}}  
| Begins key capture: calls {{code|cfg.begin()}}, {{code|BlackMesaEngine.requestKeyCapture()}}, and sets {{code|inputBindingCallback}}. On response: if the key is Escape, cancels; if the key conflicts with another command, sets {{code|conflictCfg}} and {{code|conflicts}}; otherwise executes {{code|unbind}} + {{code|bind}} commands and calls {{code|_saveBindingModifications()}}.  
|-  
| {{code|clearSelectedBinding()}}  
| Unbinds the selected key via <code><nowiki>unbind KEY</nowiki></code>, saves, emits {{code|bindingClear()}}.  
|-  
| {{code|restoreDefaultBindings()}}  
| Reads {{code|vfs:///cfg/config_default.cfg}} (result discarded), executes {{code|unbindall; exec config_default.cfg}}, saves, emits {{code|defaultsRestored()}}.  
|}  
  
====BindingList component (component-binding.qml)====  
  
Registered in {{code|qmldir}} as {{code|BindingList 1.0}}. A base {{code|Item}} ({{code|opacity: 0}} by default — made visible by the {{code|outlet}} transition) that wraps a {{code|ListView}} of binding rows.  
  
* {{code|commands}} (variant): array of binding config objects passed in by the category file.  
* {{code|refreshBindings()}}: calls <code><nowiki>InputBindingsManager.getBindings(keys, callback)</nowiki></code> via a 1ms timer, then updates {{code|key1}}/{{code|key2}} on each list model item in {{code|onBindingsChanged}}.  
* Listens to {{code|InputBindingsManager.bindingSet}}, {{code|bindingClear}}, and {{code|defaultsRestored}} to call {{code|refreshBindings()}}.  
* {{code|Component.onCompleted}}: calls {{code|refreshBindings()}}.  
* The {{code|ListView}} delegate loads {{code|component-optionslist-row.qml}} with the binding model via a {{code|Loader}}.  
* A {{code|VerticalScrollBar}} is shown only when content overflows the list height.  
  
====BindingCell component (component-optionslist-input-bindingcell.qml)====  
  
Registered in {{code|qmldir}} as {{code|BindingCell 1.0}}. Renders a single key slot ({{code|key1}} or {{code|key2}}) within a binding row. {{code|objectName: "ui:option-item"}}, {{code|type: "binding"}}.  
  
Four visual states:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! State  
! Text color  
! Background opacity  
! Meaning  
|-  
| {{code|blurred}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.buttonText}} (white)  
| 0  
| Default, unselected  
|-  
| {{code|focused}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}} (orange)  
| 0.2  
| Mouse hover  
|-  
| {{code|selected}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.text}} (white)  
| 0.5  
| Row selected, ready to edit  
|-  
| {{code|binding}}  
| {{code|transparent}}  
| 1.0 (solid orange)  
| Key capture in progress  
|}  
  
Click behavior is a two-step interaction:  
1. First click (from {{code|blurred}} or {{code|focused}}): calls {{code|makeSelectionRequest()}}, which emits {{code|requestInputSelection(beginBinding, endBinding)}}. The upstream handler (in {{code|component-optionslist-row.qml}}) calls {{code|InputBindingsManager.selectBinding(cfg)}} and invokes {{code|beginBinding()}}, transitioning the cell to {{code|"binding"}} state.  
2. Second click (from {{code|selected}}): calls {{code|startBinding()}}, which calls <code><nowiki>InputBindingsManager.startBindingRequest()</nowiki></code> directly.  
  
All state transitions animate over 150ms with {{code|OutQuint}} easing.  
  
====Category files====  
  
Each category file is a {{code|BindingList}} that passes a fixed {{code|commands}} array to the base component. All commands are resolved via <code><nowiki>InputBindingsManager.getBindingComponentConfig(command)</nowiki></code>.  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! File  
! Commands  
|-  
| {{code|component-binding-movement.qml}}  
| {{code|+forward}}, {{code|+back}}, {{code|+moveleft}}, {{code|+moveright}}, {{code|+jump}}, {{code|+duck}}, {{code|+speed}} (7)  
|-  
| {{code|component-binding-combat.qml}}  
| {{code|+use}}, {{code|+attack}}, {{code|+attack2}}, {{code|+reload}}, {{code|impulse 100}}, {{code|+zoom}}, {{code|invnext}}, {{code|invprev}}, {{code|lastinv}}, {{code|slot1}}–{{code|slot6}} (15)  
|-  
| {{code|component-binding-misc.qml}}  
| {{code|save quick}}, {{code|load quick}}, {{code|toggleconsole}}, <code><nowiki>incrementvar closecaption 0 1 1</nowiki></code>, {{code|+moveup}}, {{code|+movedown}}, {{code|+lookup}}, {{code|+lookdown}}, {{code|+left}}, {{code|+right}} (10)  
|-  
| {{code|component-binding-weapons.qml}}  
| {{code|use weapon_crowbar}}, {{code|use weapon_glock}}, {{code|use weapon_357}}, {{code|use weapon_mp5}}, {{code|use weapon_shotgun}}, {{code|use weapon_crossbow}}, {{code|use weapon_rpg}}, {{code|use weapon_tau}}, {{code|use weapon_gluon}}, {{code|use weapon_hivehand}}, {{code|use weapon_frag}}, {{code|use weapon_tripmine}}, {{code|use weapon_satchel}}, {{code|use weapon_snark}} (14)  
|-  
| {{code|component-binding-multiplayer.qml}}  
| {{code|chooseteam}}, {{code|+voicerecord}} (2)  
|}  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''{{code|headerHeight}} uses {{code|widthScale}}.''' <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(85 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code> is used for both the header and footer height instead of {{code|Theme.heightScale}}. At non-16:9 resolutions the header and footer will be incorrectly sized vertically.  
  
2. '''{{code|bindingsKeyMap.push(command)}} bug.''' In {{code|getBindings()}}, when a key already exists in {{code|bindingsKeyMap}}, the code calls <code><nowiki>bindingsKeyMap.push(command)</nowiki></code> instead of <code><nowiki>bindingsKeyMap[key].push(command)</nowiki></code>. {{code|bindingsKeyMap}} is a plain object, not an array, so {{code|push}} is {{code|undefined}} and this call throws a {{code|TypeError}} at runtime. The second command for any multiply-bound key is never added to the reverse map, causing conflict detection to miss cases where a key is bound to more than one command.  
  
3. '''{{code|restoreDefaultBindings()}} ignores the parsed config.''' The function reads {{code|vfs:///cfg/config_default.cfg}} via XHR and parses it, but the parsed {{code|response}} is never used — the commented-out code shows the original intent was to iterate over it and re-apply bindings individually. The current implementation simply executes {{code|unbindall; exec config_default.cfg}} unconditionally, making the XHR call and parse entirely redundant.  
  
4. '''{{code|confirmBindingOverwriteAlert}} has no initial {{code|messageText}}.''' The property is only set dynamically by {{code|onConflictsChanged}}. If the alert were shown before the first conflict event, it would display an empty message body.  
  
5. '''Trailing comma in {{code|component-binding-multiplayer.qml}}.''' The {{code|commands}} array ends with a trailing comma after {{code|'+voicerecord'}}. This is valid in ES5+ but may cause issues in older V8/QML engine versions.  
  
6. '''{{code|visibleRows: 7}} with 6 category list items.''' The {{code|body}} height is {{code|7 * rowHeight}}, but the category {{code|ListView}} only has 6 items (5 categories + Reset). The category list is shorter than the body, leaving a blank gap at the bottom of the left panel.  
  
7. '''No pending-changes guard.''' Unlike {{code|routeview-video.qml}}, there is no {{code|onRouteWillChange}} confirmation dialog. Navigating away while a key capture is in progress ({{code|isBinding}} is {{code|true}}) silently cancels the capture with no feedback to the user.

===routeview-campaigns.qml===  
{{code|routeview-campaigns.qml}} is a 9-line stub file. It is a {{code|RouteView}} containing a single {{code|Text}} element that displays the literal string {{code|"..."}} centered on screen. It has no properties, no logic, no signals, and no interactive elements.  

{{code|routeview-campaigns.qml}} is '''not registered''' in {{code|routes.qml}}. There is no {{code|campaigns}} route defined anywhere in the routing system. A search across the entire QML codebase finds '''zero references''' to this file by name — it is not listed in {{code|qmldir}}, not referenced in {{code|component-header.qml}}, and not loaded by any router or engine call visible in the QML layer.

===routeview-empty.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-empty.qml}} is the '''blank/null route view'''. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|empty}} route and serves as the router's initial state and as the visual placeholder used during level loading and UI-hidden transitions.  
  
The file is 4 lines and contains no content beyond the base class instantiation:
  
<syntaxhighlight lang="qml">  
import QtQuick 2.9  
import CrowbarCollective 1.0  
  
RouteView {}  
</syntaxhighlight>  
  
It renders nothing — no children, no properties, no signals overridden.  
  
====Route Registration====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|name}}  
| {{code|"empty"}}  
|-  
| {{code|path}}  
| {{code|"empty"}}  
|-  
| {{code|qmlFile}}  
| {{code|"routeview-empty.qml"}}  
|-  
| {{code|alert}}  
| (not set)  
|-  
| {{code|rank}}  
| (not set)  
|-  
| {{code|passthrough}}  
| (not set)  
|}  
  
The comment in {{code|routes.qml}} reads: ''"empty route that displays nothing"''.  
  
====Role in the Router====  
  
=====Initial State=====  
  
{{code|router.qml}} (a {{code|StackView}}) transitions to the empty route in its {{code|Component.onCompleted}} handler, making it the first view pushed onto the stack before any engine events have fired:  
  
<syntaxhighlight lang="qml">  
Component.onCompleted: {  
    outlet.setRoute(emptyRoute.name)  
}  
</syntaxhighlight>  
  
=====Transition Behaviour=====  
  
The {{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}} function in {{code|router.qml}} contains three branches that reference the empty route:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Condition  
! Transition used  
! Notes  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code> (navigating ''to'' empty)  
| {{code|routeTransitions.up}} — slides in from y=+300  
| Caught by the same branch as alert routes: <code><nowiki>route.alert || route.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code> (second check, line 65)  
| {{code|routeTransitions.down}} — slides in from y=−300  
| '''Dead branch.''' This condition is unreachable because the first branch (line 62) already matches any navigation to the empty route and returns early. The {{code|down}} transition is never applied via this path.  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>currentRoute.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code> (navigating ''away'' from empty)  
| {{code|routeTransitions.down}} — slides in from y=−300  
| Applied when the router transitions from empty to any non-alert route (e.g. to {{code|index}} on startup).  
|}  
  
====Usage in main.qml====  
  
{{code|main.qml}} aliases the route as {{code|emptyRoute}} for convenience:  
  
<syntaxhighlight lang="qml">  
readonly property Route emptyRoute: Routes.empty  
</syntaxhighlight>  
  
It is routed to in two engine event handlers:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Handler  
! Call  
! Behaviour  
|-  
| {{code|onGameUIHidden}}  
| <code><nowiki>router.setRoute(emptyRoute.name, didLoadLevel)</nowiki></code>  
| Transitions to empty whenever the game UI is hidden. If {{code|didLoadLevel}} is {{code|true}} (the UI is hiding because a level just finished loading), the second argument causes {{code|setRoute}} to use {{code|StackView.Immediate}}, skipping the animation entirely. If the user dismissed the UI manually (Esc), {{code|didLoadLevel}} is {{code|false}} and the {{code|up}} transition plays.  
|-  
| {{code|onLevelLoadingStarted}}  
| <code><nowiki>router.setRoute(emptyRoute.name, true)</nowiki></code>  
| Immediately transitions to empty (no animation) as soon as a level begins loading, clearing all menu content from the screen before the loading screen takes over.  
|}  
  
====Usage in component-footer.qml====  
  
The footer uses the empty route name in two visibility conditions:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Expression  
! Effect  
|-  
| {{code|_showBackButton}}  
| <code><nowiki>currentRoute.name !== Routes.index.name && currentRoute.name !== Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code>  
| The Back button is hidden on the empty route (there is nothing to go back to from a blank screen).  
|-  
| {{code|_showBackToGameButton}}  
| <code><nowiki>isInGame && (currentRoute.name === Routes.index.name || currentRoute.name === Routes.empty.name)</nowiki></code>  
| The "Back to Game" button is shown on the empty route when {{code|isInGame}} is {{code|true}}, allowing the player to dismiss the UI from the loading/blank state.  
|}  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''Dead branch in {{code|_getRouteTransitionConfig}}.''' The condition <code><nowiki>route.name === Routes.empty.name</nowiki></code> appears twice in the transition logic. The first occurrence (line 62, combined with the alert check) always matches first and returns {{code|routeTransitions.up}}. The second occurrence (line 65) is therefore unreachable, and the intended {{code|routeTransitions.down}} transition for navigating to the empty route is never applied via that path.  
  
2. '''{{code|_showBackToGameButton}} on the empty route is unreachable in practice.''' The empty route is only active when the UI is hidden ({{code|onGameUIHidden}}) or a level is loading ({{code|onLevelLoadingStarted}}). In both cases {{code|appRoot.visible}} is {{code|false}} or the footer is in loading mode, so the "Back to Game" button condition is technically satisfied but never visible to the user.

===routeview-game.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-game.qml}} is the '''Game Options''' settings page. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|game}} route at path {{code|options.game}}, rank 3.1. Because the parent {{code|options}} route is {{code|passthrough: true}} with {{code|destination: "game"}}, navigating to the Options main-nav button lands directly on this page. It is the first entry in {{code|optionsNavModel}} in {{code|component-header.qml}}, with tab label <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.menus.game</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Menu_Options_Game}}).  
  
The file is 263 lines and contains one top-level property, four helper functions, and one {{code|OptionsList}}.  
  
====Top-level Property====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Default  
! Purpose  
|-  
| {{code|availableMultiplayerModels}}  
| {{code|var}}  
| {{code|[]}}  
| Populated in {{code|Component.onCompleted}} from <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getAvailableMultiplayerModels()</nowiki></code>. Used as the source data for the multiplayer character model selector.  
|}  
  
====Helper Functions====  
  
=====generateModelSelectData()=====  
  
Maps {{code|availableMultiplayerModels}} to an array of <code><nowiki>{ label, value }</nowiki></code> objects. Labels are resolved via <code><nowiki>L10n.getString(item.name)</nowiki></code>; values are {{code|item.model}} (the model path string).  
  
=====findSkinsForCurrentSelectedModel(model)=====  
  
Calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getCurrentMultiplayerModelSkins(model)</nowiki></code> and returns the result directly. The return value is a skin count (integer), not an array, despite the variable name {{code|skins}} used at the call site.  
  
=====generateModelSkinsSelectData(model, skinCount)=====  
  
Builds a skin options array by iterating from {{code|0}} to {{code|skinCount - 1}}. Each entry is <code><nowiki>{ label: L10n.getString("#" + model + "_Skin" + i), value: Number(i).toString() }</nowiki></code>. Skin localization keys follow the pattern <code><nowiki>#<modelpath>_Skin0</nowiki></code>, <code><nowiki>#<modelpath>_Skin1</nowiki></code>, etc.  
  
=====setCurrentMultiplayerModel(model, skin)=====  
  
Calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.setCurrentMultiplayerModel(model, skin)</nowiki></code> inside a {{code|try}}/{{code|catch}} block. The error handler body is entirely commented out (<code><nowiki>//BlackMesaEngine.printConsoleMessage("#AFE4AA", e);</nowiki></code>), so errors are silently swallowed.  
  
====gameOptions OptionsList====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|title}}  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.headers.gameOptions</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Header_Options_Game}})  
|-  
| {{code|visibleRows}}  
| {{code|7}} (list scrolls; there are 13 items total)  
|-  
| {{code|anchors.centerIn}}  
| {{code|parent}}  
|-  
| {{code|anchors.verticalCenterOffset}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(20 * Theme.heightScale)</nowiki></code>  
|}  
  
{{code|onRequestSettingsChange}} calls {{code|setValue(model, value)}} directly with no custom logic. There is no {{code|onRouteWillChange}} override, so navigating away silently discards any unapplied changes.  
  
=====groups=====  
  
Two group handler functions are defined on the {{code|groups}} {{code|QtObject}}:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Function  
! Trigger  
! Behaviour  
|-  
| {{code|mp_multiplayer_character_model_group()}}  
| Character model selector changes  
| Fetches the skin count for the newly selected model, regenerates the skin options list via {{code|generateModelSkinsSelectData}}, resets the skin value to {{code|"0"}}, disables the skin selector if the skin count is less than 2, then calls {{code|setCurrentMultiplayerModel}} with the new model and skin {{code|"0"}}.  
|-  
| {{code|mp_multiplayer_character_skin_group()}}  
| Character skin selector changes  
| Calls {{code|setCurrentMultiplayerModel}} with the current model value and the newly selected skin value.  
|}  
  
=====Settings Items=====  
  
All 13 items are appended in {{code|gameOptionsModel.Component.onCompleted}}. Items 1–11 use direct CVar bindings; items 12–13 are managed entirely through the {{code|groups}} mechanism and have no {{code|cvar}} property.  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! # !! Key !! Type !! CVar !! Options / Range  
|-  
| 1 || {{code|game_difficulty}} || {{code|select}} (int) || {{command|skill}} || Easy = {{code|1}}, Normal = {{code|2}}, Hard = {{code|3}}  
|-  
| 2 || {{code|game_alwaysRun}} || {{code|select}} (int) || {{command|sv_always_run}} || No = {{code|0}}, Yes = {{code|1}}  
|-  
| 3 || {{code|game_viewRoll}} || {{code|slider}} (real) || {{command|cl_view_roll}} || 0.0 – 3.5, step 0.1, precision 1, {{code|decimal}} formatter  
|-  
| 4 || {{code|game_fastWeapon}} || {{code|select}} (int) || {{command|hud_fastswitch}} || No = {{code|0}}, Yes = {{code|1}}  
|-  
| 5 || {{code|game_AutoWeaponSwitch}} || {{code|select}} (int) || {{command|cl_weapon_autoswitch}} || No = {{code|0}}, Yes = {{code|1}}  
|-  
| 6 || {{code|game_classicIronSight}} || {{code|select}} (int) || {{command|cl_ironsight_classic}} || No = {{code|0}}, Yes = {{code|1}}  
|-  
| 7 || {{code|game_autoCrouch}} || {{code|select}} (int) || {{command|cl_auto_crouch_jump}} || No = {{code|0}}, Yes = {{code|1}}  
|-  
| 8 || {{code|game_toggleCrouch}} || {{code|select}} (int) || {{command|cl_toggle_duck}} || No = {{code|0}}, Yes = {{code|1}}  
|-  
| 9 || {{code|game_announcer}} || {{code|select}} (string) || {{command|bm_killstreaks_voice_pack_file}} || Marine = value of {{command|bm_marine_announcer}}, Scientist = value of {{command|bm_scientist_announcer}}  
|-  
| 10 || {{code|game_devConsole}} || {{code|select}} (int) || {{command|con_enable}} || No = {{code|0}}, Yes = {{code|1}}  
|-  
| 11 || {{code|game_autoAim}} || {{code|select}} (int) || {{command|sk_autoaim_enabled}} || No = {{code|0}}, Yes = {{code|1}}  
|-  
| 12 || {{code|mp_multiplayer_character_model}} || {{code|select}} (string) || (none — group-managed) || Dynamic; generated by {{code|generateModelSelectData()}} at construction time  
|-  
| 13 || {{code|mp_multiplayer_character_skin}} || {{code|select}} (int) || (none — group-managed) || Dynamic; generated by {{code|generateModelSkinsSelectData()}} for the current model  
|}  
  
The announcer option values are read from the engine at construction time via <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getConsoleVariableAsString("bm_marine_announcer")</nowiki></code> and <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getConsoleVariableAsString("bm_scientist_announcer")</nowiki></code>, making the option values indirect CVar references rather than hardcoded strings.  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''{{code|game_classicIronSight}} label bypasses {{code|L10n}}.''' Its label uses <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString("#BlackMesaUI_Options_Game_IronSightClassic")</nowiki></code> directly instead of the {{code|L10n}} singleton, inconsistent with all other items in the list which use {{code|L10n.strings.options.labels.*}}.  
  
2. '''{{code|game_fastWeapon}} key/label mismatch.''' The item's {{code|key}} is {{code|"game_fastWeapon"}} but the corresponding {{code|L10n}} label key is {{code|game_weaponSwitch}}. The two identifiers are inconsistent, though this has no functional impact.  
  
3. '''{{code|skins < 2}} compares a count, not an array.''' {{code|findSkinsForCurrentSelectedModel}} returns an integer count. The variable is named {{code|skins}}, which implies an array. The comparison <code><nowiki>if (skins < 2)</nowiki></code> is numerically correct but semantically misleading.  
  
4. '''Multiplayer skin initial value is hardcoded.''' {{code|mp_multiplayer_character_skin}} is initialized with {{code|value: "0"}} unconditionally. The current skin is never read from the engine on page load, so the skin selector always resets to the first skin when the page is opened.  
  
5. '''Multiplayer model/skin section is wrapped in a silent try/catch.''' If {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getAvailableMultiplayerModels()}} or {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getCurrentMultiplayerModel()}} throws, {{code|currentMultiplayerModel}} and {{code|currentMultiplayerModelSkins}} remain {{code|undefined}}. The subsequent {{code|append}} calls for items 12 and 13 will then use {{code|undefined.model}} and pass {{code|undefined}} as the skin list, producing broken selectors with no error surfaced to the user.  
  
6. '''No pending-changes guard.''' Unlike {{code|routeview-video.qml}}, there is no {{code|onRouteWillChange}} override. Navigating away after changing settings but before they are flushed silently discards all changes.  
  
7. '''{{code|visibleRows: 7}} with 13 items.''' The list requires scrolling to reach items 8–13 (Auto Crouch Jump through Multiplayer Skin). The multiplayer model and skin selectors are below the fold by default.

===routeview-host.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-host.qml}} is the '''Host Game''' configuration page. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|host}} route at path {{code|multiplayer.host}}, rank 2.2. It has no explicit {{code|id}} on the root {{code|RouteView}}.  
  
The page is accessed via the '''Host''' sub-nav tab in the Multiplayer section. The tab is defined in {{code|component-header.qml}} inside {{code|multiplayerNavModel}} with {{code|isEnabled: true}}. Notably, {{code|multiplayerNavModel.Component.onCompleted}} also executes <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.executeClientCommandUnrestricted("exec server.cfg;")</nowiki></code> before appending the tab entries; a comment in that file reads "this one is needed for correct host settings load". This means {{command|server.cfg}} is loaded whenever the user first navigates to any Multiplayer sub-route, not only when the Host tab is opened.  
  
====Scaling Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
! Notes  
|-  
| {{code|headerHeight}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(80 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code>  
| Uses {{code|widthScale}}, not {{code|heightScale}}. See Notable Issues.  
|-  
| {{code|headerMargin}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(16 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code>  
|  
|-  
| {{code|headerFontSize}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(32 * Theme.heightScale)</nowiki></code>  
|  
|-  
| {{code|optionHeight}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(72 * Theme.heightScale)</nowiki></code>  
| Used for all settings rows except the map row.  
|}  
  
====handleSettingsChange====  
  
<syntaxhighlight lang="javascript">function handleSettingsChange(model, value) {  
    model.value = value; // lol  
}</syntaxhighlight>  
  
The sole change handler for all settings rows. It updates the in-memory {{code|ListElement}} value only. It does '''not''' write to any CVar, does not validate input, and does not track pending changes. All CVar writes are deferred until the Launch Game button is clicked.  
  
====Layout====  
  
The entire page is a single {{code|Item}} sized at 1024×768 (scaled) and centered in the parent with a vertical offset of <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(20 * Theme.heightScale)</nowiki></code>. It is divided into three vertically stacked sections:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Section id  
! Height  
! Background  
|-  
| {{code|header}}  
| {{code|headerHeight}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.modalBackground}} at {{code|Theme.opacity.modal}}  
|-  
| (background rectangle)  
| Fills between {{code|header.bottom}} and {{code|footer.top}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.skrim}} at {{code|Theme.opacity.modal}}  
|-  
| {{code|contents}}  
| Fills between {{code|header.bottom}} and {{code|footer.top}}  
| Transparent (overlays the background rectangle)  
|-  
| {{code|footer}}  
| {{code|headerHeight}}  
| {{code|Theme.colors.modalBackground}} at {{code|Theme.opacity.modal}}  
|}  
  
=====header=====  
  
Contains a single {{code|Text}} element displaying the localized string {{code|#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_HostGame}} in {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}}, bold, uppercase, at {{code|headerFontSize}}.  
  
=====contents=====  
  
Contains six {{code|OptionsListRow}} items stacked vertically. {{code|Component.onCompleted}} connects each row's {{code|requestSettingsChange}} signal to {{code|handleSettingsChange}} via {{code|row.requestSettingsChange.connect(handleSettingsChange)}}. See Notable Issues regarding the inline {{code|onRequestSettingsChange}} handlers also present on each row.

! Container height

| {{code|optionHeight}}

| {{code|map}}  
| {{code|#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_Map}}  
| {{code|map}}  
| {{code|"dm_stalkyard"}} (hardcoded)  
| <code><nowiki>200 * Theme.widthScale</nowiki></code>

| {{code|fragLimit}}

|-  
| {{code|timeLimit}}  
| {{code|#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_TimeLimit}}  
| {{code|text}}  
| <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getConsoleVariableAsInt("mp_timelimit") / 60</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|optionHeight}}  
|-  
| {{code|teams}}  
| {{code|#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_TeamPlay}}  
| {{code|checkbox}}  
| <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getConsoleVariableAsBoolean("mp_teamplay")</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|optionHeight}}  
|-  
| {{code|respawn}}  
| {{code|#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_ForceRespawn}}  
| {{code|checkbox}}  
| <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getConsoleVariableAsBoolean("mp_forcerespawn")</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|optionHeight}}  
|}  
  
The {{code|map}} row's {{code|options}} array is a hardcoded list of 11 deathmatch maps:  
  
{{code|dm_bounce}}, {{code|dm_chopper}}, {{code|dm_crossfire}}, {{code|dm_gasworks}}, {{code|dm_lambdabunker}}, {{code|dm_power}}, {{code|dm_rail}}, {{code|dm_stack}}, {{code|dm_stalkyard}}, {{code|dm_subtransit}}, {{code|dm_undertow}}.  
  
The {{code|timeLimit}} row reads {{command|mp_timelimit}} (stored in seconds) and divides by 60 for display. On write, the stored value is multiplied by 60 before being passed to the engine.  
  
=====footer=====  
  
Contains a {{code|Timer}} (id: {{code|launchGameTimer}}) and a {{code|CTAButton}} (id: {{code|launchButton}}).  
  
======launchButton======  
  
{{code|objectName: "ui:btn:action[apply]"}}, text: {{code|#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_LaunchGame}}, centered in the footer.  
  
{{code|onClicked}}:  
# Calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.executeClientCommandUnrestricted("disconnect")</nowiki></code>.  
# Starts {{code|launchGameTimer}}.  
# Disables itself ({{code|launchButton.enabled = false}}).  
  
======launchGameTimer======  
  
Interval: 125 ms, {{code|repeat: true}}. On each tick, checks <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.isInGame()</nowiki></code>. If the engine reports it is '''not''' in a game session, the timer:  
  
# Writes all user-configured CVars to the engine:  
#* {{command|hostname}} from {{code|serverNameModel.value}}.
#* {{command|mp_timelimit}} from <code><nowiki>timeLimitModel.value * 60</nowiki></code>.
#* {{command|mp_fraglimit}} from {{code|fragLimitModel.value}}.
#* {{command|mp_teamplay}} from <code><nowiki>JSON.parse(teamsModel.value)</nowiki></code>.
#* {{command|mp_forcerespawn}} <- <code><nowiki>JSON.parse(respawnModel.value)</nowiki></code>  
# Hardcodes the following CVars regardless of any user preference:  
#* {{command|mp_friendlyfire}} = {{code|false}}  
#* {{command|mp_flashlight}} = {{code|false}}  
#* {{command|mp_warmup_time}} = {{code|15}}  
#* {{command|sv_lan}} = {{code|false}}  
# Executes: <code><nowiki>sv_cheats 0; maxplayers 12; map <mapname></nowiki></code> (player count hardcoded to 12).  
# Stops the timer and re-enables {{code|launchButton}}.  
# Calls {{code|BlackMesaEngine.writeHostMultiplayerSettings()}}.  
  
The polling approach is used to wait for the {{code|disconnect}} command to fully take effect before issuing the {{code|map}} command.  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''{{code|headerHeight}} uses {{code|widthScale}}.''' Both the header and footer heights are computed as <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(80 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code> instead of {{code|Theme.heightScale}}. At non-16:9 resolutions, the header and footer will be disproportionately tall or short relative to the rest of the UI.  
  
2. '''{{code|mapContainer}} height uses {{code|widthScale}}.''' The map row container height is <code><nowiki>200 * Theme.widthScale</nowiki></code>, inconsistent with {{code|optionHeight}} which uses {{code|heightScale}}.  
  
3. '''Inline {{code|onRequestSettingsChange}} handlers are no-ops.''' Each {{code|OptionsListRow}} declares <code><nowiki>onRequestSettingsChange: handleSettingsChange</nowiki></code>. In QML, this evaluates {{code|handleSettingsChange}} as an expression when the signal fires, returning the function reference without calling it. The actual connections are made in {{code|contents.Component.onCompleted}} via {{code|connect()}}. The inline declarations are dead code.  
  
4. '''No pending-changes guard.''' Unlike {{code|routeview-video.qml}}, there is no {{code|onRouteWillChange}} override. Navigating away after editing settings but before clicking Launch silently discards all changes.  
  
5. '''Map default is hardcoded, not read from a CVar.''' {{code|mapModel.value}} is initialized to {{code|"dm_stalkyard"}} unconditionally. It does not read any engine variable, so the previously used map is never restored on page load.  
  
6. '''Player count is hardcoded to 12 (instead of 32).''' The {{code|maxplayers}} argument in the launch command is a literal {{code|12}} with no corresponding UI control.  
  
7. '''{{command|mp_friendlyfire}}, {{command|mp_flashlight}}, and {{command|mp_warmup_time}} are silently forced.''' These three settings are always written to fixed values ({{code|false}}, {{code|false}}, {{code|15}}) on every launch, with no UI option to change them.  
  
8. '''{{command|sv_lan}} is always {{code|false}}.''' The server is always launched as an internet server. There is no LAN-only option.  
  
9. '''{{code|teamsModel}} and {{code|respawnModel}} use fragile boolean parsing.''' The checkbox rows store their values as strings via {{code|getConsoleVariableAsBoolean()}}. On write, <code><nowiki>JSON.parse(teamsModel.value)</nowiki></code> is used to convert back to a boolean. If the engine returns a value other than {{code|"true"}} or {{code|"false"}} (e.g. {{code|"1"}} or {{code|"0"}}), {{code|JSON.parse}} will throw a {{code|SyntaxError}}.  
  
10. '''No {{code|objectName}} on any {{code|OptionsListRow}}.''' None of the six rows have an {{code|objectName}} set, so {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements()}} will not register them as option items. Keyboard and controller navigation cannot move between rows on this page.  
  
11. '''All label strings bypass {{code|L10n}}.''' Every label and the page title use {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString()}} directly with {{code|#BlackMesaUI_*}} keys, inconsistent with the rest of the options pages which use the {{code|L10n}} singleton.

===routeview-index.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-index.qml}} is the '''main menu landing page'''. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|index}} route with no {{code|alert}} flag and no {{code|rank}}.  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|name}}  
| {{code|"index"}}  
|-  
| {{code|path}}  
| {{code|"index"}}  
|-  
| {{code|qmlFile}}  
| {{code|"routeview-index.qml"}}  
|}  
  
The router navigates to this route from {{code|main.qml}} in three situations:  
  
1. {{code|onGameUIActived}} — when the game UI is activated and either this is the initial load or the UI was previously hidden. 
 
2. {{code|onLevelLoadingFinished}} — after any level finishes loading (both the main menu background map and real game levels).  

3. {{code|autoBackgroundMusicTimer.onTriggered}} — a 1250 ms fallback timer that fires once on startup in case the normal activation path does not run.  
  
====Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Default  
! Description

| {{code|lastSave}}  
| {{code|variant}}

|-  
| {{code|isInGame}}  
| {{code|bool}}  
| {{code|false}}  
| Whether the player is currently in an active game session. Set externally by {{code|main.qml}} via the {{code|Router}} {{code|isInGame}} binding.  
|-

====Component.onCompleted====  
  
On construction, the route:  
  
1. Fetches <code><nowiki>lastSave = BlackMesaUtils.getSaveGames()[0]</nowiki></code>.  

2. Calls {{code|loadRoguePointCTAVisibility()}} to restore the CTA preference from SQLite.  

3. Selects which sub-component to load into the {{code|Loader}}:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Condition (evaluated in order)  
! Sub-component loaded  
|-  
| {{code|isInGame [[=]][[=]][[=]] true}}  
| {{code|indexInGame}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>!isInGame && lastSave</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|indexContinue}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>!isInGame && !lastSave</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|indexNew}}  
|}  
  
====LocalStorage persistence====  
  
The file imports {{code|QtQuick.LocalStorage 2.0}} and uses a synchronous SQLite database to persist the CTA visibility preference across sessions.  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Function  
! Behaviour  
|-  
| {{code|saveRoguePointCTAVisibility()}}  
| Opens the {{code|"BlackMesa"}} database (version {{code|"1.0"}}, 100 KB limit). Creates a {{code|Settings(key TEXT, value TEXT)}} table if it does not exist. Inserts or replaces the row <code><nowiki>["showRoguePointCTA", showRoguePointCTA]</nowiki></code>.  
|-  
| {{code|loadRoguePointCTAVisibility()}}  
| Opens the same database. Reads the {{code|showRoguePointCTA}} row and sets <code><nowiki>showRoguePointCTA = (value === 'true')</nowiki></code> if the row exists.  
|}  
  
====Loader====  
  
A {{code|Loader}} (id: {{code|loader}}) fills the entire {{code|RouteView}}. Its {{code|onStatusChanged}} handler fires when {{code|loader.status [[=]][[=]][[=]] Loader.Ready}} and re-registers the newly loaded sub-component with the navigation system by calling:  
  
1. <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.clearUIElementsData()</nowiki></code>  

2. <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements(appRoot)</nowiki></code>  

3. <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.afterTraverseCallback()</nowiki></code>  
  
====roguePointCTA — Rogue Point Wishlist Banner====  
  
An {{code|Image}} anchored to the top-right corner of the page. It displays {{code|ui/images/rp-wishlist-cta.png}} at a nominal 652×144 pixels (both dimensions scaled by {{code|Theme.widthScale}}). It is only visible when {{code|showRoguePointCTA}} is {{code|true}}, which is forced to {{code|false}} when {{code|isInGame}} is {{code|true}}.  
  
Opacity animates over 200 ms via a {{code|Behavior on opacity}} {{code|NumberAnimation}}.  
  
=====MouseArea (hoverArea)=====  
  
Covers the entire banner. {{code|hoverEnabled: true}}, {{code|cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor}}.  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Handler  
! Action  
|-  
| {{code|onClicked}}  
| Calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.activateSteamOverlayToWebPage("https://store.steampowered.com/app/1843840?utm_source=blackmesa_ui&utm_campaign=wishlists")</nowiki></code> and plays {{code|Theme.sounds.buttonClicked}}.  
|-  
| {{code|onEntered}}  
| Sets {{code|roguePointCTA.opacity}} to {{code|0.6}} and plays {{code|Theme.sounds.buttonFocused}}.  
|-  
| {{code|onExited}}  
| Restores {{code|roguePointCTA.opacity}} to {{code|1.0}}.  
|}  
  
=====closeButton=====  
  
A 30×30 transparent {{code|Rectangle}} with a white border and {{code|radius: 15}} (circle), positioned at the top-right corner of the banner with 15 px margins. Contains a centered white "X" {{code|Text}} element. Its {{code|MouseArea.onClicked}} sets {{code|showRoguePointCTA = false}}, calls {{code|saveRoguePointCTAVisibility()}}, and plays {{code|Theme.sounds.buttonClicked}}.  
  
====Sub-components====

Loaded when {{code|isInGame}} is {{code|true}}. Displays the current chapter name, a live clock, and save/disconnect controls.

Action: <code><nowiki>index.loadGameRequest(lastSave)</nowiki></code>, passing the full save object to the router, which extracts {{code|savegame.chapterName}} and {{code|savegame.savename}} and calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.loadGame(savegame.savename)}}.

====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''Redundant {{code|loader.sourceComponent}} assignment in Disconnect handler.''' The Disconnect button manually sets <code><nowiki>loader.sourceComponent = indexContinue</nowiki></code> before calling <code><nowiki>index.routeRequest(Routes.index.name)</nowiki></code>. The route request causes the router to destroy and recreate the entire index route, which re-runs {{code|Component.onCompleted}} and selects the correct sub-component independently. The manual assignment is therefore redundant and executes a component instantiation that is immediately discarded.  
  
2. '''Redundant {{code|!isInGame}} guard in {{code|indexContinue}}.''' <code><nowiki>continueGame.visible: lastSave && !isInGame</nowiki></code> — the {{code|!isInGame}} condition is always {{code|true}} at the time {{code|indexContinue}} is loaded, since it is only selected when {{code|isInGame}} is {{code|false}}. The guard would only matter if {{code|isInGame}} changed after the component was loaded without triggering a route reload, which does not happen in normal flow.  
  
3. '''{{code|roguePointCTA}} uses hardcoded {{code|topMargin: -50}}.''' The top margin is not scaled by {{code|Theme.heightScale}}, unlike every other margin in the file. This intentionally positions the banner partially off-screen at the top edge, but the offset will be visually inconsistent at non-1080p resolutions.  
  
4. '''Both dimensions of {{code|roguePointCTA}} use {{code|Theme.widthScale}}.''' The {{code|height}} property uses {{code|Theme.widthScale}} instead of {{code|Theme.heightScale}}, causing the banner to scale uniformly with screen width rather than independently in each axis. This matches the pattern in {{code|routeview-unavailable.qml}} and appears intentional for aspect-ratio preservation.  
  
5. '''{{code|closeButton}} {{code|MouseArea}} has no hover feedback.''' Unlike the parent {{code|hoverArea}}, the close button's {{code|MouseArea}} does not set {{code|hoverEnabled}}, does not change the cursor, and does not animate opacity on hover.  
  
6. '''{{code|indexInGame}} Save Game button has no {{code|objectName}}.''' The Quick Save button has <code><nowiki>objectName: "ui:btn:action[apply]"</nowiki></code> and the Disconnect button has <code><nowiki>objectName: "ui:btn:action[discard]"</nowiki></code>, but the Save Game button has no {{code|objectName}}. This means {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements()}} will not register it as an action button, so it cannot be triggered by the controller Enter key via the action button system — only by direct mouse click or keyboard focus.

===routeview-input.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-input.qml}} is the '''Mouse & Controller Input Options''' page. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|input}} route at path {{code|options.input}}, rank 3.2. It is accessible via the '''Input''' tab in {{code|optionsNavModel}} inside {{code|component-header.qml}}, with tab label <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.menus.input</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Menu_Options_Input}}). The tab is always enabled ({{code|isEnabled: true}}).  
  
The file is 331 lines. The outer structure is a {{code|RouteView}} containing a single {{code|Item}} (id: {{code|container}}). A second {{code|OptionsList}} (id: {{code|controllerOptions}}) that was originally intended to sit below the first is entirely commented out.  
  
====container (Item)====  
  
The {{code|container}} item is centered in the parent via {{code|anchors.centerIn: parent}} with a {{code|verticalCenterOffset}} of <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(20 * Theme.heightScale)</nowiki></code>. Its dimensions are derived from the active {{code|OptionsList}}:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|width}}  
| <code><nowiki>inputOptions.width</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| {{code|height}}  
| <code><nowiki>inputOptions.height + Math.ceil(20 * Theme.heightScale)</nowiki></code>  
|}  
  
A commented-out version of the height property reads <code><nowiki>//height: inputOptions.height + Math.ceil(20 * Theme.heightScale) + controllerOptions.height</nowiki></code>, showing the original intent to include the second (now fully commented-out) {{code|OptionsList}}.  
  
====inputOptions (OptionsList)====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|title}}  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.headers.inputOptions</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Header_Options_Input}})  
|-  
| {{code|visibleRows}}  
| {{code|6}}  
|}  
  
=====getComplex / setComplex=====  
  
{{code|inputOptions}} defines a {{code|getComplex}} and {{code|setComplex}} object, each with a single function: {{code|controller_horizontalSensitivity}}.  
  
The getter reads {{code|in_look_yaw_sensitivity}} via {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getConsoleVariableAsFloat}} and returns its negated value as a string. The setter writes <code><nowiki>-Number(value)</nowiki></code> back to {{code|in_look_yaw_sensitivity}}. This negation is required because the engine stores the horizontal look sensitivity as a negative float for non-inverted look.  
  
'''Note:''' No model item in the active {{code|mouseModel}} uses the key {{code|controller_horizontalSensitivity}}. The active controller sensitivity items use direct {{code|cvar}} bindings instead. The {{code|getComplex}}/{{code|setComplex}} functions are therefore dead code in the current state of the file.  
  
=====mouseModel (ListModel)=====  
  
The model is populated in {{code|Component.onCompleted}}. Items are appended in the following order:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! # !! Key !! Type !! CVar !! Range / Options !! L10n label key  
|-  
| — || (separator) || {{code|separator}} || — || — || <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.headers.mouseOptions</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Header_Options_Input_Mouse}})  
|-  
| 1 || {{code|mouse_invert}} || {{code|select}} || {{command|m_pitch}} || Off: {{code|"0.022"}}, On: {{code|"-0.022"}} || {{code|#QTUI_Option_Mouse_Invert}}  
|-  
| 2 || {{code|mouse_raw}} || {{code|select}} || {{command|m_rawinput}} || Off: {{code|"0"}}, On: {{code|"1"}} || {{code|#QTUI_Option_Mouse_RawInput}}  
|-  
| 3 || {{code|mouse_accelerationswitch}} || {{code|select}} || {{command|m_customaccel}} || Off: {{code|"0"}}, On: {{code|"3"}} || {{code|#QTUI_Option_Mouse_AccelerationSwitch}}  
|-  
| 4 || {{code|mouse_sensitivity}} || {{code|slider}} || {{command|sensitivity}} || 0.1 – 6.0, step 0.1 || {{code|#QTUI_Option_Mouse_Sensitivity}}  
|-  
| 5 || {{code|mouse_acceleration}} || {{code|slider}} || {{command|m_customaccel_exponent}} || 1.0 – 1.4, step 0.1 || {{code|#QTUI_Option_Mouse_Acceleration}}  
|-  
| 6 || {{code|mouse_zoomAcceleration}} || {{code|slider}} || {{command|zoom_sensitivity_ratio}} || 0.0 – 2.0, step 0.1 || {{code|#QTUI_Option_Mouse_ZoomAcceleration}}  
|-  
| — || (separator) || {{code|separator}} || — || — || <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.headers.controllerOptions</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Header_Options_Input_Controller}})  
|-  
| 7 || {{code|controller_lookVerticalSensitivity}} || {{code|slider}} || {{command|in_joy_look_pitch_sensitivity}} || 0.0 – 2.0, step 0.01 || {{code|#QTUI_Option_Controller_LookVerticalSensitivity}}  
|-  
| 8 || {{code|controller_lookHorizontalSensitivity}} || {{code|slider}} || {{command|in_joy_look_yaw_sensitivity}} || 0.0 – 2.0, step 0.01 || {{code|#QTUI_Option_Controller_LookHorizontalSensitivity}}  
|-  
| 9 || {{code|controller_gyroVerticalSensitivity}} || {{code|slider}} || {{command|in_gyro_look_pitch_sensitivity}} || 0.0 – 2.0, step 0.01 || {{code|#QTUI_Option_Controller_GyroVerticalSensitivity}}  
|-  
| 10 || {{code|controller_gyroHorizontalSensitivity}} || {{code|slider}} || {{command|in_gyro_look_yaw_sensitivity}} || 0.0 – 2.0, step 0.01 || {{code|#QTUI_Option_Controller_GyroHorizontalSensitivity}}  
|}  
  
All sliders use {{code|valueType: "real"}}, {{code|precision: 2}}, and {{code|formatter: "decimal"}}.  
  
Two items are commented out inside the model block: a {{code|controller_rumble}} select ({{command|cl_rumblescale}}) and a {{code|controller_invert}} select ({{command|joy_inverty}}), both with Off/On options.  
  
====Commented-out controllerOptions (OptionsList)====  
  
The entire second {{code|OptionsList}} (id: {{code|controllerOptions}}) is wrapped in a block comment. It was designed to sit below {{code|inputOptions}} with {{code|visibleRows: 4}} and {{code|title: L10n.strings.headers.controllerOptions}}.  
  
It contained:  
  
* A {{code|setControllerState()}} function that disabled or enabled the {{code|controller_invert}}, {{code|controller_horizontalSensitivity}}, and {{code|controller_verticalSensitivity}} rows based on whether {{code|controller_enabled.value === "0"}}.  
* {{code|onSettingsReset}} and {{code|onRequestSettingsChange}} handlers that called {{code|setControllerState()}} to keep the disabled state in sync.  
* Its own {{code|getComplex}}/{{code|setComplex}} for {{code|controller_horizontalSensitivity}}, using the CVar {{code|joy_yawsensitivity}} (distinct from the {{code|in_look_yaw_sensitivity}} used in the active {{code|inputOptions}}).  
* A {{code|controllerModel}} with four items:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Key !! Type !! CVar !! Range / Options  
|-  
| {{code|controller_enabled}} || {{code|select}} || {{command|joystick}} || No: {{code|"0"}}, Yes: {{code|"1"}}  
|-  
| {{code|controller_invert}} || {{code|select}} || {{command|joy_inverty}} || No: {{code|"0"}}, Yes: {{code|"1"}}  
|-  
| {{code|controller_horizontalSensitivity}} || {{code|slider}} (complex) || {{command|joy_yawsensitivity}} (negated) || 0.5 – 7.0, step 0.001  
|-  
| {{code|controller_verticalSensitivity}} || {{code|slider}} || {{command|joy_pitchsensitivity}} || 0.5 – 7.0, step 0.001  
|}  
  
====Notable Issues====  
  
1. '''Dead {{code|getComplex}}/{{code|setComplex}} on {{code|inputOptions}}.''' The active {{code|inputOptions}} defines {{code|getComplex.controller_horizontalSensitivity}} and {{code|setComplex.controller_horizontalSensitivity}}, but no item in {{code|mouseModel}} uses the key {{code|controller_horizontalSensitivity}}. The controller sensitivity items in the active model (items 8–10) use direct {{code|cvar}} bindings. These complex getter/setter functions are never called.  
  
2. '''CVar mismatch between active and commented-out code.''' The active {{code|getComplex}}/{{code|setComplex}} targets {{code|in_look_yaw_sensitivity}}, while the commented-out {{code|controllerOptions}} targeted {{code|joy_yawsensitivity}}. These are different CVars. If the commented-out section were re-enabled without reconciling this, the two panels would read and write different CVars for the same conceptual setting.  
  
3. '''{{code|mouse_invert}} {{code|valueType}} mismatch.''' The {{code|mouse_invert}} select item declares {{code|valueType: "int"}}, but its option values are the float strings {{code|"0.022"}} and {{code|"-0.022"}}. The {{code|m_pitch}} CVar is a float. The {{code|valueType}} should be {{code|"real"}}.  
  
4. '''Narrow {{code|mouse_acceleration}} range.''' The {{code|mouse_acceleration}} slider ({{code|m_customaccel_exponent}}) spans 1.0 – 1.4 with a step of 0.1, yielding only five discrete positions. The exponent range for {{code|m_customaccel}} mode 3 is typically 1.0 – 1.4, so the range is technically correct, but the coarse step size means the slider has very limited granularity.  
  
5. '''Redundant {{code|anchors.verticalCenterOffset: 0}} on {{code|inputOptions}}.''' The {{code|inputOptions}} item sets {{code|anchors.verticalCenterOffset: 0}}, which is the default value and has no effect.  
  
6. '''Verbose step values.''' The controller sensitivity sliders use {{code|stepValue: 0.010000}} (six decimal places). This is functionally identical to {{code|0.01}}.

===routeview-join.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-join.qml}} is the '''Join Game''' page, the first sub-route of the '''Multiplayer''' section. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|join}} route at path {{code|multiplayer.join}}, rank 2.1. The parent {{code|multiplayer}} route is {{code|passthrough: true}} with {{code|destination: "join"}}, so navigating to Multiplayer always lands here first.  
  
====Navigation Entry Point====  
  
{{code|component-header.qml}} defines {{code|multiplayerNavModel}}, which appends two sub-nav tabs on {{code|Component.onCompleted}}:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Label (L10n key)  
! {{code|routeName}}  
! {{code|isEnabled}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.menus.join</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Menu_Multiplayer_Join}})  
| {{code|join}}  
| {{code|true}}  
|-  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.menus.host</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Menu_Multiplayer_Host}})  
| {{code|host}}  
| {{code|true}}  
|}  
  
As a side effect of {{code|multiplayerNavModel}} initialization, {{code|component-header.qml}} also executes <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.executeClientCommandUnrestricted("exec server.cfg;")</nowiki></code> to pre-load server configuration before the Host page is visited.  
  
====Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Value / Purpose  
|-  
| {{code|total}}  
| {{code|int}}  
| Declared but never read or written anywhere in the file. Dead property.  
|-  
| {{code|cellH}}  
| {{code|int}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(200 * Theme.heightScale)</nowiki></code> — height of each server row in the list.  
|-

| {{code|int}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(32 * Theme.heightScale)</nowiki></code> — base font size used for server info text.

| {{code|int}}  
| State machine variable: {{code|0}} = before search, {{code|1}} = searching LAN, {{code|2}} = searching Internet.  
|-  
| {{code|searchTextState}}  
| {{code|string}}  
| Status message displayed above the list. Initialized to {{code|#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_SearchingServersLocal}}.

| {{code|variant}}

|}  
  
A private {{code|QtObject}} (id: {{code|d}}) holds one property, {{code|queryHandle}} (string), which stores the handle returned by the server list request calls. It is written but never read.  
  
====Server Discovery Sequence====  
  
=====deferredCompletedTimer=====  
  
A one-shot {{code|Timer}} with a 1250 ms delay that fires automatically on page load ({{code|running: true}}). Its {{code|onTriggered}} handler:  
  
# Calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.listSaveEntries()</nowiki></code> and discards the result (dead code).  
# Calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.requestLANServerList()</nowiki></code>, stores the handle in {{code|d.queryHandle}}, and sets {{code|stepServerList [[=]] 1}}.  
  
The 1250 ms delay defers the server query until after the route transition animation has completed.  
  
=====Connections (BlackMesaEngine signals)=====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Signal  
! Handler behaviour  
|-  
| {{code|onMatchmakingServerResponded}}  
| Checks that <code><nowiki>serverEntry.steamApplicationId === BlackMesaEngine.getSteamApplicationId()</nowiki></code> and <code><nowiki>serverEntry.gameDirectory === "bms"</nowiki></code>. Sets {{code|serverEntry.lan}} to {{code|1}} if currently in LAN phase, {{code|0}} otherwise. Filters out any entry whose {{code|name}} or {{code|gameDescription}} matches {{code|fastpathServerName}}. Appends surviving entries to {{code|serverList}}.  
|-  
| {{code|onMatchmakingQueryCompleted}}  
| If {{code|stepServerList === 1}} (LAN done): updates {{code|searchTextState}} to {{code|#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_SearchingServersInternet}}, calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.requestInternetServerList()</nowiki></code>, sets {{code|stepServerList = 2}}. If {{code|stepServerList === 2}} (Internet done): sets {{code|searchTextState}} to {{code|#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_NoServersFound}} or <code><nowiki>#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_ServersFoundCount.arg(serverList.count)</nowiki></code>, then calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.clearUIElementsData()}}, {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements(appRoot)}}, and {{code|BlackMesaUtils.afterTraverseCallback()}} to register the newly populated list rows with the keyboard/controller navigation system.  
|}  
  
====Layout====  
  
The page uses a two-row, one-column {{code|GridLayout}} with 16 px margins filling the entire {{code|RouteView}}.  
  
=====Row 1 — Status Bar=====  
  
An {{code|Item}} of fixed height <code><nowiki>40 * Theme.heightScale</nowiki></code> containing:  
  
* {{code|serverSearchingState}} ({{code|Text}}): displays {{code|searchTextState}} in bold, {{code|Theme.colors.headerText}}, at {{code|serverDescriptionMainTextSize * 0.6}}.  
* A spinner {{code|Image}} ({{code|Rolling-1s-200px.png}}), 32×32 px (scaled by {{code|heightScale}}), anchored to the right of the status text. It is {{code|visible}} only when <code><nowiki>stepServerList !== 2</nowiki></code> (i.e. hidden once the Internet search completes). A {{code|RotationAnimation}} spins it continuously from 0° to 360° over 500 ms.  
  
=====Row 2 — Server List=====  
  
A {{code|Rectangle}} (id: {{code|serverListContainer}}) with a 2 px white border and a near-transparent background ({{code|#10000000}}), filling the remaining height.  
  
Inside it, a {{code|GridView}} (id: {{code|grid}}) with:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|cellWidth}}  
| {{code|parent.width}} — full width, single column  
|-  
| {{code|cellHeight}}  
| {{code|cellH}} (200 px scaled)  
|-  
| {{code|cacheBuffer}}  
| {{code|8000}}  
|-  
| {{code|boundsBehavior}}  
| {{code|Flickable.StopAtBounds}}  
|-  
| {{code|ScrollBar.vertical}}  
| {{code|VerticalScrollBar}} (always visible)  
|-  
| {{code|model}}  
| {{code|ListModel}} (id: {{code|serverList}}), populated dynamically by {{code|onMatchmakingServerResponded}}  
|}  
  
======Server Row Delegate======  
  
Each row is a {{code|MouseArea}} with:  
  
* {{code|objectName: "ui:option-item"}} — registers the row with {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements()}} as a navigable option item.  
* {{code|type: "join"}} — a custom type value not used by the navigation system.  
* Two states: {{code|blurred}} (no border, {{code|#40000000}} background) and {{code|focused}} (2 px {{code|Theme.colors.highlight}} border, same background).  
* {{code|onEntered}}: sets state to {{code|"focused"}}, plays {{code|Theme.sounds.buttonFocused}}.  
* {{code|onExited}}: sets state to {{code|"blurred"}}.  
* {{code|onDoubleClicked}}: executes <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.executeClientCommandUnrestricted("disconnect; connect " + connectionAddressString)</nowiki></code>.  
* A {{code|callback}} property holding the same connect command as a function, intended to be invoked by the keyboard/controller navigation system when Enter is pressed on a focused row.  
  
The inner layout is a {{code|GridLayout}} with four columns:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Column  
! Width  
! Contents  
|-  
| Map thumbnail  
| {{code|cellH}} (square)  
| {{code|Rectangle}} ({{code|#111111}} bg) containing an async {{code|Image}} loaded from <code><nowiki>materials/vgui/multiplayer/map_backgrounds/<map>_0_widescreen_16_10.vtf</nowiki></code>, and a {{code|Text}} label below it showing the map name with the {{code|dm_}} prefix stripped.  
|-  
| Player count  
| 96 px fixed  
| Three {{code|Text}} elements showing <code><nowiki>playersOnServerCurrently / playersOnServerMaximum</nowiki></code> (current in {{code|headerText}}, separator and max in {{code|highlight}}), plus a {{code|serverNetworkType}} label at the bottom showing {{code|#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_ServerTypeLocal}} or {{code|#BlackMesaUI_Multiplayer_ServerTypeInternet}} in {{code|highlight}} color.  
|-  
| Server name  
| Fill remaining width  
| {{code|Text}} (id: {{code|serverName}}) showing {{code|name.trim()}} in bold, {{code|headerText}} color, with {{code|elide: Text.ElideRight}}.  
|-  
| Ping  
| 64 px fixed  
| {{code|Text}} (id: {{code|serverPing}}) showing {{code|ping}} in regular weight, {{code|headerText}} color (overridden to {{code|headerText}} in both states — the state change has no visible effect on this element).  
|}  
  
====Notable Issues====  
1. '''{{code|total}} is a dead property.''' It is declared as {{code|property int total}} but is never assigned or read anywhere in the file.  

2. '''Dead engine call in the timer.''' <code><nowiki>var listSaveEntries = BlackMesaEngine.listSaveEntries()</nowiki></code> is called inside {{code|deferredCompletedTimer.onTriggered}} and the result is immediately discarded. This makes an unnecessary engine call on every page load.  

3. '''{{code|d.queryHandle}} is write-only.''' The handle returned by {{code|requestLANServerList()}} and {{code|requestInternetServerList()}} is stored in {{code|d.queryHandle}} but never read. There is no mechanism to cancel an in-progress query when the user navigates away from the page, which may cause {{code|onMatchmakingServerResponded}} to fire and attempt to append to {{code|serverList}} after the route has been destroyed.  

4. '''{{code|type: "join"}} has no effect.''' {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements()}} classifies items with {{code|objectName: "ui:option-item"}} as option items regardless of their {{code|type}} property. The {{code|type: "join"}} value is unique to this file and is not handled by any navigation logic.  

5. '''No single-click handler.''' The delegate only handles {{code|onDoubleClicked}} for connecting. Single-click focuses the row visually. The {{code|callback}} property provides the Enter-key connect action via the navigation system, but there is no explicit {{code|onClicked}} handler, making mouse single-click a no-op.

===routeview-loadgame.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-loadgame.qml}} is the '''Load Game''' page. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|loadgame}} route at path {{code|campaign.loadgame}}, rank {{code|1.2}}.  
  
The file is 70 lines and contains one property, one {{code|Carousel}}, and one {{code|Alert}}.  
  
====Route Registration and Activation====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|name}}  
| {{code|loadgame}}  
|-  
| {{code|path}}  
| {{code|campaign.loadgame}}  
|-  
| {{code|rank}}  
| {{code|1.2}}  
|-  
| {{code|alert}}  
| (not set — standard replace transition)  
|}  
  
The Load Game tab is the second entry in {{code|campaignNavModel}} inside {{code|component-header.qml}}, with {{code|isEnabled}} initialized to {{code|false}}. It is enabled at runtime by {{code|checkButtonsActive()}} only when <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.getSaveGames().length > 0 && !appRoot.isInMultiplayerMode</nowiki></code>. If there are no saved games, or the player is in multiplayer mode, the tab remains disabled and the route is inaccessible from the header. The tab label is <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.menus.loadgame</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Menu_Campaign_LoadGame}}).  
  
Because {{code|loadgame}} has rank {{code|1.2}}, navigating to it from {{code|newgame}} (rank {{code|1.1}}) uses the left-enter transition (slides in from the right); navigating from {{code|savegame}} (rank {{code|1.3}}) uses the right-enter transition (slides in from the left).  
  
====selectedModel Property====  
  
A single {{code|variant}} property (id: {{code|loadGame}}) that stores the model object of the save entry currently pending deletion. It is set in {{code|onSelectedSecondary}} and cleared after the delete confirmation is handled.  
  
====loadGameCarousel====  
  
A {{code|Carousel}} (id: {{code|loadGameCarousel}}) using {{code|CarouselLoadGameItem}} as its {{code|itemComponent}}.  
  
=====Model Population=====  
  
{{code|Component.onCompleted}} calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.getSaveGames()</nowiki></code> and appends every returned entry to {{code|levels}} without any filtering. This means '''all save types''' — manual, autosave, and quicksave — are shown, sorted by date descending (the sort is performed inside {{code|BlackMesaUtils.getSaveGames()}}). This differs from {{code|routeview-savegame.qml}}, which filters out autosaves and quicksaves.  
  
Each appended model object has the following fields:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Field  
! Source  
|-  
| {{code|savename}}  
| <code><nowiki>save.savename</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| {{code|chapter}}  
| <code><nowiki>save.chapter</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| {{code|chapterName}}  
| <code><nowiki>save.chapterName</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| {{code|date}}  
| <code><nowiki>save.dateCreated</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| {{code|thumbnailUrl}}  
| <code><nowiki>save.thumbnail</nowiki></code>  
|-  
| {{code|saveType}}  
| <code><nowiki>save.type</nowiki></code> ({{code|"manual"}}, {{code|"autosave"}}, or {{code|"quick"}})  
|-  
| {{code|buttonText}}  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.buttons.resume</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Buttons_Resume}}) — same for all entries  
|}  
  
=====CarouselLoadGameItem Delegate=====  
  
The {{code|itemComponent}} passes the following to each {{code|CarouselLoadGameItem}}:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|buttonText}}  
| <code><nowiki>model.buttonText</nowiki></code> (always {{code|#QTUI_Buttons_Resume}})  
|-

|-  
| {{code|saveType}}

|-

| {{code|true}} — always shown for every card (unlike {{code|routeview-savegame.qml}} where it is conditional on {{code|!!model.savename}})  
|-  
| {{code|buttonTextSecondary}}  
| <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString("#GameUI_Delete")</nowiki></code>  
|}  
  
{{code|CarouselLoadGameItem}} ({{code|component-carousel-item-loadgame.qml}}) renders three text lines in a {{code|Column}}: the chapter name ({{code|Theme.colors.headerText}}, 26px), the formatted date ({{code|Theme.colors.text}}, 16px), and the save type label in all-caps ({{code|L10n.strings.labels.autoSave}}/{{code|quickSave}}/{{code|manualSave}}; bold font for manual saves; {{code|Theme.colors.dimmedText}} for autosaves, {{code|Theme.colors.text}} otherwise).  
  
=====Selection Handlers=====  
Both {{code|onSelected}} and {{code|onSelectedWithDoubleClick}} are identical: they immediately fire <code><nowiki>loadGame.loadGameRequest(model)</nowiki></code> with no intermediate confirmation dialog. There is no overwrite or confirmation step — selecting a save loads it instantly.  
  
{{code|onSelectedSecondary}} sets <code><nowiki>loadGame.selectedModel = model</nowiki></code> and calls {{code|confirmDeleteAlert.show()}}.  
  
====loadGameRequest Signal Chain====  
Passing the full model object to {{code|loadGameRequest}} triggers the following chain:  
1. <code><nowiki>loadGame.loadGameRequest(model)</nowiki></code> fires the signal declared in the {{code|RouteView}} base class ({{code|routeview.qml}}): <code><nowiki>signal loadGameRequest(variant savegame)</nowiki></code>.  
2. {{code|router.qml}} has connected this to {{code|_loadGameRequestHandler(savegame)}}, which re-emits <code><nowiki>outlet.loadGameRequest(savegame)</nowiki></code>.  
3. {{code|main.qml}} handles {{code|onLoadGameRequest}}: sets <code><nowiki>footer.levelName = savegame.chapterName</nowiki></code> (so the loading screen shows the correct chapter name), then calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.loadGame(savegame.savename)</nowiki></code>.  
4. {{code|BlackMesaUtils.loadGame(filename)}} issues the engine command <code><nowiki>disconnect;wait;wait;sv_cheats 0; maxplayers 1; load <filename></nowiki></code>.  
  
====confirmDeleteAlert====  
  
An {{code|Alert}} (id: {{code|confirmDeleteAlert}}) shown when the user clicks the secondary Delete button on any save card.  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|titleText}}  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.headers.deleteSaveConfirmation</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Header_DeleteSaveConfirmation}})  
|-  
| {{code|messageText}}  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.modalMessages.deleteSaveConfirmation</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Messages_DeleteSaveConfirmation}})  
|}  
  
=====Buttons=====  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Button type  
! {{code|objectName}}  
! Label  
! Action  
|-  
| {{code|WarningButton}}  
| <code><nowiki>ui:btn:action[apply]</nowiki></code>  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.buttons.deleteSave</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Buttons_DeleteSave}})  
| If <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.removeSaveEntry(selectedModel.savename)</nowiki></code> returns {{code|true}}, clears {{code|loadGameCarousel.levels}} and attempts to repopulate via <code><nowiki>loadGameCarousel.levels.Component.completed()</nowiki></code>. Unconditionally nulls {{code|selectedModel}} and hides the alert regardless of whether the delete succeeded.  
|-  
| {{code|DefaultButton}}  
| <code><nowiki>ui:btn:action[cancel]</nowiki></code>  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.buttons.cancel</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Buttons_Cancel}})  
| Hides the alert.  
|}  
  
====Comparison with routeview-savegame.qml====  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Behaviour  
! {{code|routeview-loadgame.qml}}  
! {{code|routeview-savegame.qml}}  
|-  
| Save types shown  
| All (manual, autosave, quicksave)  
| Manual only  
|-  
| "New Save" slot  
| No  
| Yes (first card)  
|-  
| Primary action  
| Immediate load, no confirmation  
| Overwrite confirmation dialog  
|-  
| Secondary button visibility  
| Always {{code|true}}  
| Only when {{code|!!model.savename}}  
|-  
| Number of Alert dialogs  
| 1 (delete only)  
| 2 (overwrite + delete)  
|}  
  
====Notable Issues====  
1. '''{{code|loadGameCarousel.levels.Component.completed()}} is not valid QML.''' {{code|Component.completed}} is a signal, not a callable function. Calling it directly does not re-run the {{code|Component.onCompleted}} handler. After a successful delete, the carousel model is cleared but never repopulated, leaving the user with an empty save list until they navigate away and back.  
  
2. '''{{code|selectedModel}} is nulled unconditionally.''' Even if {{code|BlackMesaEngine.removeSaveEntry()}} returns {{code|false}} (delete failed), {{code|selectedModel}} is still set to {{code|null}} and the alert is hidden. The carousel is only cleared on success, but the UI state is reset regardless of the outcome.  
  
3. '''Secondary button label bypasses {{code|L10n}}.''' The Delete button uses <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString("#GameUI_Delete")</nowiki></code> directly instead of the {{code|L10n}} singleton, inconsistent with all other button labels in the file.  
  
4. '''{{code|onSelected}} and {{code|onSelectedWithDoubleClick}} are identical.''' Single-click and double-click produce the same result with no distinction in behavior.

===routeview-newgame.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-newgame.qml}} is the '''New Game page''', registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|newgame}} route at path {{code|campaign.newgame}}, rank 1.1. The parent {{code|campaign}} route (rank 1.0) is a {{code|passthrough: true}} route with {{code|destination: "newgame"}}, meaning navigating to {{code|campaign}} automatically redirects here.  
  
The file is 92 lines and contains a single {{code|Carousel}} component with two delegate definitions and two-phase selection logic.  
  
====Route Registration====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|name}}  
| {{code|newgame}}  
|-  
| {{code|path}}  
| {{code|campaign.newgame}}  
|-  
| {{code|rank}}  
| {{code|1.1}}  
|-  
| {{code|qmlFile}}  
| {{code|routeview-newgame.qml}}  
|}  
  
The sub-nav tab that triggers this route is defined in {{code|component-header.qml}} and labeled with <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.menus.newgame</nowiki></code> (engine key: {{code|#QTUI_Menu_Campaign_NewGame}}).  
  
====Two-Phase Selection====  
  
The page operates in two sequential phases controlled by the {{code|currentlySelectedCampaign}} property on the {{code|Carousel}}:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Phase  
! Condition  
! Active delegate  
! Items shown  
|-  
| Campaign selection  
| <code><nowiki>currentlySelectedCampaign === null</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|campaignDelegate}}  
| One card per official campaign returned by {{code|BlackMesaEngine.listCampaignEntries()}}  
|-  
| Chapter selection  
| <code><nowiki>currentlySelectedCampaign !== null</nowiki></code>  
| {{code|chapterDelegate}}  
| One card per chapter in the selected campaign  
|}  
  
The active delegate is selected via <code><nowiki>itemComponent: currentlySelectedCampaign !== null ? chapterDelegate : campaignDelegate</nowiki></code>.  
  
====newGameCarousel Properties====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Type  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|campaignEntries}}  
| {{code|var}}  
| Result of <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.listCampaignEntries().filter(function (campaign) { return campaign.isOfficial })</nowiki></code> — only official campaigns are included.  
|-  
| {{code|currentlySelectedCampaign}}  
| {{code|var}}  
| Initially {{code|null}}. Set to a campaign object when the user selects a campaign card, triggering the switch to chapter selection.  
|}  
  
====Component.onCompleted====  
  
Iterates {{code|campaignEntries}}, sets {{code|levelName: ""}} on each entry (clearing any engine-provided level name that would be inappropriate at the campaign level), then appends each entry to {{code|levels}}.  
  
If {{code|levels.count [[=]][[=]][[=]] 1}} after population, {{code|currentlySelectedCampaign}} is immediately set to {{code|campaignEntries[0]}}, skipping the campaign selection phase entirely.  
  
Note: setting {{code|levelName: ""}} mutates the original objects in {{code|campaignEntries}} directly, since JavaScript objects are passed by reference.  
  
====onCurrentlySelectedCampaignChanged====  
  
Fires whenever {{code|currentlySelectedCampaign}} is assigned. It:  
  
1. Clears {{code|levels}} and repopulates it with the chapters of the newly selected campaign.  
2. Sets <code><nowiki>showLeft = false</nowiki></code> and <code><nowiki>showRight = levels.count > 4</nowiki></code> to reset carousel pagination.  
3. If {{code|levels.count [[=]][[=]][[=]] 1}}, immediately fires <code><nowiki>newGame.loadMapRequest(currentlySelectedCampaign.chapters[0].levelName, localizedTitle)</nowiki></code>, skipping chapter selection entirely.  
  
====campaignDelegate====  
  
A {{code|Component}} wrapping a {{code|CarouselNewGameItem}} used during the campaign selection phase.  
  
{| class="standard-table"

|-

====loadMapRequest Signal Chain====  
  
{{code|loadMapRequest(string filename, string mapname)}} is declared in the {{code|RouteView}} base class ({{code|routeview.qml}}). It is handled by {{code|router.qml}} (7 connection points), which ultimately calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.loadMap(filename)}}, issuing the engine command <code><nowiki>disconnect;wait;wait;sv_cheats 0; maxplayers 1; map {filename}</nowiki></code>.  
  
====CarouselNewGameItem====

|-

| <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString("#GameUI_Chapter")</nowiki></code>  
| Label prefix shown before the chapter number (e.g. "Chapter 3").  
|}  
  
Its {{code|textComponent}} is a {{code|Column}} with two {{code|Text}} elements:

| Regular, 24px (width-scaled)
