| Light, 15px (height-scaled)  
| {{code|Theme.colors.text}}  
|}  
  
====CarouselItem Base Class====  
  
{{code|CarouselItem}} ({{code|component-carousel-item.qml}}) provides the shared card layout used by all carousel delegates. Key behaviors:  
  
* {{code|objectName: "ui:carousel-item"}} — registered by {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements()}} as a navigable carousel item for controller/keyboard support.  
* {{code|isLocked}} — when {{code|true}}, hides the {{code|textComponent}} and shows a {{code|lock.png}} overlay on the thumbnail.  
* The primary action button is loaded dynamically via <code><nowiki>loader.setSource("button-" + buttonType + ".qml")</nowiki></code>. For {{code|CarouselNewGameItem}}, {{code|buttonType}} defaults to {{code|"cta"}}, loading {{code|button-cta.qml}}.  
* Three visual states: {{code|initializing}} (all hidden), {{code|blurred}} (thumbnail dimmed at 0.7 opacity, text at 0.7 opacity, no button), {{code|focused}} (thumbnail clear, text fully visible, button visible, background rectangle at 0.9 opacity). Entering {{code|focused}} plays {{code|Theme.sounds.buttonFocused}} via {{code|Sound.playEffect}}.  
* Thumbnail images load asynchronously and fade in over 250ms once ready.  
  
====Carousel Component====  
  
{{code|Carousel}} ({{code|component-carousel.qml}}) provides the horizontal scrolling container. Key properties:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Description  
|-  
| {{code|levels}}  
| The {{code|ListModel}} backing the {{code|ListView}}.  
|-  
| {{code|itemComponent}}  
| The {{code|Component}} used as the delegate.  
|-  
| {{code|showLeft}} / {{code|showRight}}  
| Control visibility of the left/right {{code|CarouselButton}} pagination arrows.  
|-  
| {{code|visibleCount}}  
| <code><nowiki>Math.floor(maskWidth / (itemWidth + itemSpacing))</nowiki></code> — typically 4 items at 1920×1080.  
|}  
  
{{code|changePagination(type)}} animates {{code|contentX}} of the internal {{code|ListView}} by {{code|visibleCount}} items over 100ms with {{code|InOutQuint}} easing. After the animation completes, it calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaUtils.parseTraversalTree(appRoot)</nowiki></code> and {{code|BlackMesaUtils.updateCurrentUISelection()}} to re-register the newly visible carousel items with the navigation system.  
  
====Notable Issues====  
1. '''Chapter locking disabled.''' The {{code|chapterDelegate}} has a commented-out line:  
<syntaxhighlight lang="qml">// isLocked: (index + 1) > newGameCarousel.officialChaptersUnlockedCount && newGameCarousel.currentlySelectedCampaign.isOfficial</syntaxhighlight>  
The supporting property is also commented out:  
<syntaxhighlight lang="qml">// property int officialChaptersUnlockedCount: BlackMesaEngine.getConsoleVariableAsInt("sv_unlockedchapters")</syntaxhighlight>  
As a result, {{code|isLocked}} is hardcoded to {{code|false}} for all chapters, and the [[sv_unlockedchapters]] console variable has no effect on the UI.  
  
2. '''{{code|type}} label bypasses {{code|L10n}}.''' Both delegates use {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString()}} directly for the {{code|type}} label ({{code|#GameUI_NewGame}} and {{code|#GameUI_Chapter}}) instead of routing through the {{code|L10n}} singleton, inconsistent with the rest of the UI.  
  
3. '''{{code|onSelectedWithDoubleClick}} is identical to {{code|onSelected}}.''' Both delegates call {{code|selected()}} on double-click, making double-click and single-click indistinguishable.  
  
4. '''Auto-skip can bypass the page entirely.''' If {{code|listCampaignEntries()}} returns exactly one official campaign with exactly one chapter, the page will immediately fire {{code|loadMapRequest}} in {{code|Component.onCompleted}} without displaying any carousel to the user.

====Layout====  
  
A full-size {{code|Item}} containing two children: a background {{code|Rectangle}} and a {{code|ListView}}.  
  
=====Background=====  
  
A {{code|Rectangle}} filling the parent with <code><nowiki>color: Theme.colors.subNavBackground</nowiki></code> ({{code|#111111}}) and <code><nowiki>opacity: Theme.opacity.subNav</nowiki></code> ({{code|0.5}}). This is the same background style used by the sub-navigation bar itself.

{{code|Keys.onUpPressed}} and {{code|Keys.onDownPressed}} call {{code|scrollBar.decrease()}} and {{code|scrollBar.increase()}} directly, bypassing {{code|ListView}} item navigation — the same pattern used in {{code|routeview-translators.qml}}.  
  
======Model======  
  
A single-item {{code|ListModel}} that appends <code><nowiki>{ _text: policyText }</nowiki></code> in its {{code|Component.onCompleted}}. The entire privacy policy is rendered as one {{code|Text}} delegate.  
  
======Delegate (Text, id: policyTextContainer)======

====Notable Issues====  
1. '''Broken padding syntax.''' The delegate sets {{code|padding}} using a JavaScript block expression:  
<syntaxhighlight lang="qml">padding: {  
    left: Math.ceil(16 * Theme.widthScale);  
    top: Math.ceil(16 * Theme.heightScale);  
    bottom: Math.ceil(16 * Theme.heightScale);  
    right: Math.ceil(24 * Theme.widthScale);  
}</syntaxhighlight>  

2. In QML, this is not a property group assignment — it is a JavaScript block with labeled statements. The value of the block is the last expression, so {{code|padding}} (all four sides) is set to <code><nowiki>Math.ceil(24 * Theme.widthScale)</nowiki></code>. The {{code|left}}, {{code|top}}, and {{code|bottom}} lines are no-ops. The correct properties are {{code|leftPadding}}, {{code|topPadding}}, {{code|bottomPadding}}, and {{code|rightPadding}}.  
  
3. '''Wrong {{code|wrapMode}} enum type.''' The delegate uses <code><nowiki>wrapMode: TextInput.WordWrap</nowiki></code> instead of <code><nowiki>wrapMode: Text.WordWrap</nowiki></code>. Both resolve to the same integer value ({{code|3}}) at runtime, so wrapping works correctly, but the reference to {{code|TextInput}} is semantically incorrect for a {{code|Text}} element.  
  
4. '''Hardcoded color.''' The text color is {{code|"#EEEEEE"}} rather than {{code|Theme.colors.text}} ({{code|"white"}}), making it inconsistent with the rest of the UI and not updatable via the theme system.  
  
5. '''Hardcoded, non-localized content.''' The entire privacy policy is embedded as a string literal in the QML source. It is English-only, not routed through the {{code|L10n}} singleton or the engine localization system, and cannot be updated without a code change.  

6. '''Single-item {{code|ListView}}.''' Using a {{code|ListView}} with a one-item {{code|ListModel}} to render a single {{code|Text}} element is unnecessarily complex. A plain {{code|Flickable}} or {{code|ScrollView}} wrapping a {{code|Text}} directly would be simpler and more idiomatic.

===routeview-quit.qml===  
{{code|routeview-quit.qml}} is the '''Quit Game confirmation dialog'''. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|quit}} route with {{code|alert: true}}, meaning the router overlays it on top of the current view rather than replacing it. Contains one property and one {{code|Alert}}.  
  
====quotes property====  
  
An immediately-invoked function expression (IIFE) that runs at construction time. It iterates over all keys of <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.quit</nowiki></code> and collects their values into a plain JavaScript array. {{code|L10n.strings.quit}} contains 6 localized strings keyed {{code|quote01}} through {{code|quote06}}, corresponding to engine localization keys <code><nowiki>#QTUI_Quit_Quote01</nowiki></code> through <code><nowiki>#QTUI_Quit_Quote06</nowiki></code>.  
  
====alert====  
  
A single {{code|Alert}} component (id: {{code|alert}}) with the following properties:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|skrim}}  
| {{code|false}} — no darkening overlay is drawn behind the dialog, unlike most other alerts in the UI.  
|-  
| {{code|titleText}}  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.headers.quit</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Header_Quit}})  
|-  
| {{code|messageText}}  
| A randomly selected entry from the {{code|quotes}} array, chosen once at construction time via <code><nowiki>quotes[Math.floor(Math.random() * quotes.length)]</nowiki></code>.  
|}  
  
=====Buttons=====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Button type  
! {{code|objectName}}  
! Label  
! Action  
|-  
| {{code|CTAButton}}  
| <code><nowiki>ui:btn:action[apply]</nowiki></code>  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.buttons.quit</nowiki></code>  
| Calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.executeClientCommandUnrestricted("quit")</nowiki></code> to quit the game.  
|-  
| {{code|DefaultButton}}  
| <code><nowiki>ui:btn:action[cancel]</nowiki></code>  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.buttons.cancel</nowiki></code>  
| Calls <code><nowiki>quitView.routeRequest(Routes.back.name)</nowiki></code> to navigate back. {{code|Routes.back.name}} resolves to the string {{code|"back"}}, the special route that tells the router to pop the current view.  
|}

'''Routes.back.name''' resolves to the string "back", the special route that tells the router to pop the current view.
  
=====Auto-show=====  
{{code|Component.onCompleted}} immediately calls {{code|alert.show()}}, so the dialog appears as soon as the route is loaded with no additional trigger required.  

===routeview-savebeforequit.qml===  
  
{{code|routeview-savebeforequit.qml}} is the '''Save Before Quit confirmation dialog'''. It is registered in {{code|routes.qml}} as the {{code|savebeforequit}} route with {{code|alert: true}} and no rank, meaning the router pushes it onto the stack using the upward slide-in transition rather than replacing the current route.  
  
The file is 43 lines and contains a single {{code|Alert}} with no additional properties on the {{code|RouteView}} itself.  
  
====Route Registration====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|name}}  
| {{code|savebeforequit}}  
|-  
| {{code|path}}  
| {{code|savebeforequit}}  
|-  
| {{code|alert}}  
| {{code|true}}  
|-  
| {{code|qmlFile}}  
| {{code|routeview-savebeforequit.qml}}  
|}  
  
Because {{code|alert}} is {{code|true}}, {{code|router.qml}} uses the {{code|upEnterTransition}} (slides up from below, fades in) when pushing this route, and {{code|downExitTransition}} when popping it via {{code|Routes.back}}.  
  
====Trigger====  
  
The route is requested by {{code|component-footer.qml}}. The footer's Quit {{code|LetterboxButton}} checks {{code|isInGame}} at click time:  
  
* If {{code|isInGame}} is {{code|true}}: calls <code><nowiki>footer.routeRequest(Routes.savebeforequit.name)</nowiki></code> — shows this dialog.  
* If {{code|isInGame}} is {{code|false}}: calls <code><nowiki>footer.routeRequest(Routes.quit.name)</nowiki></code> — shows the standard quit dialog instead.  
  
====alert====  
  
A single {{code|Alert}} component (id: {{code|alert}}) with the following properties:  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Property  
! Value  
|-  
| {{code|skrim}}  
| {{code|false}} — no darkening overlay is drawn behind the dialog.  
|-  
| {{code|titleText}}  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.headers.saveBeforeQuit</nowiki></code> (engine key: {{code|#QTUI_Header_SaveBeforeQuit}})  
|-  
| {{code|messageText}}  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.modalMessages.saveBeforeQuit</nowiki></code> (engine key: {{code|#QTUI_Messages_SaveBeforeQuit}})  
|}  
  
=====Buttons=====  
  
{| class="standard-table"  
! Button type  
! {{code|objectName}}  
! Label (L10n key)  
! Action  
|-  
| {{code|CTAButton}}  
| <code><nowiki>ui:btn:action[cancel]</nowiki></code>  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.buttons.cancel</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Buttons_Cancel}})  
| Calls <code><nowiki>saveBeforeQuitView.routeRequest(Routes.back.name)</nowiki></code> — navigates back without quitting or saving.  
|-  
| {{code|CTAButton}}  
| <code><nowiki>ui:btn:action[discard]</nowiki></code>  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.buttons.dontSave</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Buttons_DontSave}})  
| Calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.executeClientCommandUnrestricted("quit")</nowiki></code> — quits immediately without saving.  
|-  
| {{code|DefaultButton}}  
| <code><nowiki>ui:btn:action[apply]</nowiki></code>  
| <code><nowiki>L10n.strings.buttons.save</nowiki></code> ({{code|#QTUI_Buttons_Save}})  
| Calls <code><nowiki>saveBeforeQuitView.saveGameRequest(null)</nowiki></code>, then immediately calls <code><nowiki>BlackMesaEngine.executeClientCommandUnrestricted("quit")</nowiki></code>.  
|}  
  
Note the unusual button type assignments: both '''Cancel''' and '''Don't Save''' use the prominent {{code|CTAButton}} style (orange highlight), while '''Save''' — the safest and most constructive action — uses the less prominent {{code|DefaultButton}}. This is the inverse of the visual hierarchy used elsewhere in the UI, where the primary action is typically the {{code|CTAButton}}.  
  
=====Auto-show=====  
  
{{code|Component.onCompleted}} immediately calls {{code|alert.show()}}, so the dialog appears as soon as the route is loaded with no additional trigger required.  
  
====saveGameRequest Signal Chain====  
  
Passing {{code|null}} to {{code|saveGameRequest}} triggers the following chain:  
  
# {{code|saveBeforeQuitView.saveGameRequest(null)}} fires the signal defined in the {{code|RouteView}} base class ({{code|routeview.qml}}).  
# {{code|router.qml}} has connected this signal to {{code|_saveGameRequestHandler}}, which re-emits {{code|outlet.saveGameRequest(filename)}}.  
# The upstream handler (in {{code|main.qml}}) calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.saveGame(null, callback)}}.  
# {{code|BlackMesaUtils.saveGame}} auto-generates a zero-padded filename in the format <code><nowiki>bms-NNN</nowiki></code> (e.g. {{code|bms-001}}) and issues the engine command <code><nowiki>gameui_hide;save bms-NNN</nowiki></code>.  
# The quit command then executes immediately after, without waiting for the save to complete.  
  
The lack of any callback or deferred execution between the save and quit calls means the game quits synchronously after issuing the save command, relying on the engine to flush the save before exiting.  

===routeview-savegame.qml===
This is the '''Save Game''' page, accessible as the '''campaign.savegame''' route (rank 1.3). Contains three top-level elements: a '''Carousel''', and two '''Alert''' dialogs.
====Top-level Property====
'''selectedModel''' ('''variant''') — stores the carousel item model of the save slot the user last interacted with, so the confirmation dialogs can reference it.
====saveGameCarousel — The Carousel====
Uses '''CarouselLoadGameItem''' as its '''itemComponent'''. The following properties are bound from the model into each card:
{| class="standard-table"
|- style="box-shadow:0 1px #525252; position:sticky; z-index:10; top:-1px"
! Property
! Source
|-
| buttonText
| model.buttonText
|-
| thumbnailUrl
| model.thumbnailUrl
|-
| chapterName
| model.chapterName
|-
| date
| Theme.getDateTimeString(model.date) — locale-formatted
|-
| saveType
| model.saveType
|-
| buttonType
| model.buttonType ("cta" or "warning")
|-
|}
'''CarouselLoadGameItem''' renders each card with a chapter name (white, 26px), a date (white, 16px), and a save type label (ALL CAPS, bold for manual saves, dimmed for autosave). 
=====Interaction Handlers=====
'''onSelected'''/'''onSelectedWithDoubleClick''' — both are identical:
*If '''model.savename''' is non-empty (existing save): store the model in '''selectedModel''' and show '''confirmOverwriteAlert'''.
*If '''model.savename''' is empty (the "New Save" slot): emit {{code|saveGameRequest(null)}} directly, which the router handles by calling {{code|BlackMesaUtils.saveGame(null, callback)}} to auto-generate a file name

'''Secondary button''' (Delete): only visible when {{code|!!model.savename}} (hidden on the '''New Save''' slot). Its label comes from {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString("#GameUI_Delete")}}. Clicking it stores the model and shows '''confirmDeleteAlert'''.

=====levels ListModel=====
Built in '''Component.onCompleted''' from {{code|BlackMesaUtils.getSaveGames()}}:
1. First item always: a "New Save" slot with {{code|savename: ""}}, {{code|thumbnailUrl: "ui/images/newSaveThumbnail.png"}}, {{code|buttonText: createSaveButtonText}}, {{code|buttonType: "cta"}}.
2. '''Remaining items''': all existing saves where '''savename''' does not match '''/^(autosave|quick)\d*$/''' — i.e. only manual saves are shown. Autosaves and quicksaves are excluded. Each gets {{code|buttonType: "warning"}} and {{code|buttonText: overwriteButtonText}}.

====confirmOverwriteAlert====
Shown when the user selects an existing save slot.
*'''Title''': {{code|L10n.strings.headers.overwriteSaveConfirmation}}
*'''Message''': {{code|L10n.strings.modalMessages.overwriteSaveConfirmation}}
*'''WarningButton''' ('''ui:btn:action[apply]'''): emits {{code|saveGameRequest(selectedModel.savename)}} then nulls '''selectedModel'''. The router handles this signal by calling {{code|BlackMesaUtils.saveGame(filename, callback)}}.
*'''DefaultButton''' ('''ui:btn:action[cancel]'''): hides the alert.

====confirmDeleteAlert====
Shown when the user clicks the secondary Delete button on an existing save.
*'''Title''': {{code|L10n.strings.headers.deleteSaveConfirmation}}
*'''Message''': {{code|L10n.strings.modalMessages.deleteSaveConfirmation}}
*'''WarningButton''' ('''ui:btn:action[apply]'''): calls {{code|BlackMesaEngine.removeSaveEntry(selectedModel.savename)}}. If successful, clears the carousel model and attempts to reload it via {{code|saveGameCarousel.levels.Component.completed()}}, then nulls '''selectedModel''' and hides the alert.
*'''DefaultButton''' ('''ui:btn:action[cancel]'''): hides the alert.

====saveGameRequest Signal====
Defined in the '''RouteView''' base class ('''routeview.qml'''). The comment there says: "if savegame is null, a new save should be created."

It is handled by '''router.qml''' (7 matches) which calls {{code|BlackMesaUtils.saveGame()}}.

====Notable Issues====
1. {{code|saveGameCarousel.levels.Component.completed()}} (line 110) is not valid QML. {{code|Component.completed()}} is a signal, not a callable function — can't be invoked directly to re-run '''Component.onCompleted'''. This means after a successful delete, the carousel model is cleared but never repopulated, leaving the user with an empty save list until they navigate away and back.

2. '''onSelected''' and '''onSelectedWithDoubleClick''' are identical — there is no difference in behavior between a single click and a double click on a save slot.

3. Secondary button label uses {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString("#GameUI_Delete")}} directly instead of the '''L10n''' singleton used for all other strings in the file — inconsistent with the rest of the UI.

===routeview-translators.qml===
This is the '''Translators''' credits page, accessible as the '''options.translators''' route (rank 3.7).

====Top-level Properties====
*'''translators_proofreaders'''	- hardcoded JSON array of proofreader objects, fields: '''crowdin_name''', '''discord_name''', '''language''', '''full_name_nick_name'''.
*'''translators_credits''' - hardcoded JSON array of translator objects, fields: '''name''', '''language''', '''translated_words'''.
*'''pageWidth''' - {{code|1600 * Theme.widthScale}}, unused in layout, defined but not referenced.
*'''headerHeight'''	- {{code|64 * Theme.heightScale}}, not referenced in the layout.
*'''headerOffset''' - {{code|128 * Theme.heightScale}}, not referenced in the layout.
*'''footerOffset''' - {{code|72 * Theme.heightScale}}, not referenced in the layout.
*'''cellW''' - {{code|360 * Theme.widthScale}}, minimum card width.
*'''cellH''' - {{code|220 * Theme.heightScale}}, card height.
*'''itemsToShowOnRow''' - {{code|floor(proofreadersContainer.width / cellW)}} — broken: references '''proofreadersContainer''' which does not exist in the file.
*'''activeTab''' - integer, default 0, controls which tab/dataset is shown.

The data is entirely hardcoded in the QML source — no engine API is called to fetch it.

====proofreaderDelegate Component====
A card for each entry in '''translators_proofreaders'''. Each card is an '''Item''' sized to the grid cell, containing a centered inner '''Item''' with 32px padding on all sides.
Inside the inner item:
*A Rectangle background with radius: 4 and color: '#A6000000' (~65% opaque black).
*An Image ('''ui/images/user-avatar.png'''), 48×48px (height-scaled), centered horizontally at the top.
*Four Text elements stacked vertically, all in '''Theme.colors.highlight''' (orange):
**'''_text_label''' (16px, bold): crowdin_name.
**'''_nickname''' (14px): "Full Name: ''<full_name_nick_name>''".
**'''_discord_nickname''' (14px): "Discord: ''<discord_name>''".
**''' _lang''' (14px): "Language: ''<language>''".

====translatorDelegate Component====
A card for each entry in '''translators_credits'''. Structurally identical to '''proofreaderDelegate''' (same background, same avatar image, same styling), but shows different fields:
*'''_text_label''' (16px, bold): name (includes Crowdin username in parentheses).
*'''_language''' (14px): "Language: ''<language>''".
*'''_translated_words''' (14px): "Translated words: ''<translated_words>''".

No '''discord_name''' or '''full_name_nick_name''' fields — this delegate has one fewer text row.

====Main Layout====
A full-size '''Item''' ('''translators_main_container''') containing a '''GridLayout''' with 1 column and 2 rows.

=====Row 1 — Tab Bar=====
{| class="standard-table"
|- style="box-shadow:0 1px #525252; position:sticky; z-index:10; top:-1px"
! Button
! objectName
! selected condition
! Label (localized)
! onClicked
|-
| Proofreaders
| "ui:tab"
| !activeTab (i.e. activeTab === 0)
| #BlackMesaUI_TranslatorsTab_Proofreaders
| activeTab = 0
|-
| Translators
| "ui:tab"
| activeTab (i.e. activeTab === 1)
| #BlackMesaUI_TranslatorsTab_Translators
| activeTab = 1
|-
| skins
| int
| Currently selected skin index from the player configuration.
|-
|}

Both have {{code|type: "sub-nav"}} and {{code|objectName: "ui:tab"}}, so they are picked up by {{code|BlackMesaUtils.traverseUIElements()}} as navigable tabs.

=====Row 2 — GridView (grid_view)=====
The scrollable grid of cards. Key properties:
* '''cellWidth''': {{code|max(cellW, grid_view.width / 5) - 16 * Theme.widthScale}} — tries to fit 5 columns but never goes below cellW (360px scaled); subtracts 16px for the scrollbar.
* '''cellHeight''': {{code|cellH}} (220px scaled).
* '''clip: true''', {{code|keyNavigationEnabled: true}}.
* '''model'''}: '''translators_proofreaders''' when {{code|activeTab === 0}}, else '''translators_credits'''.
* '''delegate''': '''proofreaderDelegate''' when {{code|activeTab === 0}}, else '''translatorDelegate'''.
* '''ScrollBar.vertical''': '''VerticalScrollBar''' (always visible).
* '''Component.onCompleted''': calls {{code|grid_view.forceActiveFocus()}} so keyboard navigation works immediately on page load.

====Keyboard scrolling quirk====
'''Keys.onUpPressed''' and '''Keys.onDownPressed''' call {{code|scrollBar.decrease()}}/{{code|scrollBar.increase()}} directly on the scrollbar, rather than letting '''keyNavigationEnabled''' move between grid cells. This means Up/Down scroll the list rather than moving the selection row by row.

====Notable Issues====
1. '''itemsToShowOnRow''' references '''proofreadersContainer.width''', which does not exist anywhere in the file. This property will always be NaN/undefined at runtime, though it is never used in the layout either.

2. '''pageWidth''', '''headerHeight''', '''headerOffset''', '''footerOffset''' are all defined but never referenced in the layout — dead properties.

3. Tab labels use {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString()}} directly instead of the '''L10n''' singleton used everywhere else in the UI.

4. All card text uses '''Theme.colors.highlight''' (orange) for every field — including secondary info like Discord name and word count — rather than using '''Theme.colors.text''' or '''Theme.colors.dimmedText''' for secondary fields as other components do.

===routeview-unavailable.qml===
Unused. It is a '''RouteView''' containing a single '''Image''' element. The image is:
*Sourced from '''ui/images/unavailable.png''' via the engine's {{code|image://game/}} provider.
*Sized at a nominal 609×108 pixels, scaled by '''Theme.widthScale'''. {{note|Both dimensions use '''widthScale''', not '''heightScale''' — so it scales uniformly with screen width.}}
*Centered in the parent via {{code|anchors.centerIn: parent}}.

It has no interactive elements, no text, no buttons, and no logic — it is purely a static "this feature is unavailable" splash image.

===routeview-video.qml===
This is the Video Options settings page. It is a '''RouteView''' (id: '''videoOptionsView''') containing a single '''OptionsList''', a footer description panel, a warning header, and a confirmation alert.

====Top-level Properties====
*'''blockingRouteName''' - stores the destination route when navigation is intercepted due to unsaved changes.
*'''costFontSize''' - scaled font size ({{code|16 * Theme.widthScale}}) for the footer description panel.
*'''headerWarningVisibility''' - boolean that shows/hides the warning icon in the top-right corner.
*'''advancedQualityKeys''' - array of 17 setting keys that are controlled by the overall quality preset.

====Navigation Guard====
{{code|onRouteWillChange(newRouteName)}} intercepts any attempt to leave the page. If '''videoOptions.hasPendingChanges''' is true, it stores the destination in '''blockingRouteName''', shows '''confirmAlert''', and returns true to block the navigation.

====OptionsList — videoOptions====
The main content area. It uses {{code|batchUpdates: true}} and shows 8 visible rows at a time.

=====Quality Preset System=====
{{code|Component.onCompleted}} defines a '''qualitySettings''' object with 5 presets (keys "0"–"4"): '''Potato''', '''Low''', '''Medium''', '''High''', '''Ultra'''. Each preset is a map of all 17 '''advancedQualityKeys''' to their target values.

'''onSettingsReset'''' reads [[mat_easymenu_quality_level]] and calls {{code|setAdvancedSettingsState()}} to restore the preset.

'''onRequestSettingsChange''' handles two cases, after every change it calls {{code|updateHeaderWarningVisibility()}}:
*If the changed key is '''video_quality''', it applies the matching preset via {{code|setAdvancedSettingsState()}}.
*If the changed key is one of the 17 advanced keys, it sets '''video_quality''' to "5" ('''Custom''').

{{code|setAdvancedSettingsState(settings)}} iterates '''advancedQualityKeys''' and calls {{code|setValue()}} for each key, but only if the current quality is not already '''Custom''' ("5").

{{code|updateHeaderWarningVisibility()}} scans all select-type model items and counts how many currently-selected options have warning: 1. Sets '''headerWarningVisibility''' accordingly.

====groups — Grouped Apply Callbacks====
A single function {{code|displayMode()}} handles both '''video_resolution''' and '''video_displayMode''' together. It parses the resolution string, immediately updates '''appRoot.width'''/'''height''' and '''Theme.widthScale'''/'''heightScale''' so the UI rescales at once, sets {{code|reloadPageAfterApplyChanges [[=]] true}}, then issues [[mat_setvideomode]] with the correct windowed/borderless flags.

====setComplex — Multi-CVar Setters====
Settings that map to more than one engine CVar have a named function here instead of a simple cvar property:
*'''video_filtering''' - [[mat_trilinear]], [[mat_forceaniso]].
*'''video_waterDetail''' - [[r_waterforceexpensive]], [[r_waterforcereflectentities]].
*'''video_dynamicShadowQuality''' - [[nr_shadow_active]], [[nr_shadow_quality]], [[nr_shadow_max_passes_per_frame]].
*'''video_dynamicShadowMemory''' - [[nr_shadow_res]].
*'''video_dynamicShadowSoftness''' - [[nr_shadow_filter_quality]].
*'''video_dynamicLightsQuality''' - [[nr_lights_quality]], [[r_4way_minimal_mode]], [[r_4way_use_fast_normals]] (with override support).
*'''video_godRays''' - [[np_gr_quality]], [[np_gr_quality_sample_count]], [[np_gr_quality_pass_count]].
*'''video_csmQuality''' - [[cl_csm_enabled]], [[cl_csm_qualitymode]].
*'''video_csmMemory''' - [[cl_csm_memoryusage]].
*'''video_unlockHighendUnsupported''' - [[mat_unlock_gfx_highend_unsupported]]; also sets {{code|reloadPageAfterApplyChanges [[=]] true}}.

Several setters enforce caps when [[mat_unlock_gfx_highend_unsupported]] is false (e.g. dynamic shadow quality capped at 4, CSM quality capped at 3, dynamic lights capped at 4).

====getComplex — Multi-CVar Getters====
Mirror functions that read the same console variables and reconstruct the UI value. They also silently clamp out-of-range values back to valid ones (the comment calls this "terrible and hacky" but intentional — it self-heals console-edited values).

{{note|'''video_resolution''' reads from '''Theme.rootWidth''' and '''Theme.rootHeight''' rather than from an engine console variable.}}
{{bug|hidetested=1|{{code|getComplex.video_godRays()}} has an unreachable return "0" at line 591 after the else branch already returns.}}

====ListModel — The 25 Settings Items====
Built dynamically in '''Component.onCompleted'''. Some of the components has {{code|warning: 1}}, contributes to '''headerWarningVisibility'''.
{{note|A '''Potato''' quality tier and '''video_unlockPotato''' checkbox are fully implemented in the code but entirely commented out. '''unlockedPotato''' is hardcoded to true in all getters/setters, meaning '''Potato'''-tier options are silently available in the advanced settings dropdowns but the preset and unlock checkbox are disabled.}}

====Footer Description Panel — footerGraphicDescription====
An Item anchored to the bottom-left of the page (leaving 600px on the right for the options list scrollbar area). It contains 7 text elements:
*'''footerGraphicDescriptionText_desc''' — the setting's '''description_main''' string (wraps to a calculated width).
*'''footerGraphicDescriptionText_cost_CPU'''/'''GPU'''/'''RAM''' — labels "CPU:", "GPU:", "RAM:" (white, only shown if the setting has a cost).
*'''footerGraphicDescriptionText_cost_CPU'''/'''GPU'''/'''RAM_value''' — the cost values, colored dynamically.

{{code|onSelectionChanged(modelSelected)}} populates all 7 texts and applies a color map to the value texts:
*Major - #FF4D2D (red).
*Moderate - #FFD53E (yellow).
*Minor - #55E835 (green).
*None - white.
*(empty) - #000000 (black/hidden).

====Warning Header — warningHeaderSign/warningHeaderText====
A warning icon ('''ui/images/warning.png''') and text label anchored to the top-right of the page. Both are {{code|visible: headerWarningVisibility}}. They appear whenever any currently-selected option has {{code|warning: 1}} (i.e. Water High, CSM Insane, Dynamic Lights Ultra/Insane).

====Confirmation Alert — confirmAlert====
An Alert dialog shown when the user tries to navigate away with unsaved changes. It has three buttons:
*Apply ('''CTAButton''') — calls {{code|videoOptions.flushPendingChanges()}} then navigates to '''blockingRouteName'''.
*Discard ('''WarningButton''') — navigates to blockingRouteName without saving.
*Cancel ('''DefaultButton''') — clears blockingRouteName and hides the alert.

===sound.qml===
Registered as the Sound singleton in qmldir. It is a pragma Singleton QtObject that wraps '''BlackMesaEngine''''s audio API into a clean, named interface for the rest of the UI.

====Properties====
*'''ambientId''' — a writable int that stores the handle returned by {{code|BlackMesaEngine.playAmbientSound}}, used to track and stop the currently playing ambient/music track.
*'''music''' — a readonly array of the two music track paths pulled from '''Theme.sounds.music1''' and '''Theme.sounds.music2'''.

====Functions====
*{{code|startMusic()}} - picks one of the two music tracks at random and calls {{code|startAmbient()}} with it.
*{{code|stopMusic()}} - delegates to {{code|stopAmbient()}}.
*{{code|playEffect(filestr)}} - plays a one-shot UI sound effect via '''BlackMesaEngine.playSoundEffect'''.
*{{code|isPlayingAmbient()}} - returns true if ambientId is non-null/non-zero.
*{{code|startAmbient(filestr)}} - stops any current ambient, reads [[snd_musicvolume]] from the console, then calls '''BlackMesaEngine.playAmbientSound''' and stores the returned ID in '''ambientId'''.
*{{code|stopAmbient()}} - if '''ambientId''' is set and the sound is still playing, stops it via '''BlackMesaEngine.stopSoundById''' and nulls '''ambientId'''.

====Bug====
There is a typo on line 41: '''ambiendId''' (extra d) instead of ambientId. This means {{code|stopAmbient()}} will throw a ReferenceError at runtime when it tries to stop a playing sound, so ambient tracks can never actually be stopped once started. sound.qml:39-44

====Usage across the codebase====
Sound is used in two distinct patterns.


Music lifecycle — startMusic/stopMusic are called from:
*'''main.qml''' (5 matches) — likely on '''gameui_activated'''/'''gameui_hidden''' signals.
*'''routeview-index.qml''' (6 matches) — starts/stops music when the main menu index page is entered/exited.
*'''component-footer.qml''' (4 matches) — manages music during the loading screen.


UI sound effects — '''playEffect''' is called from virtually every interactive component, passing '''Theme.sounds.buttonFocused''' on hover and '''Theme.sounds.buttonClicked''' on press:
*All button types: '''button-default.qml''', '''button-letterbox.qml''', '''button-triangle.qml''', '''button-carouselnav.qml'''.
*All options list inputs: '''component-optionslist-input-slider.qml''', '''component-optionslist-input-select.qml''', '''component-optionslist-input-map.qml''', '''component-optionslist-input-checkbox.qml''', '''component-optionslist-input-bindingcell.qml'''.
*List rows and carousel items: '''component-optionslist-row.qml''', '''component-carousel-item.qml'''.
*Route views: '''routeview-bindings.qml''', '''routeview-join.qml'''.

===theme.qml===
Registered as the '''Theme''' singleton in qmldir and serves as the global design system for the entire UI — fonts, colors, opacities, sounds, and locale utilities all live here.

====Layout====
It is a pragma Singleton QtObject with multiple top-level members.

=====Scaling properties=====
'''rootWidth''', '''rootHeight''', '''widthScale''', and '''heightScale''' are writable properties (no readonly). They are set externally, in '''main.qml''', the primary source, using '''Binding''' elements for the scale properties and '''onWidthChanged'''/'''onHeightChanged''' handlers for the raw dimensions, and '''routeview-video.qml''' — directly reassigns all four when the user applies a resolution change from the video settings page, so the UI rescales immediately without waiting for a window resize event. Also used throughout the UI to scale elements to the current resolution. '''Triangle''' for example uses '''Theme.heightScale''' directly.

=====dictionary — locale map=====
A nested QtObject mapping '''Steam''' language names (e.g. "english", "schinese", "koreana") to BCP 47 locale codes (e.g. "en_US", "zh_CN", "ko_KR"). Used by '''getDateTimeString'''.

=====getDateTimeString(date) — locale-aware date formatter=====
Reads [[cl_language]] from the engine console, looks it up in dictionary, then formats the date using Qt's '''toLocaleString''' with a localized format string fetched from {{code|BlackMesaEngine.getLocalizedString}}.

=====colors — color palette=====
All named colors used across the UI:
*highlight	- #e47125 (orange).
*text/buttonText/headerText - white.
*dimmedText - #888888 (gray).
*subNavBackground - #111111 (black).
*skrim - #2d2d2d (dark gray).
*modalBackground - #222222 (darker gray).
*warning - #c72424 (red).
*navigationHighlight - #e88849 (lighter orange), for controller-focused active buttons.

=====opacity — named opacity levels=====
Seven named opacity values for different UI layers: subNav (0.5), mainNav (0.75), modal (0.95), modalHeader (0.85), modalSkrim (0.9), pageSkrimLight (0.75), pageSkrimDark (0.85).

=====sounds — UI sound effect paths=====
Four named sound paths: '''buttonFocused''', '''buttonClicked''', and two music tracks ('''music1''', '''music2'''). The music paths are prefixed with #.

=====fonts — font family strings=====
Loads six font files via '''FontLoader''' from the VFS ({{code|vfs:///ui/fonts/}}): Fira Mono (dev console), and NotoSans in Light/Bold/Regular plus their CJK variants. The public '''fonts.light''', '''fonts.bold''', and '''fonts.regular''' properties are comma-joined font family strings that list the CJK font first so it takes priority for CJK characters.

=====devConsole — dev console sub-theme=====
A nested color palette specifically for the developer console: background (#d0202020), message (lightgray), inputText (#ffd0d0d0). The '''fonts.devConsole''' property (Fira Mono) pairs with this.

===triangle.qml===
This a small, reusable primitive that renders a triangle shape using a rotated, clipped Rectangle. It is registered in qmldir as the Triangle component (not a singleton).

====How it works====
The component is an '''Item''' with {{code|clip: true}}. Inside it sits a '''Rectangle''' that is rotated 45° and scaled up by 1.4 * '''Theme.heightScale''', so only one corner of the rotated square peeks out through the clipping boundary — producing a triangle.

The position of that corner is controlled by the corner property (an integer, default 0). The x and y of the inner rectangle are calculated using modular arithmetic on corner to snap it to one of the four corners of the parent '''Item''' ('''triangle.qml''' around lines 14-15):

<source lang=qml>
x = component.width  * ((corner+1) % 4 < 2 ? 0 : 1) - width/2  
y = component.height * ( corner    % 4 < 2 ? 0 : 1) - height/2  
</source>

The color property is aliased directly to the inner '''rect.color''', so consumers can set it like any normal color property.

====Usage====
Triangle is used by '''button-triangle.qml''' ('''TriangleButton'''), which sets rotation on the '''Triangle''' instance to point it left or right, and animates its color between '''blurredColor''' and '''focusedColor''' on hover. 

===utils.qml===
Registered as the '''BlackMesaUtils''' singleton in qmldir and serves as the central utility and UI navigation hub for the entire menu system. Stores some utils and helpers.

====Structure Overview====
It is a pragma '''Singleton''' QtObject with two main responsibilities, save/load game helpers and controller/keyboard UI navigation.

====General Utilities====
*'''discordLink''' — a hardcoded string "https://discord.gg/crowbarcollective", used as invite link to [[Crowbar Collective]]'s server in '''Discord'''.
*{{code|setTimeout(cb, delay)}} — a polyfill for JavaScript's setTimeout, since Qt has no native equivalent. Uses a shared '''globalTimer''' and properly disconnects signals after firing.

====Save/Load Game Functions====
*{{code|getSaveGames()}} — calls {{code|BlackMesaEngine.listSaveEntries()}}, sorts by date descending, and maps each entry into a normalized object with '''savename''', '''chapter''', '''chapterName''', '''type''' ("manual", "autosave", or "quick"), '''dateCreated''', and '''thumbnail'''.
*{{code|saveGame(filename, callback)}} — auto-generates a zero-padded file name like ''bms-001'' if none is provided, then issues a {{code|gameui_hide;save}} engine command and calls back.
*{{code|loadGame(filename)}}/{{code|loadMap(filename)}} — issue {{code|disconnect;wait;wait;sv_cheats 0; maxplayers 1; load/map}} engine commands.

====UI Navigation System====
This is the bulk of the file. It implements a regex-based UI traversal and keyboard/controller navigation system.

=====State Properties=====
Tracks the current/previous focused element, a flat tabs array, indices for main/sub/sub-nav tab levels, '''actionButtons''' ('''APPLY''', '''CANCEL''', '''DISCARD''', '''QUIT''', '''BACK'''), and '''optionItems''' (list rows and carousel items).

=====Key Functions=====
*{{code|clearUIElementsData()}} - resets all navigation state before a screen transition.
*{{code|traverseUIElements(root)}} - recursively walks the QML tree, classifying elements by objectName regex into tabs, buttons, option items, or carousel items.
*{{code|afterTraverseCallback()}} - post-traversal: sets sub-tab start indices and focuses the first option item.
*{{code|processOptionSelection(sign)}}	- moves up/down through optionItems, skipping disabled ones.
*{{code|processCarouselItemSelection(sign)}} - moves left/right through carousel items, triggering pagination arrows as needed.
*{{code|processTabsNavigation(sign)}} - handles left/right navigation across 3 levels of tabs (main -> sub -> sub-nav).
*{{code|navigationUIHandle(event)}} - entry point called from {{code|Keys.onPressed}} in '''main.qml'''; dispatches all key events ('''Enter''', '''Backspace''', '''A'''/'''D''', arrows) to the above functions.
*{{code|updateCurrentUISelection()}} - applies "focused"/"blurred" states to elements and scrolls lists to keep the selection visible.

=====Navigation Priority (Backspace/Back button)=====
When backspace is pressed, it checks in order: in-game -> '''DISCARD''' -> '''CANCEL''' -> '''BACK''', giving a clear priority chain for controller "B button" behavior.
